📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期人乳头瘤病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 知识总分平均分为 25.07±5.92 分(满分为 33 分)。教育程度与知识水平显着相关(P=0.01)。 [10.24200/jogcr.11.8.683]。
  • 参与者对活性 监测 表达了积极的看法,认为它是一种不会增加长期癌症风险的安全选择。他们强调了在制定 CIN2 管理建议时多层次因素的重要性,包括诊断、患者、临床医生和政策层面的考虑。 [10.1111/ajo.70165]。
  • 影像学检查显示广泛的颈部淋巴结肿大,鼻咽几乎完全阻塞,舌扁桃体明显受压;组织病理学证实 HPV-16 阳性 OPSCC 伴有大淋巴结病变和神经系统受累。 [10.51894/001c.166043]。
  • 在八种常见的 hrHPV 类型(HPV16、18、31、33、35、45、52 和 58)中,HPV PhAM-Seq 与使用简并 L1 靶向引物的 qPCR 的总体一致性超过 80%,其中对 HPV16、31、33 和 58 的灵敏度最高。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The mean total knowledge score was 25.07±5.92 out of a possible 33 points. Educational attainment was significantly associated with knowledge levels (P=0.01). [10.24200/jogcr.11.8.683].
  • The tumor had very low tumor mutational burden (TMB, 1.21/Mb), microsatellite stability, no homologous recombination deficiency, and minimal PD‐L1 (~1%), consistent with a poorly immunogenic tumor microenvironment.
  • Participants expressed positive views about active surveillance, considering it a safe option that does not increase long-term cancer risk. [10.1111/ajo.70165].
  • Imaging revealed extensive cervical lymphadenopathy with near-complete nasopharyngeal obstruction and marked lingual tonsillar compression; histopathology confirmed HPV-16–positive OPSCC with bulky nodal disease and neurologic involvement.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期人乳头瘤病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 两项研究调查了女性对 HPV 自我采样检测的反应。两项研究都发现,相比临床医生的检测,女性更喜欢自行收集。新加坡的研究发表在《家庭医学年鉴》上,美国的研究发表在《JAMA Network Open》上。
  • 德里高等法院拒绝临时中止印度的 HPV 疫苗接种 活动,允许该活动继续进行,同时有关知情同意、安全证据和监测的问题仍在审查中。 [news-13c1364a1115a149]。
  • 新加坡的一项实用随机对照试验测试了提供自行收集的 HPV 样本以及临床医生收集的样本是否会提高 宫颈癌 筛查参与度和 HPV 检出率。干预组首先接受临床医生采样,如果拒绝则自行采样,而常规护理组仅接受临床医生采样。
  • Seegene 宣布推出一种基于综合征 PCR 的生殖道感染 (RTI) 综合检测方法,可同时检测 STI 和 HPV。在此之前,全球百万临床研究于 2026 年 7 月 8 日启动。该公告基于对 60,000 项检测结果的分析,并作为支持 PCR 检测全球新标准的科学证据。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Human papillomaviruses news developments.
  • Two studies examined women's response to HPV self-sampling testing. Both studies found that when offered, women prefer self-collection over clinician testing.
  • The Delhi High Court declined to grant an interim stay on India’s HPV vaccination campaign, allowing it to continue while questions about informed consent, safety evidence, and monitoring remain under examination.
  • A pragmatic randomized controlled trial in Singapore tested whether offering self-collected HPV samples alongside clinician-collected specimens would improve cervical cancer screening participation and HPV detection rates.
  • Seegene announced a comprehensive syndromic PCR-based testing approach for reproductive tract infections (RTIs), detecting both STIs and HPV simultaneously.

Journal: The journal of obstetrics and gynaecology research  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-01  |  DOI: 10.1111/jog.70426  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 52, 8, e70426

尽管派姆单抗联合化疗,HPV18 阳性宫颈大细胞神经内分泌癌仍快速进展:具有免疫基因组见解的 病例报告。

HPV18-Positive Cervical Large Cell Neuroendocrine Carcinoma With Rapid Progression Despite Pembrolizumab Combined With Chemotherapy: A Case Report With Immunogenomic Insights.
作者： Jumpei Ogura, Shimpei Sato, Yuichiro Koshida, Maya Kasuga, Takuya Yokoe, Naokazu Kanamoto, 摩耶 春日
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HPV18阳性宫颈大细胞神经内分泌癌(LCNEC)极为罕见,该研究旨在报告一例尽管派姆单抗联合化疗但进展迅速的病例。
设计与对象
该研究报告了一名 40 岁女性的病例,她患有 Figo 2018 IVB 期 LCNEC,尽管采用了多种 治疗 方案,但病情进展迅速。
核心方法
活检显示嗜铬粒蛋白 A 和突触素阳性、弥漫性 p16 和 Ki-67%~80%,综合分析显示肿瘤突变负荷、微卫星稳定性、同源重组缺陷和 PD-L1 表达。
主要结果
The tumor had very low tumor mutational burden (TMB, 1.21/Mb), microsatellite stability, no homologous recombination deficiency, and minimal PD‐L1 (~1%), consistent with a poorly immunogenic tumor microenvironment.
解释与创新
作者解释说,免疫基因组图谱可能解释了免疫检查点抑制在该组织学中的有限作用,该病例为临床医生提供了实用指导。
科研与公卫意义
该病例表明,仅 HPV 或 p16 阳性并不能预测晚期宫颈 LCNEC 中基于派姆单抗的治疗的临床获益,并支持在 治疗 选择之前进行仔细的生物学评估。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于单个 病例报告,限制了普遍性,但详细的免疫基因组分析为 治疗 耐药性提供了强有力的机制见解。

HPV18-Positive Cervical Large Cell Neuroendocrine Carcinoma With Rapid Progression Despite Pembrolizumab Combined With Chemotherapy: A Case Report With Immunogenomic Insights.

Authors: Jumpei Ogura, Shimpei Sato, Yuichiro Koshida, Maya Kasuga, Takuya Yokoe, Naokazu Kanamoto, 摩耶 春日
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT HPV18‐positive cervical large cell neuroendocrine carcinoma (LCNEC) is exceptionally rare. We report a 40‐years‐old woman with FIGO 2018 stage IVB LCNEC who progressed rapidly despite paclitaxel–carboplatin–bevacizumab plus pembrolizumab, followed by concurrent chemoradiotherapy and later cytotoxic regimens. Biopsy showed chromogranin A and synaptophysin positivity, diffuse p16, and Ki‐67%~80%. Comprehensive profiling revealed very low tumor mutational burden (TMB, 1.21/Mb), microsatellite stability, no homologous recombination deficiency, and minimal PD‐L1 (~1%), consistent with a poorly immunogenic tumor microenvironment (TME). This profile may explain the limited effect of immune checkpoint inhibition in this histology. The case suggests that HPV or p16 positivity alone did not predict clinical benefit from pembrolizumab‐based therapy in advanced cervical LCNEC, and supports careful biological assessment before treatment selection. These observations provide practical guidance for clinicians and support evaluation of alternative or investigational strategies for this aggressive tumor.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
HPV18‐positive cervical large cell neuroendocrine carcinoma (LCNEC) is exceptionally rare, and the study aimed to report a case with rapid progression despite pembrolizumab combined with chemotherapy.
Design and population
The study reported a case of a 40‐years‐old woman with FIGO 2018 stage IVB LCNEC who progressed rapidly despite multiple treatment regimens.
Core methods
Biopsy showed chromogranin A and synaptophysin positivity, diffuse p16, and Ki‐67%~80%, and comprehensive profiling revealed tumor mutational burden, microsatellite stability, homologous recombination deficiency, and PD‐L1 expression.
Main results
The tumor had very low tumor mutational burden (TMB, 1.21/Mb), microsatellite stability, no homologous recombination deficiency, and minimal PD‐L1 (~1%), consistent with a poorly immunogenic tumor microenvironment.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the immunogenomic profile may explain the limited effect of immune checkpoint inhibition in this histology, and this case provides practical guidance for clinicians.
Scientific and public-health significance
The case suggests that HPV or p16 positivity alone did not predict clinical benefit from pembrolizumab‐based therapy in advanced cervical LCNEC, and supports careful biological assessment before treatment selection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on a single case report, limiting generalizability, but the detailed immunogenomic profiling provides strong mechanistic insights into treatment resistance.

探索人乳头瘤病毒 16型感染且宫颈上皮内瘤变≤1级患者PVX2免疫治疗后病毒清除的免疫相关性。

Exploration of immune correlates of viral clearance after PVX2 immunotherapy in patients with human papillomavirus type 16 infection and cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade ≤1.
作者： Vikrant Palande, Tianbei Zhang, Kellie N. Smith, Kimberly Levinson, Leslie Cope, Hua-Ling Tsai, Yung-Nien Chang, T.-C. Wu, Mark H. Einstein, Richard B. S. Roden
摘要中文翻译摘要 在 PVX2 免疫治疗治疗的 CIN ≤ 1 例患者中寻找与 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 (HPV16) 清除率相关的 免疫应答。PVX2 包含肌肉注射 疫苗接种,其中含有 HPV16 E7 靶向 pNGVL4a-Sig/E7(detox)/HSP70 DNA 疫苗(3 mg,第 0 周和第 4 周)和 HPV16 L2E7E6 融合蛋白(TA-CIN;100 μg 第 8 周)。对 12 名 HPV16+ CIN ≤1 的女性施用 PVX2 引发了抗体和 CD8+ T 细胞反应,包括 TCR 多样性的变化、克隆扩张和公共克隆型的出现。第 6 个月时,5/11 名可评估参与者中未检测到 HPV16。疫苗接种 后 E6、E7 和 L2 特异性抗体滴度较高,但与清除率无关。TCR 克隆多样性/扩增和公共克隆型均与 HPV16 清除无关。虽然扩展/新克隆型的分数与 HPV16 清除无关,但在那些清除 HPV16 的人中,疫苗 抗原特异性读数在 疫苗接种 后的比例明显较高。高基线血清 MCP-1 与 HPV16 持续存在相关,而在清除 HPV16 的患者中,疫苗接种 后 MIP-1β 呈较高趋势。重要性 HPV16 是超过一半的 宫颈癌 的必要原因,在其他肛门生殖器和口咽癌中的比例甚至更高。考虑到当前 治疗 的疾病负担和局限性,需要针对 HPV16 感染和相关癌前病变的 治疗。持续感染 HPV 的女性由于频繁进行阴道镜检查、不便、主动 监测 带来的医疗费用以及将病毒传播给其他性伴侣的可能性而承受着焦虑和压力。由于大多数患者可以自发清除感染,因此免疫治疗可以促进消除疾病可能进展的持续性感染。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定 HPV16 感染和宫颈上皮内瘤变 ≤1 级患者在 PVX2 免疫治疗后病毒清除的免疫相关性,考虑到当前选择的局限性和持续感染引起的焦虑,解决对更好的 治疗 的需求。
设计与对象
一项研究对 12 名 HPV16 阳性 CIN ≤1 的女性进行了研究,她们接受了 PVX2 免疫疗法,其中包括肌肉注射 疫苗接种、HPV16 E7 靶向 DNA 疫苗 和 HPV16 L2E7E6 融合蛋白。
核心方法
该研究评估了 免疫应答,包括抗体滴度、CD8+ T 细胞反应、TCR 多样性、克隆扩增和公共克隆型,以及 疫苗接种 前后的血清细胞因子水平。
主要结果
第 6 个月时,5/11 的可评估参与者中未检测到 HPV16;疫苗接种 后 E6、E7 和 L2 特异性抗体滴度较高,但与清除率无关,并且 TCR 克隆多样性/扩展和公共克隆型均与清除率无关。
解释与创新
作者解释说,虽然引发了 免疫应答,但在 疫苗接种 后只有较高比例的 疫苗 抗原特异性读数与清除相关,这表明潜在的新型保护相关性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能通过强调 疫苗 抗原特异性 T 细胞反应的重要性,为持续性 HPV16 感染的免疫疗法的开发提供信息,从而有可能减少侵入性手术的需要和 传播 风险。
局限与证据强度
尽管该研究提供了直接的 免疫应答 测量和病毒清除数据,但由于样本量较小 (n=12) 和有限的随访,证据强度中等。

Exploration of immune correlates of viral clearance after PVX2 immunotherapy in patients with human papillomavirus type 16 infection and cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade ≤1.

Authors: Vikrant Palande, Tianbei Zhang, Kellie N. Smith, Kimberly Levinson, Leslie Cope, Hua-Ling Tsai, Yung-Nien Chang, T.-C. Wu, Mark H. Einstein, Richard B. S. Roden
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Immune responses correlated with clearance of human papillomavirus type 16 (HPV16) were sought in PVX2 immunotherapy-treated CIN ≤1 patients. PVX2 comprises intramuscular vaccinations with HPV16 E7-targeted pNGVL4a-Sig/E7(detox)/HSP70 DNA vaccine (3 mg, weeks 0 and 4) and HPV16 L2E7E6 fusion protein (TA-CIN; 100 μg week 8). Administering PVX2 to 12 women with HPV16+ CIN ≤1 elicited both antibody and CD8+ T-cell responses, including changes in TCR diversity, clonal expansion, and emergence of public clonotypes. HPV16 was not detected in 5/11 evaluable participants at month 6. Post-vaccination E6, E7, and L2-specific antibody titers were higher, but none correlated with clearance. Neither TCR clonal diversity/expansion nor public clonotypes were associated with HPV16 clearance. While the fractions of expanded/new clonotypes were not associated with HPV16 clearance, a higher proportion of vaccine antigen-specific reads was evident post-vaccination in those clearing HPV16. High baseline serum MCP-1 associated with HPV16 persistence, and MIP-1β trended higher post-vaccination in those clearing HPV16. IMPORTANCE HPV16 is a necessary cause of over half of all cervical cancers, and in even higher fractions of other anogenital and oropharyngeal cancers. Treatments for HPV16 infection and associated pre-cancerous lesions are needed given the disease burden and limitations of current treatments. Women with persistent HPV infections endure anxiety and stress due to frequent colposcopies, inconvenience, healthcare costs from active surveillance, and potential to transmit the virus to other sexual partners. Since a majority of patients can spontaneously clear their infections, administration of an immunotherapy may promote the elimination of otherwise persistent infections where disease can progress.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify immune correlates of viral clearance after PVX2 immunotherapy in patients with HPV16 infection and cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade ≤1, addressing the need for better treatments given the limitations of current options and the anxiety caused by persistent infections.
Design and population
A study was conducted in 12 women with HPV16-positive CIN ≤1, who received PVX2 immunotherapy comprising intramuscular vaccinations with HPV16 E7-targeted DNA vaccine and HPV16 L2E7E6 fusion protein.
Core methods
The study evaluated immune responses including antibody titers, CD8+ T-cell responses, TCR diversity, clonal expansion, and public clonotypes, as well as serum cytokine levels before and after vaccination.
Main results
HPV16 was not detected in 5/11 evaluable participants at month 6; post-vaccination E6, E7, and L2-specific antibody titers were higher but did not correlate with clearance, and neither TCR clonal diversity/expansion nor public clonotypes were associated with clearance.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that while immune responses were elicited, only a higher proportion of vaccine antigen-specific reads post-vaccination was associated with clearance, suggesting a potential novel correlate of protection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of immunotherapies for persistent HPV16 infections by highlighting the importance of vaccine antigen-specific T-cell responses, potentially reducing the need for invasive procedures and transmission risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size (n=12) and limited follow-up, though the study provides direct immune response measurements and viral clearance data.

爱沙尼亚与 人乳头瘤病毒 相关的癌症负担的变化:一项基于人群的登记研究。

Changing burden of cancers related to human papillomavirus in Estonia: a population-based registry study.
作者： Sandra Kase, Mari-Liis Zimmermann, Margit Mägi, Aleksei Baburin, Keiu Paapsi, Reeli Hallik, Piret Veerus, Jana Jaal, Kaire Innos, Baburin Ab
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 会导致多种癌症,了解 HPV 相关的癌症负担可能有助于实施有效的癌症预防策略。
设计与对象
爱沙尼亚癌症登记处提供了 1995 年至 2022 年爱沙尼亚诊断出的 8 个 HPV 相关癌症部位的所有病例数据。
核心方法
使用连接点回归分析年龄标准化的 发病率 趋势,估计年度百分比变化 (APC),并使用国际得出的特定部位归因分数来估计 HPV 归因病例数,因为肿瘤 HPV 状态不可用。
主要结果
In all, 11,266 cases of HPV-related cancer sites were diagnosed, of which 6,263 were estimated to be attributable to HPV; over 40% occurred before age 55. In women, estimated average annual number of HPV-attributable cases nearly quadrupled for oropharyngeal cancer and tripled for anal cancer. A significant increase in oropharyngeal and anal cancer incidence was observed among women (APC 10.0 and 3.8, respectively), while cervical cancer incidence declined after 2012 (APC -5.6). Survival improved for oropharyngeal cancer in men (from 13 to 44%) and vaginal cancer in women (from 45 to 73%).
解释与创新
爱沙尼亚的 HPV 相关癌症模式正在从宫颈癌转变为非 发病率,口咽癌和肛门癌 发病率 的增加突出表明,除了宫颈筛查之外,还需要采取预防策略。
科研与公卫意义
加强 HPV 疫苗接种 的吸收和维持有组织的宫颈筛查对于减少未来的癌症负担至关重要,因为研究结果强调了 HPV 相关癌症负担的转移。
局限与证据强度
由于无法获得肿瘤 HPV 状态,该研究依赖于国际上得出的特定位点归因分数,这可能会给归因估计带来不确定性。考虑到基于人群的登记设计,但直接 HPV 检测的局限性,证据强度中等。

Changing burden of cancers related to human papillomavirus in Estonia: a population-based registry study.

Authors: Sandra Kase, Mari-Liis Zimmermann, Margit Mägi, Aleksei Baburin, Keiu Paapsi, Reeli Hallik, Piret Veerus, Jana Jaal, Kaire Innos, Baburin Ab
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND PURPOSE: Human papillomavirus (HPV) causes multiple cancers. Understanding HPV-related cancer burden may help implement effective strategies for cancer prevention. The aim was to examine incidence and survival trends of eight HPV-related cancer sites in Estonia and estimate the number of cases attributable to HPV. Patient/material and methods: The Estonian Cancer Registry provided data on all cases of eight HPV-related cancer sites diagnosed in Estonia during 1995-2022. Age-standardized incidence trends were analyzed using joinpoint regression, estimating annual percentage change (APC). The number of HPV-attributable cases was estimated using internationally derived site-specific attributable fractions, as tumor HPV status was not available. Five-year relative survival ratios were calculated from national life tables. RESULTS: In all, 11,266 cases of HPV-related cancer sites were diagnosed, of which 6,263 were estimated to be attributable to HPV; over 40% occurred before age 55. In women, estimated average annual number of HPV-attributable cases nearly quadrupled for oropharyngeal cancer (OPC) and tripled for anal cancer. A significant increase in OPC and anal cancer incidence was observed among women (APC 10.0 and 3.8, respectively). Cervical cancer incidence declined after 2012 (APC -5.6). Survival improved for OPC in men (from 13 to 44%) and vaginal cancer in women (from 45 to 73%). INTERPRETATION: HPV-related cancer patterns in Estonia are shifting from cervical to non-cervical cancers. Increasing oropharyngeal and anal cancer incidence highlights the need for prevention strategies beyond cervical screening alone. Strengthening HPV vaccination uptake and sustaining organized cervical screening are critical for reducing future cancer burden.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) causes multiple cancers, and understanding HPV-related cancer burden may help implement effective strategies for cancer prevention.
Design and population
The Estonian Cancer Registry provided data on all cases of eight HPV-related cancer sites diagnosed in Estonia during 1995-2022.
Core methods
Age-standardized incidence trends were analyzed using joinpoint regression, estimating annual percentage change (APC), and the number of HPV-attributable cases was estimated using internationally derived site-specific attributable fractions, as tumor HPV status was not available.
Main results
In all, 11,266 cases of HPV-related cancer sites were diagnosed, of which 6,263 were estimated to be attributable to HPV; over 40% occurred before age 55. In women, estimated average annual number of HPV-attributable cases nearly quadrupled for oropharyngeal cancer and tripled for anal cancer. A significant increase in oropharyngeal and anal cancer incidence was observed among women (APC 10.0 and 3.8, respectively), while cervical cancer incidence declined after 2012 (APC -5.6). Survival improved for oropharyngeal cancer in men (from 13 to 44%) and vaginal cancer in women (from 45 to 73%).
Interpretation and novelty
HPV-related cancer patterns in Estonia are shifting from cervical to non-cervical cancers, with increasing oropharyngeal and anal cancer incidence highlighting the need for prevention strategies beyond cervical screening alone.
Scientific and public-health significance
Strengthening HPV vaccination uptake and sustaining organized cervical screening are critical for reducing future cancer burden, as the findings emphasize the shifting burden of HPV-related cancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relied on internationally derived site-specific attributable fractions due to unavailability of tumor HPV status, which may introduce uncertainty in attribution estimates. The evidence strength is moderate, given the population-based registry design but the limitation in direct HPV testing.

宫颈癌 筛查中 人乳头瘤病毒 检测呈阳性时女性的经历、反应和应对策略:一项定性研究。

Women's experiences, responses, and coping strategies when testing positive for human papillomavirus in cervical cancer screening: A qualitative study.
作者： Joan Hansen, Beth J. Maclin, Birgitte Folmann, Jane Ege Møller, Ane Kersti Skaarup Knudsen, Vibeke Raaby, Anne Hammer
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 包括丹麦在内的许多国家正在采用基于 人乳头瘤病毒 的宫颈筛查,因为其敏感性比细胞学检查更高。然而,HPV 的性传播性质可能会引起性心理困扰。我们的目的是探索:(1) 女性的性心理经历,以及 (2) 影响她们经历的因素以及与常规 宫颈癌 筛查相关的应对策略。材料和方法 我们于 2024 年 9 月至 2025 年 2 月在丹麦 Gødstrup 医院妇产科进行了一项定性研究。我们纳入了因高危 HPV 阳性筛查而转诊接受阴道镜检查的 30-60 岁女性。如果女性以前有 治疗 切除史,则被排除在外。我们进行了深入的半结构化访谈。所有访谈均被录音并逐字转录,然后使用现象学方法进行主题分析。结果 我们纳入了 21 名女性,平均年龄为 42.5 岁(范围:32-55 岁)。通过邮件收到高危 HPV 结果后,女性无法立即获得支持或信息,导致她们对癌症风险感到焦虑,对自己的健康状况和 治疗 的后续步骤感到困惑。全科医生 (GP) 被认为是令人安心的来源,但并不总是那么容易联系到。认识到 HPV 作为一种性传播病毒的影响会引发内疚、羞耻和尴尬的感觉,以及不清洁和身体脱节的感觉。诊断 还会使人际关系紧张并扰乱性亲密关系。尽管通过阴道镜进行的后续妇科会诊令人放心,但由于耻辱和羞耻等情感负担,整个过程变得复杂。简单地“遵守”的感觉也阻碍了关于 HPV 的性本质的公开讨论和个人担忧的表达。结论 基于实践理论框架,本研究表明,女性在宫颈筛查中感染高危 HPV 诊断 后的经历不仅仅是个人对医疗信息的反应,还应该在更广泛的心理、社会和文化背景下进行理解,这些背景塑造了女性如何处理和应对 诊断。这些发现强调需要采取整体的患者护理方法,为受 HPV 影响的女性提供支持和信息丰富的做法,同时还需要更广泛的社会努力来减少耻辱并提高公众意识。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨女性的性心理经历以及影响其经历的因素以及与常规 宫颈癌 筛查相关的应对策略,特别关注 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 检测的影响。
设计与对象
2024 年 9 月至 2025 年 2 月,丹麦 Gødstrup 医院妇产科进行了一项定性研究,涉及 21 名 30-60 岁的女性,因 高危型HPV 筛查呈阳性而转诊接受阴道镜检查。
核心方法
我们进行了深入的半结构化访谈、录音、逐字转录,并使用现象学方法进行主题分析。
主要结果
女性在收到邮寄的 高危型HPV 结果后会感到焦虑、困惑、内疚、羞耻和尴尬,感觉不干净、身体脱节、人际关系紧张、性亲密受到破坏。
解释与创新
研究表明,女性在 高危型HPV 诊断 后的经历是由心理、社会和文化背景决定的,而不仅仅是个人对医疗信息的反应。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要采取全面的患者护理方法,为受 HPV 影响的女性提供支持性和信息丰富的做法,同时还需要更广泛的社会努力,以减少耻辱并提高公众意识。
局限与证据强度
由于该研究的定性性质,并且排除了既往有 治疗 切除史的女性,因此证据强度中等,这可能会限制普遍性。

Women's experiences, responses, and coping strategies when testing positive for human papillomavirus in cervical cancer screening: A qualitative study.

Authors: Joan Hansen, Beth J. Maclin, Birgitte Folmann, Jane Ege Møller, Ane Kersti Skaarup Knudsen, Vibeke Raaby, Anne Hammer
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Many countries, including Denmark, are adopting human papillomavirus‐based cervical screening due to its higher sensitivity compared to cytology. However, the sexually transmitted nature of HPV may evoke psychosexual distress. We aimed to explore: (1) women's psychosexual experiences, and (2) factors that shape their experiences and coping strategies in relation to routine cervical cancer screening. Material and Methods We conducted a qualitative study at the Department of Obstetrics and Gynecology, Gødstrup Hospital, Denmark from September 2024 to February 2025. We included women aged 30–60 referred to colposcopy due to a high‐risk HPV‐positive screening. Women were excluded if they had a previous history of excisional treatment. We conducted in‐depth semi‐structured interviews. All interviews were audiotaped and transcribed verbatim, after which a thematic analysis was performed using a phenomenological approach. Results We included 21 women with a median age of 42.5 years (range: 32–55 years). Receiving a high‐risk HPV result by mail left women without immediate support or information, causing feelings of anxiety about cancer risk, confusion about their health and next steps in treatment. The general practitioner (GP) was perceived as a source of reassurance, but was not always easily accessible. Recognizing the impact of HPV as a sexually transmitted virus triggered feelings of guilt, shame, and embarrassment, as well as sensations of uncleanliness and bodily disconnection. The diagnosis also strained relationships and disrupted sexual intimacy. Although the follow‐up gynecological consultation with colposcopy was reassuring, it was experienced as complex due to emotional burdens such as stigma and shame. A sense of needing to simply “comply” also hindered open discussion about the sexual nature of HPV and the expression of personal concerns. Conclusions Based on a practice theoretical framework, this study shows that women's experiences following a high‐risk HPV diagnosis in the context of cervical screening are not merely individual responses to medical information, but should be understood within a broader psychological, social, and cultural context that shapes how women process and cope with the diagnosis. These findings underscore the need for a holistic approach to patient care that fosters supportive and informative practices for women affected by HPV, coupled with a broader societal effort to reduce stigma and increase public awareness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore women's psychosexual experiences and factors shaping their experiences and coping strategies in relation to routine cervical cancer screening, particularly focusing on the impact of human papillomavirus (HPV) testing.
Design and population
A qualitative study was conducted at the Department of Obstetrics and Gynecology, Gødstrup Hospital, Denmark, from September 2024 to February 2025, involving 21 women aged 30–60 referred to colposcopy due to a high-risk HPV-positive screening.
Core methods
In-depth semi-structured interviews were conducted, audiotaped, transcribed verbatim, and analyzed thematically using a phenomenological approach.
Main results
Women experienced anxiety, confusion, guilt, shame, and embarrassment upon receiving a high-risk HPV result by mail, with feelings of uncleanliness and bodily disconnection, and strained relationships and disrupted sexual intimacy.
Interpretation and novelty
The study shows that women's experiences following a high-risk HPV diagnosis are shaped by psychological, social, and cultural contexts, rather than being merely individual responses to medical information.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings underscore the need for a holistic approach to patient care that fosters supportive and informative practices for women affected by HPV, coupled with broader societal efforts to reduce stigma and increase public awareness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the qualitative nature of the study and the exclusion of women with a previous history of excisional treatment, which may limit generalizability.

公布最佳 宫颈癌 筛查年龄范围:印度尼西亚大都市区 75,719 名女性 HPV 感染研究(2012 年至 2022 年)。

Unveiling Optimal Cervical Cancer Screening Age Range: Study of HPV Infection Among 75,719 Women in Indonesia's Metropolitan Regions (2012 - 2022).
作者： Tofan Widya Utami, Laila Nuranna, Gatot Purwoto, Arisda Oktalia, Raysa Irzami, Andi Utama, Eva Suarthana
摘要中文翻译目标:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是导致全球健康危机 宫颈癌 的主要原因。印度尼西亚被列为东南亚 宫颈癌 发病率 和死亡率最高的国家。目前,根据国家宫颈癌消除(NCCE)计划,印度尼西亚已将基于HPV DNA的筛查从30-50岁扩大到30-69岁。然而,多种因素可能导致较年轻人群的病毒清除不足,而且印度尼西亚尚未对特定年龄的HPV感染报告进行大规模分析,使得理想的筛查年龄不确定。因此,本研究旨在调查印度尼西亚的年龄特异性 HPV 患病率,以了解 宫颈癌 筛查的最佳年龄范围。方法:在这项回顾性横断面研究中,利用了 2012 年至 2020 年印度尼西亚所有大都市区的 75,719 名受试者的数据,通过 PCR (SPF10-DEIA-LiPA25) 和杂交对他们进行了 HPV DNA 检测。然后使用 IBM SPSS 27.0 按年龄和 HPV 基因型对数据进行分析。结果:75,719 名 13-75 岁女性中,12.62% HPV 阳性。30-39岁(39.56%)和40-49岁(21.1%)女性感染率最高,其次是25-29岁(18.9%)和25岁以下(8.8%)。这与 60-65 岁(1.5%)和 66-69 岁(0.6%)女性的感染率形成鲜明对比。在所有年龄组中,HPV52、HPV16 和 HPV58 是最常见的高危基因型,感染率差异很大 (p <0.001)。在所有年龄组中,高危型HPV 的数量都超过了低危 HPV。结论:HPV 阳性 患病率 在印度尼西亚的年轻人中,尤其是 25-29 岁的人群中很常见。65 岁以后,HPV 阳性病例显着下降。有几个变量促成了这一点。本研究建议调整 25-65 岁印度尼西亚女性的 宫颈癌 筛查。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是导致全球健康危机 发病率 的主要原因,而印度尼西亚的 发病率 和死亡率是东南亚最高的。然而,由于印度尼西亚年龄特异性 HPV 感染数据不足,理想的筛查年龄范围仍不确定。
设计与对象
这项回顾性横断面研究利用了 2012 年至 2020 年印度尼西亚大都市区 75,719 名 13-75 岁女性的数据,通过 PCR (SPF10-DEIA-LiPA25) 和杂交对她们进行了 HPV DNA 检测。
核心方法
使用 IBM SPSS 27.0 按年龄和 HPV 基因型对数据进行分析,以确定年龄特异性 患病率 和基因型分布。
主要结果
75,719 名女性中有 12.62% HPV 阳性,其中 30-39 岁(39.56%)和 40-49 岁(21.1%)女性的感染率最高,其次是 25-29 岁(18.9%)和 25 岁以下(8.8%),而 65 岁以后则显着下降(60-65 岁和 60-65 岁为 1.5% 和 0.6%)。分别为 66-69)。
解释与创新
作者将年轻人群(25-29 岁)中频繁出现的 HPV 阳性以及 65 岁后的显着下降解释为调整筛查年龄范围的证据,强调了印度尼西亚大规模特定年龄 HPV 数据的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,印尼的宫颈癌筛查应重点关注25-65岁的女性,根据年龄特异性HPV 患病率优化资源分配和早期检测。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性横断面设计限制了因果推断,但大样本量和全面的年龄分析为特定年龄的 患病率 估计提供了适度的可信度。

Unveiling Optimal Cervical Cancer Screening Age Range: Study of HPV Infection Among 75,719 Women in Indonesia's Metropolitan Regions (2012 - 2022).

Authors: Tofan Widya Utami, Laila Nuranna, Gatot Purwoto, Arisda Oktalia, Raysa Irzami, Andi Utama, Eva Suarthana
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the primary cause of cervical cancer, a global health crisis. Indonesia is placed as having the highest cervical cancer incidence and fatality in Southeast Asia. Currently, under the National Cervical Cancer Elimination (NCCE) plan, Indonesia has extended HPV DNA-based screening from 30-50 to 30-69 years old. However, various factors can lead to inadequate virus clearance in younger populations, and Indonesia has not yet had a large-scale analysis of age-specific HPV infection reports, leaving the ideal screening age uncertain. Therefore, this study aims to investigate age-specific HPV prevalence in Indonesia to inform the optimal age range for cervical cancer screening. METHODS: In this retrospective cross-sectional study, 75,719 subjects' data were utilized from all metropolitan regions in Indonesia from 2012-2020, who were tested with HPV DNA using PCR (SPF10-DEIA-LiPA25) and hybridization. The data were then analyzed by age and HPV genotype using IBM SPSS 27.0. RESULTS: 12.62% of 75,719 women aged 13-75 are HPV-positive. Women aged 30-39 (39.56%) and 40-49 (21.1%) had the highest infection rates, followed by 25-29 (18.9%) and those below 25 years (8.8%). This contrasts with the infection rates of women aged 60-65 (1.5%) and 66-69 (0.6%). Across all age categories, HPV52, HPV16, and HPV58 were the most prevalent high-risk genotypes, with substantial variations in infection rates (p <0.001). High-risk HPV outnumbered low-risk HPV in all age categories. CONCLUSION: HPV-positive prevalence in the younger population, particularly among those aged 25-29, is frequent in Indonesia. After age 65, HPV-positive cases drop significantly. Several variables contribute to this. This study recommends adjusting cervical cancer screening to Indonesian women aged 25-65.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the primary cause of cervical cancer, a global health crisis, and Indonesia has the highest incidence and fatality in Southeast Asia. However, the ideal screening age range remains uncertain due to insufficient age-specific HPV infection data in Indonesia.
Design and population
This retrospective cross-sectional study utilized data from 75,719 women aged 13-75 across Indonesia's metropolitan regions from 2012-2020, who were tested for HPV DNA using PCR (SPF10-DEIA-LiPA25) and hybridization.
Core methods
The data were analyzed by age and HPV genotype using IBM SPSS 27.0 to determine age-specific prevalence and genotype distribution.
Main results
12.62% of 75,719 women were HPV-positive, with the highest rates in women aged 30-39 (39.56%) and 40-49 (21.1%), followed by 25-29 (18.9%) and below 25 (8.8%), while rates dropped significantly after age 65 (1.5% and 0.6% for 60-65 and 66-69, respectively).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the frequent HPV positivity in younger populations (25-29) and significant decline after age 65 as evidence for adjusting screening age ranges, highlighting the novelty of large-scale age-specific HPV data in Indonesia.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that cervical cancer screening in Indonesia should focus on women aged 25-65, optimizing resource allocation and early detection based on age-specific HPV prevalence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's retrospective cross-sectional design limits causal inference, but the large sample size and comprehensive age analysis provide moderate confidence in the age-specific prevalence estimates.

伊朗德黑兰转诊至三级妇科肿瘤诊所的女性对 人乳头瘤病毒 和 宫颈癌 的态度和知识

Attitudes and Knowledge About Human Papillomavirus and Cervical Cancer among Women Referred to a Tertiary Gynecologic Oncology Clinic, Tehran, Iran
作者： Elham Shirali, Fariba Yarandi, Elham Feizabad, Mariyah Zehra, Saba Sabet, Sara Ramhormozian, Arash Jafarieh, Pershang Nazemi
摘要中文翻译背景和目标:宫颈癌 是全球主要的公共卫生问题。本研究的目的是评估在三级妇科诊所就诊的女性对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 和 宫颈癌 的态度和知识。材料和方法:这项描述性横断面调查对 2023 年至 2024 年德黑兰医科大学附属亚斯综合医院妇科诊所转诊的 139 名女性进行。数据收集通过面对面采访。使用 SPSS(IBM SPSS Statistics for Windows,版本 25)进行数据分析。P<0.05 被认为具有统计学显着性。结果:平均总知识得分为 25.07±5.92,满分为 33 分。参与者表现出很强的基础意识,正确地将 HPV 识别为性传播感染,认识到 HPV 影响两性,并认识到其在导致 宫颈癌 中的作用。然而,对垂直 传播 风险的了解以及缺乏针对 HPV 的特定 抗病毒药物 治疗 的知识很少。教育程度与知识水平显着相关(P=0.01)。事实上,教育程度是知识得分的最强预测因素。结论:在这种临床环境中,女性对 HPV 的基本认知度较高。然而,对于 治疗 选项、垂直 传播 和 疫苗接种 功效仍然存在严重误解。这些发现凸显了针对这些知识差距的教育干预措施的迫切需要,特别是在教育程度较低的女性中。他们还强调需要进行结构化的 HPV 教育和针对文化的宣传活动。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 是全球主要的公共卫生问题。本研究旨在评估在三级妇科诊所就诊的女性对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 和 宫颈癌 的态度和知识。
设计与对象
这项描述性横断面调查针对 2023 年至 2024 年转诊至德黑兰医科大学附属亚斯综合医院妇科诊所的 139 名女性进行。
核心方法
数据是通过面对面访谈收集的。使用 SPSS(IBM SPSS Statistics for Windows,版本 25)进行数据分析。
主要结果
知识总分平均分为 25.07±5.92 分(满分为 33 分)。教育程度与知识水平显着相关(P=0.01)。
解释与创新
参与者表现出很强的基础意识,正确地将 HPV 识别为性传播感染,认识到 HPV 影响两性,并认识到其在导致 宫颈癌 中的作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现凸显了针对这些知识差距的教育干预措施的迫切需要,特别是在教育程度较低的女性中。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Attitudes and Knowledge About Human Papillomavirus and Cervical Cancer among Women Referred to a Tertiary Gynecologic Oncology Clinic, Tehran, Iran

Authors: Elham Shirali, Fariba Yarandi, Elham Feizabad, Mariyah Zehra, Saba Sabet, Sara Ramhormozian, Arash Jafarieh, Pershang Nazemi
Original abstract (English)
Background & Objective: Cervical cancer is a major public health concern worldwide. This study was conducted with aim to assess the attitudes and knowledge about Human Papillomavirus (HPV) and cervical cancer among women attending a tertiary gynecologic clinic.Materials & Methods: This descriptive, cross-sectional survey was conducted among 139 women referred to the gynecologic clinics of Yas Hospital Complex, affiliated with Tehran University of Medical Sciences, from 2023 to 2024. Data were collected through face-to-face interviews. Data analysis was performed using SPSS (IBM SPSS Statistics for Windows, Version 25). P<0.05 was considered statistically significant.Results: The mean total knowledge score was 25.07±5.92 out of a possible 33 points. Participants demonstrated strong foundational awareness, correctly identifying HPV as a sexually transmitted infection, recognizing that HPV affects both genders, and recognizing its role in causing cervical cancer. However, knowledge of the risks of vertical transmission and the lack of a specific antiviral treatment for HPV was low. Educational attainment was significantly associated with knowledge levels (P=0.01). Indeed, educational attainment was the strongest predictor of knowledge scores.Conclusion: In this clinical setting, basic HPV awareness was high among women. However, substantial misconceptions persisted regarding treatment options, vertical transmission, and vaccination efficacy. These findings highlight an urgent need for educational interventions targeting these knowledge gaps, particularly among women with lower educational attainment. They also underscore the need for structured HPV education and culturally tailored awareness campaigns.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer is a major public health concern worldwide. This study aimed to assess attitudes and knowledge about Human Papillomavirus (HPV) and cervical cancer among women attending a tertiary gynecologic clinic.
Design and population
This descriptive, cross-sectional survey was conducted among 139 women referred to the gynecologic clinics of Yas Hospital Complex, affiliated with Tehran University of Medical Sciences, from 2023 to 2024.
Core methods
Data were collected through face-to-face interviews. Data analysis was performed using SPSS (IBM SPSS Statistics for Windows, Version 25).
Main results
The mean total knowledge score was 25.07±5.92 out of a possible 33 points. Educational attainment was significantly associated with knowledge levels (P=0.01).
Interpretation and novelty
Participants demonstrated strong foundational awareness, correctly identifying HPV as a sexually transmitted infection, recognizing that HPV affects both genders, and recognizing its role in causing cervical cancer.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight an urgent need for educational interventions targeting these knowledge gaps, particularly among women with lower educational attainment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 E6/E7 癌蛋白的免疫组织化学检测与前列腺癌的侵袭性临床病理特征相关:苏丹 队列研究 研究

Immunohistochemical detection of high-risk human papillomavirus E6/E7 oncoproteins is associated with aggressive clinicopathological features in prostate cancer: a Sudanese cohort study
作者： Alsmawal Elimam, Ibrahim Hadad, Elsadig Mohammed, Stefano Cacciatore, Dalia Ahmed
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 与前列腺癌之间的潜在联系,因为尽管有越来越多的证据表明 HPV 在前列腺癌发展中的作用仍不确定,但在某些人群中可能存在关联。
设计与对象
这项回顾性病例对照研究是在苏丹 队列研究 上进行的,涉及 100 份福尔马林固定石蜡包埋的前列腺组织标本,其中包括 50 例经组织学证实的前列腺癌病例和 50 例良性前列腺增生对照。
核心方法
使用单克隆抗体进行免疫组织化学评估高危型HPV E6/E7癌蛋白表达,并使用H评分系统进行半定量评分;统计分析包括卡方、Mann-Whitney U 检验、逻辑回归和优势比计算,以评估与临床病理变量的关联。
主要结果
High-risk HPV E6/E7 immunoreactivity was significantly more common in prostate cancer (48%) than in benign prostatic hyperplasia (0%), with prostate cancer tissues having over four times higher odds of HPV positivity (OR = 4.21, 95% CI: 1.69–10.43); positive immunostaining was associated with higher Gleason Grade groups (p = 0.0035) and advanced tumor stages (p = 0.004), but not with serum PSA levels.
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明 HPV 相关蛋白表达与前列腺癌的侵袭性肿瘤特征之间可能存在联系,这为了解 HPV 在苏丹人群中这种恶性肿瘤中的作用做出了新的贡献。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调需要进一步进行分子 HPV 检测和基因分型研究,以阐明 HPV 在前列腺癌中的生物学作用,并可能为 HPV 相关前列腺癌的 监测 策略和治疗方法提供信息。
局限与证据强度
由于该研究的回顾性设计和对免疫组织化学的依赖,证据强度中等,无法确认活动性 HPV 感染或建立因果关系;局限性包括无法证明 HPV 的因果作用以及需要更全面的分子方法。

Immunohistochemical detection of high-risk human papillomavirus E6/E7 oncoproteins is associated with aggressive clinicopathological features in prostate cancer: a Sudanese cohort study

Authors: Alsmawal Elimam, Ibrahim Hadad, Elsadig Mohammed, Stefano Cacciatore, Dalia Ahmed
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The role of human papillomavirus (HPV) in prostate cancer development remains uncertain, despite accumulating evidence of a potential link in certain populations. While HPV is a known cause of several epithelial cancers, its connection to prostate cancer (PCa) has not been definitively proven. This research assessed the immunohistochemical presence of high-risk HPV E6/E7 oncoproteins in prostate cancer and benign prostatic hyperplasia (BPH) tissues from a Sudanese cohort and explored their association with clinicopathological features. Methods A retrospective case-control study involved 100 prostate tissue specimens fixed in formalin and embedded in paraffin, consisting of 50 histologically confirmed PCa cases and 50 BPH controls. The expression of high-risk HPV E6/E7 proteins was assessed by immunohistochemistry with monoclonal antibodies, and staining was semi-quantitatively scored using the H-score system. The relationship between HPV immunoreactivity and clinicopathological variables was analyzed with various statistical tests, including chi-square, Mann-Whitney U tests, logistic regression, and odds ratio calculations. Results High-risk HPV E6/E7 immunoreactivity was significantly more common in prostate cancer than in benign prostatic hyperplasia (48% vs. Prostate cancer tissues had over four times higher odds of HPV positivity compared to benign tissues (OR = 4.21, 95% CI: 1.69–10.43). Positive immunostaining was notably associated with higher Gleason Grade groups (p = 0.0035) and advanced tumor stages (p = 0.004), while no significant link was found with serum prostate-specific antigen (PSA) levels. Multivariable logistic regression confirmed that higher tumor grade and pathological stage were independently associated with HPV E6/E7 immunoreactivity, even after adjustment for PSA. Conclusions In this Sudanese cohort, high-risk HPV E6/E7 immunoreactivity was associated with prostate cancer and more severe pathological features. These results suggest a possible connection between HPV-related protein expression and aggressive tumor traits. However, since immunohistochemistry alone cannot confirm active HPV infection or prove its causal role in prostate cancer development, caution is needed when interpreting these findings. Future research should include molecular HPV detection, viral genotyping, and RNA-based studies to better understand HPV's biological role in prostate cancer.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the potential link between high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) and prostate cancer, as the role of HPV in prostate cancer development remains uncertain despite accumulating evidence of a possible association in certain populations.
Design and population
This retrospective case-control study was conducted on a Sudanese cohort, involving 100 formalin-fixed paraffin-embedded prostate tissue specimens, comprising 50 histologically confirmed prostate cancer cases and 50 benign prostatic hyperplasia controls.
Core methods
Immunohistochemistry with monoclonal antibodies was used to assess high-risk HPV E6/E7 oncoprotein expression, which was semi-quantitatively scored using the H-score system; statistical analyses included chi-square, Mann-Whitney U tests, logistic regression, and odds ratio calculations to evaluate associations with clinicopathological variables.
Main results
High-risk HPV E6/E7 immunoreactivity was significantly more common in prostate cancer (48%) than in benign prostatic hyperplasia (0%), with prostate cancer tissues having over four times higher odds of HPV positivity (OR = 4.21, 95% CI: 1.69–10.43); positive immunostaining was associated with higher Gleason Grade groups (p = 0.0035) and advanced tumor stages (p = 0.004), but not with serum PSA levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as suggesting a possible connection between HPV-related protein expression and aggressive tumor traits in prostate cancer, representing a novel contribution to understanding HPV's role in this malignancy within the Sudanese population.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the need for further molecular HPV detection and genotyping studies to clarify HPV's biological role in prostate cancer, potentially informing surveillance strategies and therapeutic approaches in HPV-associated prostate cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the study's retrospective design and reliance on immunohistochemistry, which cannot confirm active HPV infection or establish causality; limitations include the inability to prove HPV's causal role and the need for more comprehensive molecular methods.

持续感染 HPV 的绝经后女性的细胞学阴性下生殖道病变负担:基因型特异性和多中心关联

Cytology-negative lower-genital-tract lesion burden in postmenopausal women with persistent HPV infection: genotype-specific and multicentric associations
作者： Hong Tang, Luyao Xu, Zhaofeng Fan, Mingli Chen, Jinyan Zhu, Lili Zhou, Meicheng Luo, Huachan Gan, Jing Xiao, Xu Luyao
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于上皮萎缩、取样困难和转化区迁移,绝经后持续的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染在临床上具有挑战性,这可能会降低细胞学提供的保证。该研究旨在描述持续感染 HPV 的细胞学阴性绝经后女性的病变负担,特别是同时评估宫颈、阴道和外阴疾病时。
设计与对象
对2023年至2025年广东省中医院大学城分院妇科门诊治疗的绝经后HPV持续感染女性进行回顾性研究。所有353份筛查记录均符合持续感染定义,其中223名女性(63.2%)存在NILM细胞学检查。
核心方法
持续感染被定义为相同基因型 HPV 感染在记录的感染开始日期和首次就诊之间持续至少 12 个完整的日历月。细胞学阴性状态定义为 ThinPrep 细胞学检测上皮内病变或恶性肿瘤 (NILM) 呈阴性。主要病理结果是病变状态和级别,分为无HPV相关病变、低级别病变(LSIL/低级别病变)或高级别病变(HSIL/高级别病变)。
主要结果
Among women with NILM cytology, 134 (60.1%) had no HPV-related lesion, 89 (39.9%) had an HPV-related lower genital tract lesion, 79 (35.4%) had a low-grade lesion, 10 (4.5%) had a high-grade lesion, and 22 (9.9%) had multicentric involvement. Women with abnormal cytology had higher odds of low-grade lesion (OR 1.88, 95% CI 1.21–2.92) and high-grade lesion (OR 4.58, 95% CI 2.10–9.97).
解释与创新
作者解释说,NILM 细胞学检查并不能排除持续 HPV 感染的绝经后妇女中具有临床意义的病变负担。他们还指出,HPV16/18 阳性表明女性的病变几率较高,而多重 HPV 感染与多中心受累相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在绝经后持续 HPV 管理中考虑 HPV 基因型和整个下生殖道的评估,这可以为更准确的 监测 和 诊断 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的选择偏倚而受到限制。由于缺乏前瞻性数据且依赖现有记录,证据强度中等。

Cytology-negative lower-genital-tract lesion burden in postmenopausal women with persistent HPV infection: genotype-specific and multicentric associations

Authors: Hong Tang, Luyao Xu, Zhaofeng Fan, Mingli Chen, Jinyan Zhu, Lili Zhou, Meicheng Luo, Huachan Gan, Jing Xiao, Xu Luyao
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Persistent human papillomavirus (HPV) infection after menopause is clinically challenging because epithelial atrophy, sampling difficulty, and transformation-zone migration may reduce the reassurance provided by cytology. Although HPV genotyping is now central to risk-based management, lesion burden among cytology-negative postmenopausal women with persistent HPV infection remains insufficiently characterized, particularly when cervical, vaginal, and vulvar disease are assessed together. Methods We conducted a retrospective study of postmenopausal women with persistent human papillomavirus (HPV) infection who were managed in the gynecological outpatient department of Guangdong Provincial Hospital of Chinese Medicine, University Town Branch, from 2023 to 2025. Persistent infection was defined as same-genotype HPV infection lasting at least 12 completed calendar months between the recorded infection-start date and the index visit. Cytology-negative status was defined as negative for intraepithelial lesion or malignancy (NILM) on ThinPrep cytologic testing. The primary pathological outcome was lesion status and grade, classified as no HPV-related lesion, low-grade lesion (LSIL/low-grade lesion), or high-grade lesion (HSIL/high-grade lesion). Secondary outcomes included anatomic lesion site and multicentric lower-genital-tract involvement, defined as HPV-related lesions involving at least two distinct genital sites among the cervix, vagina, and vulva. Results All 353 screened records met the persistent-infection definition. NILM cytology was present in 223 women (63.2%). Among women with NILM cytology, 134 (60.1%) had no HPV-related lesion, 89 (39.9%) had an HPV-related lower genital tract lesion, 79 (35.4%) had a low-grade lesion, 10 (4.5%) had a high-grade lesion, and 22 (9.9%) had multicentric involvement. Compared with women with NILM cytology, women with abnormal cytology had higher odds of low-grade lesion (OR 1.88, 95% CI 1.21–2.92) and high-grade lesion (OR 4.58, 95% CI 2.10–9.97). HPV16/18 positivity was associated with any lesion in the overall cohort (OR 3.02, 95% CI 1.87–4.87) and among NILM women (OR 2.72, 95% CI 1.53–4.84). Multiple HPV infection was associated with multicentric involvement in the overall cohort (OR 2.00, 95% CI 1.05–3.78) and in the NILM subgroup (OR 2.79, 95% CI 1.14–6.82). Conclusions NILM cytology did not exclude clinically meaningful lesion burden in postmenopausal women with persistent HPV infection. HPV16/18 positivity identified women with higher lesion odds, whereas multiple HPV infection was associated with multicentric involvement. These findings support assessment that considers both HPV genotype and the full lower genital tract in postmenopausal persistent HPV management.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent human papillomavirus (HPV) infection after menopause is clinically challenging due to epithelial atrophy, sampling difficulty, and transformation-zone migration, which may reduce the reassurance provided by cytology. The study aimed to characterize lesion burden among cytology-negative postmenopausal women with persistent HPV infection, particularly when cervical, vaginal, and vulvar disease are assessed together.
Design and population
A retrospective study was conducted on postmenopausal women with persistent HPV infection managed in the gynecological outpatient department of Guangdong Provincial Hospital of Chinese Medicine, University Town Branch, from 2023 to 2025. All 353 screened records met the persistent-infection definition, and NILM cytology was present in 223 women (63.2%).
Core methods
Persistent infection was defined as same-genotype HPV infection lasting at least 12 completed calendar months between the recorded infection-start date and the index visit. Cytology-negative status was defined as negative for intraepithelial lesion or malignancy (NILM) on ThinPrep cytologic testing. The primary pathological outcome was lesion status and grade, classified as no HPV-related lesion, low-grade lesion (LSIL/low-grade lesion), or high-grade lesion (HSIL/high-grade lesion).
Main results
Among women with NILM cytology, 134 (60.1%) had no HPV-related lesion, 89 (39.9%) had an HPV-related lower genital tract lesion, 79 (35.4%) had a low-grade lesion, 10 (4.5%) had a high-grade lesion, and 22 (9.9%) had multicentric involvement. Women with abnormal cytology had higher odds of low-grade lesion (OR 1.88, 95% CI 1.21–2.92) and high-grade lesion (OR 4.58, 95% CI 2.10–9.97).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that NILM cytology did not exclude clinically meaningful lesion burden in postmenopausal women with persistent HPV infection. They also noted that HPV16/18 positivity identified women with higher lesion odds, whereas multiple HPV infection was associated with multicentric involvement.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the assessment that considers both HPV genotype and the full lower genital tract in postmenopausal persistent HPV management, which can inform more accurate surveillance and diagnosis strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of prospective data and the reliance on existing records.

'...but look how many lives would have been saved if this was available then' 探讨未来人乳头瘤病毒自我采样的可接受性,以及苏格兰智力障碍人群进行宫颈癌筛查的障碍与促进因素

'...but look how many lives would have been saved if this was available then' Exploring the acceptability of future Human Papillomavirus self-sampling, and the barriers and facilitators of cervical cancer screening for those with intellectual disabilities in Scotland.
作者： Catherine B White, Lesley M McGregor
摘要中文翻译背景:苏格兰智障女性 (ID) 的 宫颈癌 筛查出勤率约为 30%,远低于总人口 69% 的比率和世界卫生组织建议的 70% 目标。HPV 自我采样可以为这一服务不足的人群提供更方便的筛查选择。本研究旨在了解患有 ID 的女性对 宫颈癌 筛查以及 HPV 自我采样的可接受性的看法和经验。方法:利用焦点小组对参与苏格兰第三部门社区团体的智障女性及其护理人员进行了一项定性研究。2024 年 9 月至 10 月期间举办了四次焦点小组,共有 13 名参与者(11 名患有智力障碍的女性,2 名护理人员)。使用反思性主题分析对数据进行分析。结果:出现了五个主题:(1) 国家卫生服务 (NHS) 沟通是痛苦的根源;(2) 医疗保健提供者和智障妇女之间的权力失衡:感觉被忽视、被忽视和失控;(3) 护理不足造成的持久后果;(4)过去的创伤塑造了未来的回避;(5) 自我抽样作为赋权的途径。参与者表现出对自我采样的高度接受度,将其视为克服传统筛查障碍的潜在解决方案。结论:这项研究揭示了当前 宫颈癌 针对 ID 女性的筛查服务存在显着的系统性缺陷,同时强调了自我采样的变革潜力。成功的实施需要全面的可获取信息、加强的医疗保健专业培训和创伤知情护理方法。自我采样可以显着推进健康公平目标,同时改善这一服务不足人群的医疗保健结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
苏格兰智障女性 (ID) 参加 宫颈癌 筛查的比例约为 30%,远低于总人口 69% 的比例和世界卫生组织 70% 的目标。该研究旨在探索筛查的障碍和促进因素,并评估未来 HPV 自我采样作为这一服务不足群体更容易获得的选择的可接受性。
设计与对象
通过焦点小组对苏格兰各地的智障女性及其护理人员进行了一项定性研究。2024 年 9 月至 10 月期间举办了四次焦点小组,涉及 13 名参与者(11 名患有智障的女性和 2 名护理人员)。
核心方法
通过焦点小组收集数据,并使用反思性主题分析进行分析,以确定与筛查经验和自我抽样可接受性相关的关键主题。
主要结果
出现了五个主题:NHS 沟通是痛苦的根源;医疗保健提供者和智障女性之间的权力失衡;护理不足造成的持久后果;过去的创伤塑造了未来的回避;自我抽样作为赋权的途径。参与者表现出对自我采样的高度接受度,将其视为克服传统筛查障碍的潜在解决方案。
解释与创新
该研究强调了当前针对智障女性的 宫颈癌 筛查服务存在系统性不足,同时将自我采样确定为一种变革性的潜在解决方案。新颖之处在于集中智障女性的声音来揭示未满足的需求并提出可行的改变。
科研与公卫意义
通过解决 宫颈癌 筛查项目中的准入障碍和创伤知情护理差距,自我采样可以推进健康公平目标并改善患有智力障碍的女性的医疗保健结果。
局限与证据强度
定性设计限制了普遍性,而且样本量较小(n = 13)可能无法捕捉智力障碍女性经历的全部多样性。由于缺乏定量验证并且依赖自我报告的观点,证据强度中等。

'...but look how many lives would have been saved if this was available then' Exploring the acceptability of future Human Papillomavirus self-sampling, and the barriers and facilitators of cervical cancer screening for those with intellectual disabilities in Scotland.

Authors: Catherine B White, Lesley M McGregor
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Cervical cancer screening attendance among women with intellectual disabilities (ID) in Scotland is approximately 30%, significantly below the general population rate of 69% and the WHO's recommended 70% target. HPV self-sampling could provide a more accessible screening option for this underserved population. This study aimed to understand the views and experiences of women with ID regarding cervical cancer screening and the acceptability of HPV self-sampling. METHODS: A qualitative study using focus groups was conducted with women with ID who attended third-sector community groups across Scotland, along with their carers. Four focus groups were held between September and October 2024, with a total of 13 participants (11 women with ID, 2 carers). Data were analysed using reflexive thematic analysis. RESULTS: Five main themes emerged: (1) National Health Service (NHS) communication as a source of distress; (2) power imbalances between health care providers and women with ID: feeling unheard, dismissed and out of control; (3) enduring consequences of inadequate care; (4) past trauma shapes future avoidance; and (5) self-sampling as a pathway to empowerment. Participants demonstrated high acceptability of self-sampling, viewing it as a potential solution to overcome barriers to traditional screening. CONCLUSION: This research reveals significant systemic inadequacies in current cervical cancer screening services for women with ID, whilst highlighting self-sampling's transformative potential. Successful implementation requires comprehensive accessible information, enhanced health care professional training and trauma-informed care approaches. Self-sampling could significantly advance health equity goals whilst improving health care outcomes for this underserved population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Women with intellectual disabilities (ID) in Scotland attend cervical cancer screening at approximately 30%, far below the general population rate of 69% and the WHO target of 70%. The study sought to explore barriers and facilitators to screening and to assess the acceptability of future HPV self-sampling as a more accessible option for this underserved group.
Design and population
A qualitative study using focus groups was conducted with women with ID and their carers across Scotland. Four focus groups were held between September and October 2024, involving 13 participants (11 women with ID and 2 carers).
Core methods
Data were collected through focus groups and analysed using reflexive thematic analysis to identify key themes related to screening experiences and self-sampling acceptability.
Main results
Five main themes emerged: NHS communication as a source of distress; power imbalances between healthcare providers and women with ID; enduring consequences of inadequate care; past trauma shaping future avoidance; and self-sampling as a pathway to empowerment. Participants demonstrated high acceptability of self-sampling, viewing it as a potential solution to overcome barriers to traditional screening.
Interpretation and novelty
The research highlights systemic inadequacies in current cervical cancer screening services for women with ID while identifying self-sampling as a transformative potential solution. The novelty lies in centring the voices of women with ID to reveal unmet needs and propose actionable changes.
Scientific and public-health significance
Self-sampling could advance health equity goals and improve healthcare outcomes for women with ID by addressing access barriers and trauma-informed care gaps in cervical cancer screening programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The qualitative design limits generalisability, and the small sample size (n=13) may not capture the full diversity of experiences among women with ID. Evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of quantitative validation and reliance on self-reported perspectives.

一名患有声门鳞状细胞原位癌的年轻女性的 HPV18 DNA 检测和病理性喉咽反流:简要报告

HPV18 DNA detection and pathological laryngopharyngeal reflux in a young woman with glottic squamous cell carcinoma in situ: a brief report
作者： Zhi Liu, Jinhong Zhang, Wenqing Chen, Jiaming Zheng, Thiansze Wong, Wenbin Lei, Kexing Lyu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在没有传统危险因素的年轻女性中,声门鳞状细胞原位癌很少见,这增加了吸烟和饮酒以外病因的不确定性。
设计与对象
这是一名 26 岁女性单例患者 病例报告,患有声门鳞状细胞原位癌且持续性声音嘶哑,接受经口激光切除术和术后抗反流管理。
核心方法
使用 Lcn PCR 在福尔马林固定石蜡包埋的肿瘤组织中检测到高危 HPV18 DNA,并通过 24 小时多通道腔内阻抗 pH 监测确定病理性咽喉反流。对 535 个基因进行靶向测序,评估驱动 突变、肿瘤 突变 负荷和微卫星不稳定性。
主要结果
在肿瘤组织中检测到HPV18 DNA,证实病理性咽喉反流,靶向测序显示无驱动突变、肿瘤突变负荷升高或微卫星不稳定。随访时患者未复发。
解释与创新
作者将 HPV18 的检测和病理反流解释为这种非典型病例中致癌的可能因素,并指出不存在常规危险因素和基因组改变。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持对临床非典型喉鳞状病变,特别是年轻不吸烟女性进行选择性病毒学评估和个体化反流评估。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于单一案件,排除因果推断;仅摘要来源缺乏详细的方法和长期后续数据,因此评估可信度较低。

HPV18 DNA detection and pathological laryngopharyngeal reflux in a young woman with glottic squamous cell carcinoma in situ: a brief report

Authors: Zhi Liu, Jinhong Zhang, Wenqing Chen, Jiaming Zheng, Thiansze Wong, Wenbin Lei, Kexing Lyu
Original abstract (English)
Laryngeal cancer predominantly affects elderly males with long-term smoking and alcohol histories, and its occurrence in young females is extremely rare. We report a 26-year-old woman with glottic squamous cell carcinoma in situ who presented with persistent hoarseness. She had no history of smoking, alcohol use, or radiation exposure. Upon evaluation, high-risk HPV18 DNA was detected in FFPE tumor tissue by Lcn PCR, and 24-hour MII-pH monitoring indicated pathological laryngopharyngeal reflux. Targeted sequencing of 535 genes showed no driver mutations, tumor mutation burden elevation, or microsatellite instability. The patient underwent transoral laser resection followed by postoperative anti-reflux management. At follow-up, she remained recurrence-free. This case suggests a possible contribution of HPV18 and laryngopharyngeal reflux to carcinogenesis in a young non-smoking woman with glottic carcinoma in situ. The findings support selective virologic evaluation and individualized reflux assessment in clinically atypical laryngeal squamous lesions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The rarity of glottic squamous cell carcinoma in situ in young females without traditional risk factors raised uncertainty about contributing aetiologies beyond smoking and alcohol exposure.
Design and population
This is a single-patient case report of a 26-year-old woman with glottic squamous cell carcinoma in situ and persistent hoarseness, treated with transoral laser resection and postoperative anti-reflux management.
Core methods
High-risk HPV18 DNA was detected in formalin-fixed paraffin-embedded tumor tissue using Lcn PCR, and 24-hour multichannel intraluminal impedance pH monitoring identified pathological laryngopharyngeal reflux. Targeted sequencing of 535 genes assessed for driver mutations, tumor mutation burden, and microsatellite instability.
Main results
HPV18 DNA was detected in the tumor tissue, pathological laryngopharyngeal reflux was confirmed, and targeted sequencing revealed no driver mutations, elevated tumor mutation burden, or microsatellite instability. The patient remained recurrence-free at follow-up.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the detection of HPV18 and pathological reflux as possible contributors to carcinogenesis in this atypical case, noting the absence of conventional risk factors and genomic alterations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support selective virologic evaluation and individualized reflux assessment in clinically atypical laryngeal squamous lesions, particularly in young non-smoking women.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to a single case, precluding causal inference; the abstract-only source lacks detailed methodology and long-term follow-up data, warranting a low confidence assessment.

智利中北部地区公共卫生系统中的患病率或人乳头瘤病毒。

Prevalence of human papillomavirus in the public health system of the central-northern region of Chile.
作者： Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
摘要中文翻译目标 此前,我们发现在私人医疗系统就诊的女性中 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 的 患病率 较高。因此,本研究的目的是确定在公共卫生系统就诊的大量 队列研究 女性中 HR-HPV 的频率,并比较这些结果。方法 使用 qPCR 和巴氏技术对 2020 年至 2024 年间参加妇科检查的 18 至 91 岁女性的 39,512 份宫颈样本进行分析。结果 2020-2024年HR-HPV感染总体患病率为13.70%(95% CI:13.37-14.05%),2020年、2021年分别为13.07%、12.64%、14.80%、14.24%和14.15%。分别为 2022 年、2023 年和 2024 年。在25岁以下女性中,评估期间HR-HPV的患病率为34.03%,而25至44岁女性为16.35%,45至64岁女性为10.47%,64岁以上女性为11.52%。子宫颈抹片检查显示,HPV 阴性女性的 NILM 结果为 89.71%,而 HPV 阳性女性的 NILM 结果为 74.59%。HPV阴性女性中LSIL和HSIL的发生率分别为0.14%和0.07%;然而,在 HPV 阳性女性中,这些值分别增加到 4.73% 和 4.43%。HPV 阴性女性中只有 0.006% 可能患有癌症,而 HPV 阳性女性中则有 0.46% 可能患有癌症。结论 中北部地区公共卫生系统女性 HR-HPV 感染 患病率 似乎与智利之前报告的相似,但低于同一地区私营医疗系统女性报告的 患病率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定就读公共卫生系统的女性中 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 的频率,并将这些结果与之前报告的私人卫生系统数据进行比较。
设计与对象
队列研究 的一项研究分析了 2020 年至 2024 年间在智利中北部地区接受妇科检查的 39,512 份 18 至 91 岁女性的宫颈样本。
核心方法
使用 qPCR 和 Papanicolaou 技术对样本进行测试,以检测 HR-HPV 感染和细胞学异常。
主要结果
总体 HR-HPV 患病率 为 13.70%(95% CI:13.37-14.05%),与年龄较大的群体相比,25 岁​​以下女性的比率较高(34.03%)。与 HPV 阴性女性相比,HPV 阳性女性的 LSIL(4.73% vs. 0.14%)和 HSIL(4.43% vs. 0.07%)发生率更高。
解释与创新
作者解释说,公共卫生系统中的 HR-HPV 患病率 与之前的国家报告相似,但低于私人卫生系统,突显了各个医疗部门 HPV 感染率的潜在差异。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为有针对性的 HPV 筛查策略(特别是针对年轻女性)提供了信息,并支持公共卫生干预措施,以减少服务不足人群中的 HPV 相关疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究的优势在于其大样本量和纵向设计,但其局限性包括来自单区域数据的潜在选择偏差以及对自我报告的医疗就诊情况的依赖。

Prevalence of human papillomavirus in the public health system of the central-northern region of Chile.

Authors: Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE Previously, we found a high prevalence of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) in women attending the private health system. Therefore, the objective of this study was to determine the frequency of HR-HPV in a large cohort of women attending the public health system and to compare these findings. METHODS A total of 39,512 cervical samples from women aged 18 to 91 years who attended gynecological check-ups between 2020 and 2024 were analyzed using qPCR and the Papanicolaou technique. RESULTS The overall prevalence of HR-HPV infection between 2020 and 2024 was 13.70% (95% CI: 13.37-14.05%), with annual values of 13.07%, 12.64%, 14.80%, 14.24%, and 14.15% in 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, and 2024, respectively. In women under 25 years of age, the prevalence of HR-HPV was 34.03% during the evaluated period, compared to 16.35% in women aged 25 to 44 years, 10.47% in those aged 45 to 64 years, and 11.52% in women over 64 years of age. Pap smears showed 89.71% NILM results in HPV-negative women, compared to 74.59% in HPV-positive women. The rates of LSIL and HSIL were 0.14% and 0.07% in HPV-negative women, respectively; however, these values increased to 4.73% and 4.43% in HPV-positive women, respectively. Probable carcinoma was found in only 0.006% of HPV-negative women, but in 0.46% of HPV-positive women. CONCLUSIONS The prevalence of HR-HPV infection in women attending the public health system in the central-northern region appears to be similar to that previously reported in Chile, but lower than that reported in the same region for women attending the private health system.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine the frequency of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) in women attending the public health system, comparing these findings to previously reported data from the private health system.
Design and population
A cohort study analyzed 39,512 cervical samples from women aged 18 to 91 years who attended gynecological check-ups between 2020 and 2024 in the central-northern region of Chile.
Core methods
Samples were tested using qPCR and the Papanicolaou technique to detect HR-HPV infection and cytological abnormalities.
Main results
The overall HR-HPV prevalence was 13.70% (95% CI: 13.37-14.05%), with higher rates in women under 25 years (34.03%) compared to older age groups. HPV-positive women showed increased rates of LSIL (4.73% vs. 0.14%) and HSIL (4.43% vs. 0.07%) compared to HPV-negative women.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HR-HPV prevalence in the public health system was similar to previous national reports but lower than in the private health system, highlighting potential disparities in HPV infection rates across healthcare sectors.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings inform targeted HPV screening strategies, particularly for younger women, and support public health interventions to reduce HPV-related diseases in underserved populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's strength lies in its large sample size and longitudinal design, but limitations include potential selection bias from single-region data and reliance on self-reported healthcare attendance.

定相扩增子多重测序可经济有效地检测宫颈样本中的高风险 人乳头瘤病毒

Phased amplicon multiplex sequencing for cost-effective detection of high-risk human papillomavirus from cervical samples
作者： Dipesh Solanky, Charlotte Low, Christine L. Hathaway, Stephen Cherne, Elizabeth Brown, Thesla Palanee-Phillips, Ruanne V. Barnabas, Roby Paul Bhattacharyya, Brittany Berdy, Jonathan Livny
摘要中文翻译获得准确、经济高效的高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 分型技术对于扩大 宫颈癌 筛查和为 疫苗接种 策略提供信息至关重要。与临床标准定量聚合酶链反应 (qPCR) 检测相比,通过新一代测序 (NGS) 进行 HPV 基因分型提供了更大的灵活性、可扩展性和基因型特异性。我们开发了一种 HPV 基因分型方法,即 HPV 定相扩增子多重测序 (PhAM-Seq),该方法使用具有短、可变长度内联序列的扩增子组合条形码,与传统的扩增子测序方法相比,可实现更高的通量和更低的每个样本成本。我们使用针对 L1 和 E6-E7 基因位点的简并和类型特异性引物,在之前通过 Seegene Anyplex II HPV28 检测 qPCR 测定进行分型的 170 个宫颈样本的盲法 队列研究 中评估了 HPV PhAM-Seq。在八种常见的 hrHPV 类型(HPV16、18、31、33、35、45、52 和 58)中,HPV PhAM-Seq 与使用简并 L1 靶向引物的 qPCR 的总体一致性超过 80%,其中 HPV16、31、33 和 58 的灵敏度最高。HPV35、45 和 52 的灵敏度提高到使用靶向基因 E6/E7 的类型特异性引物可达到 85% 或更高。并行处理和测序使单个技术人员能够每周检测数百个样本,每个样本的试剂成本约为 10 美元,实验室自动化和更高输出平台上的测序可进一步扩展规模并降低成本,达到适合群体水平 监测 的规模。我们包含详细的 SOP;用于引物设计、测序文库构建和样品跟踪的工具;以及数据分析所需的所有脚本,以确保 HPV PhAM-Seq 可以轻松实现可扩展、经济高效的 hrHPV 基因分型或扩展到其他类似应用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发和评估一种经济有效的方法来检测高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV),以改进 宫颈癌 筛查和 疫苗接种 策略。
设计与对象
该研究使用盲法 队列研究 评估了 HPV 定相扩增子多重测序 (PhAM-Seq),该样本包含 170 个宫颈样本,这些样本之前由 Seegene Anyplex II HPV28 检测 qPCR 分析进行分型。
核心方法
该方法称为 HPV 定相扩增子多重测序 (PhAM-Seq),使用具有短、可变长度内联序列的扩增子组合条形码,与传统的扩增子测序方法相比,可实现更高的通量和更低的每个样本成本。并行处理和测序允许一名技术人员每周分析数百个样本,每个样本的试剂成本约为 10 美元。
主要结果
在八种常见的 hrHPV 类型(HPV16、18、31、33、35、45、52 和 58)中,HPV PhAM-Seq 与使用简并 L1 靶向引物的 qPCR 的总体一致性超过 80%,其中对 HPV16、31、33 和 58 的灵敏度最高。
解释与创新
作者解释说,该方法为 hrHPV 基因分型提供了一种可扩展且经济高效的替代传统 qPCR 的方法,并且在使用类型特异性引物时对某些类型的灵敏度有所提高。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,HPV PhAM-Seq 可用于人群水平的 监测,并提高 宫颈癌 筛查和 疫苗接种 策略的效率。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 170 个宫颈样本的盲法 队列研究,并且证据基于单一方法,这可能会影响普遍性和可扩展性。

Phased amplicon multiplex sequencing for cost-effective detection of high-risk human papillomavirus from cervical samples

Authors: Dipesh Solanky, Charlotte Low, Christine L. Hathaway, Stephen Cherne, Elizabeth Brown, Thesla Palanee-Phillips, Ruanne V. Barnabas, Roby Paul Bhattacharyya, Brittany Berdy, Jonathan Livny
Original abstract (English)
Access to accurate cost-effective technologies for typing high-risk human papillomaviruses (hrHPV) is critical to expand cervical cancer screening and inform vaccination strategies. Compared with clinical-standard quantitative polymerase chain reaction (qPCR) assays, HPV genotyping by next-generation sequencing (NGS) provides greater flexibility, scalability, and genotype specificity. We have developed a method for HPV genotyping, HPV Phased Amplicon Multiplex Sequencing (PhAM-Seq), that uses combinatorial barcoding of amplicons with short, variable-length inline sequences to enable higher throughput and lower per-sample costs than conventional amplicon sequencing approaches. We evaluated HPV PhAM-Seq using degenerate and type-specific primers targeting the L1 and E6-E7 gene loci in a blinded cohort of 170 cervical samples previously typed by the Seegene Anyplex II HPV28 Detection qPCR assay. Across eight common hrHPV types (HPV16, 18, 31, 33, 35, 45, 52, and 58), HPV PhAM-Seq demonstrated >80% overall agreement with qPCR using degenerate L1-targeting primers, with the highest sensitivity for HPV16, 31, 33, and 58. Sensitivity for HPV35, 45, and 52 improved to 85% or greater with type-specific primers targeting genes E6/E7. Parallel processing and sequencing enables a single technician to assay hundreds of samples per week at a reagent cost of around $10 USD per sample, with laboratory automation and sequencing on higher-output platforms enabling further scaling and cost reduction to a scale amenable to population-level surveillance. We include a detailed SOP; tools for primer design, sequencing library construction, and sample tracking; and all scripts needed for data analysis to ensure HPV PhAM-Seq can be readily implemented for scalable, cost-effective hrHPV genotyping or extended to other similar applications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop and evaluate a cost-effective method for detecting high-risk human papillomaviruses (hrHPV) to improve cervical cancer screening and vaccination strategies.
Design and population
The study evaluated HPV Phased Amplicon Multiplex Sequencing (PhAM-Seq) using a blinded cohort of 170 cervical samples previously typed by the Seegene Anyplex II HPV28 Detection qPCR assay.
Core methods
The method, HPV Phased Amplicon Multiplex Sequencing (PhAM-Seq), uses combinatorial barcoding of amplicons with short, variable-length inline sequences to enable higher throughput and lower per-sample costs than conventional amplicon sequencing approaches. Parallel processing and sequencing allow a single technician to assay hundreds of samples per week at a reagent cost of around $10 USD per sample.
Main results
Across eight common hrHPV types (HPV16, 18, 31, 33, 35, 45, 52, and 58), HPV PhAM-Seq demonstrated >80% overall agreement with qPCR using degenerate L1-targeting primers, with the highest sensitivity for HPV16, 31, 33, and 58.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the method provides a scalable and cost-effective alternative to conventional qPCR for hrHPV genotyping, with improved sensitivity for certain types when using type-specific primers.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that HPV PhAM-Seq could be used for population-level surveillance and improve the efficiency of cervical cancer screening and vaccination strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a blinded cohort of 170 cervical samples, and the evidence is based on a single methodological approach, which may affect generalisability and scalability.

在阿片类药物 治疗 计划中使用 人乳头瘤病毒 自收集测试进行 宫颈癌 筛查

Cervical Cancer Screening With Human Papillomavirus Self-Collection Testing in an Opioid Treatment Program
作者： Elizabeth M. Swisher, Mallory Davis, Mayumi Rubin-Saika, Enna Manhardt, Emiko Oshima, Renata R. Urban, Stephen L. Cherne, John Lin, Rachel L. Winer, Eric C. Huang
摘要中文翻译重要性:患有阿片类药物使用障碍和物质使用障碍的个体的 宫颈癌 筛查率低于一般人群。人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 自采集检测提供了将筛查范围扩大到以前未覆盖的患者群体的机会。目的:评估将用于 宫颈癌 筛查的 HPV 自采集检测纳入阿片类药物使用障碍和物质使用障碍患者常规护理的可行性和采用情况,并跟踪该人群的 高危型HPV 率和指南一致的后续护理。设计、设置和参与者:这项前瞻性 队列研究 研究于 2024 年 6 月 24 日至 2025 年 12 月 31 日在 We Care Daily Clinics、联邦认证的阿片类药物 治疗 计划 (OTP) 和位于华盛顿州奥本的美国印第安人健康设施进行。符合资格的患者年龄为 25 岁或以上,有子宫颈,且逾期进行 宫颈癌 筛查或不确定上次筛查。干预:在患者年度健康访问期间,使用自我收集套件进行筛查,并在诊所卫生间私下进行自我收集。在实验室对样品进行了 14 种 高危型HPV 类型的分析。筛查出 HPV 阳性结果的患者接受了教育并获得了符合指南的护理建议。OTP 工作人员协助患者转诊至当地临床医生以获得妇科服务,并跟踪随访情况。主要结果和措施:主要结果是 HPV 自我采集检测的采用率、HPV 阳性率以及与后续护理的成功联系。结果:共有 194 名患者(中位年龄 [范围],41 [25-65] 岁)接受了评估。在 174 名符合 宫颈癌 筛查资格的患者 (89.7%) 中,158 名 (90.8%) 完成了 HPV 自我采集检测。0 例检测结果不理想,128 例患者 (81.0%) HPV 阴性结果,7 例 (4.4%) HPV-16 或 HPV-18 阳性结果,23 例 (14.6%) 其他 高危型HPV 型(仅限非 HPV-16 或 HPV-18 型)结果阳性。在 30 例 HPV 检测结果呈阳性的患者中,有 2 例 (6.7%) 失访,12 例 (40.0%) 迄今为止已完成随访,这通常需要临床医生进行多次协调尝试。30 名患者中的其余 16 名患者 (53.3%) 目前正在积极参与各个阶段的转诊和安排。结论和相关性:在这项 队列研究 研究中,将 宫颈癌 筛查纳入使用 HPV 自我收集检测的 OTP 中是可行的,患者资格和接受率很高。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
患有阿片类药物使用障碍和物质使用障碍的个体的 宫颈癌 筛查率低于一般人群,而 HPV 自我收集检测为将筛查范围扩大到以前未覆盖的患者群体提供了机会。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性 队列研究 研究于 2024 年 6 月 24 日至 2025 年 12 月 31 日在 We Care Daily Clinics、联邦认证的阿片类药物 治疗 计划和华盛顿州奥本的美洲印第安人健康设施进行,符合条件的患者为 25 岁或以上、有宫颈、逾期进行 宫颈癌 筛查或不确定是否上次筛查。
核心方法
在患者每年的健康访问期间,使用自我收集套件进行筛查,并在诊所卫生间私下进行自我收集;在实验室对样本进行了 14 种 高危型HPV 类型分析,HPV 阳性患者接受了教育并获得了符合指南的护理建议。
主要结果
总共对 194 名患者进行了评估,174 名符合条件的患者中有 158 名(90.8%)完成了 HPV 自采集检测;检测结果不理想的有 0 例,其中 128 例(81.0%)HPV 阴性,7 例(4.4%)HPV-16 或 HPV-18 阳性,23 例(14.6%)其他 高危型HPV 类型阳性。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,使用 HPV 自我收集检测将 宫颈癌 筛查纳入阿片类药物 治疗 计划是可行的,患者资格和采用率较高。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为扩大对阿片类药物使用障碍服务不足人群的 宫颈癌 筛查范围提供了依据,证明了自我收集测试在提高高危人群筛查率方面的潜力。
局限与证据强度
由于单中心设置和有限的随访数据,证据强度中等,其局限性包括潜在的普遍性限制和 HPV 阳性患者的随访不完整。

Cervical Cancer Screening With Human Papillomavirus Self-Collection Testing in an Opioid Treatment Program

Authors: Elizabeth M. Swisher, Mallory Davis, Mayumi Rubin-Saika, Enna Manhardt, Emiko Oshima, Renata R. Urban, Stephen L. Cherne, John Lin, Rachel L. Winer, Eric C. Huang
Original abstract (English)
Importance: Individuals with opioid use disorder and substance use disorder have lower cervical cancer screening rates than the general population. Human papillomavirus (HPV) self-collection testing provides opportunities to expand screening access to previously unreached patient populations. Objectives: To assess the feasibility and uptake of incorporating HPV self-collection testing for cervical cancer screening into routine care for patients with opioid use disorder and substance use disorder, and to track the rates of high-risk HPV and guideline concordant follow-up care in this population. Design, Setting, and Participants: This prospective cohort study was conducted from June 24, 2024, through December 31, 2025, at We Care Daily Clinics, a federally certified opioid treatment program (OTP) and American Indian Health Facility in Auburn, Washington. Eligible patients were 25 years or older, had a cervix, and were either overdue for cervical cancer screening or unsure of their last screening. Intervention: Screening using self-collection kits was offered during the patient's annual wellness visit, and self-collection occurred privately in the clinic restroom. Samples were analyzed for 14 high-risk HPV types at a laboratory. Screened patients with HPV-positive results received education and recommendations for guideline-concordant care. OTP staff facilitated patient referrals to local clinicians for gynecologic services, and follow-up was tracked. Main Outcomes and Measures: The primary outcomes were the rates of HPV self-collection testing uptake, HPV positivity, and successful linkage to follow-up care. Results: A total of 194 patients (median [range] age, 41 [25-65] years) were evaluated. Of 174 patients (89.7%) eligible for cervical cancer screening, 158 (90.8%) completed HPV self-collection testing. There were 0 unsatisfactory test results, and 128 patients (81.0%) had HPV-negative results, 7 (4.4%) had HPV-16- or HPV-18-positive results, and 23 (14.6%) had positive results for other high-risk HPV types (non-HPV-16 or HPV-18 types only). Of the 30 patients with HPV-positive test results, 2 (6.7%) were lost to follow-up and 12 (40.0%) completed follow-up to date, which often required multiple coordination attempts by clinicians. The remaining 16 of 30 patients (53.3%) are currently actively engaged in various stages of referral and scheduling. Conclusions and Relevance: In this cohort study, incorporation of cervical cancer screening into an OTP using HPV self-collection testing was feasible with high patient eligibility and uptake.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Individuals with opioid use disorder and substance use disorder have lower cervical cancer screening rates than the general population, and HPV self-collection testing provides opportunities to expand screening access to previously unreached patient populations.
Design and population
This prospective cohort study was conducted from June 24, 2024, through December 31, 2025, at We Care Daily Clinics, a federally certified opioid treatment program and American Indian Health Facility in Auburn, Washington, with eligible patients being 25 years or older, having a cervix, and either overdue for cervical cancer screening or unsure of their last screening.
Core methods
Screening using self-collection kits was offered during the patient's annual wellness visit, with self-collection occurring privately in the clinic restroom; samples were analyzed for 14 high-risk HPV types at a laboratory, and HPV-positive patients received education and recommendations for guideline-concordant care.
Main results
A total of 194 patients were evaluated, with 158 (90.8%) of 174 eligible patients completing HPV self-collection testing; there were 0 unsatisfactory test results, with 128 (81.0%) HPV-negative, 7 (4.4%) HPV-16 or HPV-18-positive, and 23 (14.6%) positive for other high-risk HPV types.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that incorporation of cervical cancer screening into an opioid treatment program using HPV self-collection testing was feasible with high patient eligibility and uptake.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform strategies to expand cervical cancer screening access for underserved populations with opioid use disorder, demonstrating the potential of self-collection testing to improve screening rates in high-risk groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the single-site setting and limited follow-up data, with limitations including potential generalizability constraints and incomplete follow-up for HPV-positive patients.
