头颈鳞状细胞癌中 HPV16 E7 相关的 SERPINB3 抑制和 MYC 相关上皮可塑性

HPV16 E7-Associated SERPINB3 Suppression and MYC-Related Epithelial Plasticity in Head and Neck Squamous Cell Carcinoma
作者： Zengchen Liu, Siwei Zhang, Tianyang Liu, Yanjing Li, Rui Li, Huan Liu, Dongcun Wang, Yunyan Tang, Heng Ma, Yuting Zhang
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染定义了头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC) 的一种独特亚型。尽管 HPV 阳性 (HPV+) HNSCC 通常比 HPV 阴性 (HPV−) 疾病表现出更好的总体生存率,但它经常与颈部淋巴结转移相关。然而,HPV 相关转移进展的上皮细胞状态和病毒基因相关机制仍不完全清楚。本研究旨在鉴定 HPV+ HNSCC 中与转移相关的上皮亚群,并探讨 HPV16 E7 在调节转移程序中的潜在作用。方法:对来自配对的原发性和转移性 HNSCC 样本的公共单细胞 RNA 测序数据集进行整合和分析,以表征恶性上皮亚群。进行拷贝数变异(CNV)推断、聚类、通路富集、干性评分和轨迹分析来定义转移相关的上皮状态。TCGA 转录组数据和基于组织的验证用于支持候选基因筛选。使用 SERPINB3 敲低和 HPV16 早期基因过表达 CAL27 细胞模型进行体外功能测定。结果:单细胞分析鉴定出 HPV+ 和 HPV− HNSCC 中的干细胞样转移上皮亚群。在 HPV+ 转移性病变中,ALDH2+/LAMB3+ 上皮亚群表现出上皮-间质转化活性升高和干细胞样特征。SERPINB3 在 HPV+ 转移进展过程中表现出动态表达模式。功能分析表明,SERPINB3 敲除增强了 CAL27 细胞迁移和侵袭,并与 MYC 和上皮间质转化相关转录程序的激活相关。在HPV16早期基因中,E5、E6、E6*和E7表现出不同程度的SERPINB3抑制,而表达E7的细胞表现出与上皮可塑性和转移相关程序相关的明显转录改变。结论:本研究鉴定了 HPV 相关 HNSCC 中的干细胞样转移上皮状态,并提出了参与转移上皮可塑性的潜在 HPV16 早期基因 -SERPINB3-MYC 相关调节机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染定义了头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC) 的一种独特亚型,尽管 HPV 阳性 (HPV+) HNSCC 通常比 HPV 阴性 (HPV−) 疾病表现出更好的总体生存率,但它经常与颈部淋巴结转移相关。
设计与对象
本研究整合并分析了配对原发性和转移性 HNSCC 样本的公共单细胞 RNA 测序数据集,以表征恶性上皮亚群。
核心方法
使用 SERPINB3 敲低和 HPV16 早期基因过表达 CAL27 细胞模型进行拷贝数变异 (CNV) 推断、聚类、通路富集、干性评分、轨迹分析、TCGA 转录组数据、基于组织的验证和体外功能测定。
主要结果
单细胞分析确定了 HPV+ 和 HPV− HNSCC 中的干细胞样转移上皮亚群,其中 HPV+ 转移性病变中的 ALDH2+/LAMB3+ 上皮亚群显示出上皮-间质转化活性升高和干细胞样特征。
解释与创新
该研究鉴定了 HPV 相关 HNSCC 中的干细胞样转移上皮状态,并提出了与转移上皮可塑性相关的潜在 HPV16 早期基因 SERPINB3-MYC 相关调节机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 HPV 相关转移进展的分子机制提供了见解,这可能为 HPV+ HNSCC 靶向治疗的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于公共数据集和体外模型,这可能会限制研究结果的普遍性;然而,多种分析方法的整合支持中等水平的证据强度。

HPV16 E7-Associated SERPINB3 Suppression and MYC-Related Epithelial Plasticity in Head and Neck Squamous Cell Carcinoma

Authors: Zengchen Liu, Siwei Zhang, Tianyang Liu, Yanjing Li, Rui Li, Huan Liu, Dongcun Wang, Yunyan Tang, Heng Ma, Yuting Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection defines a distinct subtype of head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC). Although HPV-positive (HPV+) HNSCC generally shows better overall survival than HPV-negative (HPV−) disease, it is frequently associated with cervical lymph node metastasis. However, the epithelial cell states and viral gene-associated mechanisms underlying HPV−related metastatic progression remain incompletely understood. This study aimed to identify metastasis-associated epithelial subpopulations in HPV+ HNSCC and explore the potential role of HPV16 E7 in regulating metastatic programs. Methods: Public single-cell RNA-sequencing datasets from paired primary and metastatic HNSCC samples were integrated and analyzed to characterize malignant epithelial subpopulations. Copy number variation (CNV) inference, clustering, pathway enrichment, stemness scoring, and trajectory analysis were performed to define metastasis-associated epithelial states. TCGA transcriptomic data and tissue-based validation were used to support candidate gene screening. In vitro functional assays were performed using SERPINB3-knockdown and HPV16 early gene-overexpressing CAL27 cell models. Results: Single-cell analysis identified stem-like metastatic epithelial subpopulations in HPV+ and HPV− HNSCC. In HPV+ metastatic lesions, an ALDH2+/LAMB3+ epithelial subpopulation showed elevated epithelial–mesenchymal transition activity and stem-like features. SERPINB3 displayed a dynamic expression pattern during HPV+ metastatic progression. Functional assays showed that SERPINB3 knockdown enhanced CAL27 cell migration and invasion and was associated with activation of MYC- and epithelial–mesenchymal transition-related transcriptional programs. Among HPV16 early genes, E5, E6, E6*, and E7 showed different degrees of SERPINB3 suppression, while E7-expressing cells exhibited distinct transcriptional alterations associated with epithelial plasticity and metastatic-related programs. Conclusions: This study identifies a stem-like metastatic epithelial state in HPV-associated HNSCC and suggests a potential HPV16 early gene-SERPINB3-MYC-related regulatory mechanism involved in metastatic epithelial plasticity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection defines a distinct subtype of head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC), and although HPV-positive (HPV+) HNSCC generally shows better overall survival than HPV-negative (HPV−) disease, it is frequently associated with cervical lymph node metastasis.
Design and population
This study integrated and analyzed public single-cell RNA-sequencing datasets from paired primary and metastatic HNSCC samples to characterize malignant epithelial subpopulations.
Core methods
Copy number variation (CNV) inference, clustering, pathway enrichment, stemness scoring, trajectory analysis, TCGA transcriptomic data, tissue-based validation, and in vitro functional assays using SERPINB3-knockdown and HPV16 early gene-overexpressing CAL27 cell models were performed.
Main results
Single-cell analysis identified stem-like metastatic epithelial subpopulations in HPV+ and HPV− HNSCC, with an ALDH2+/LAMB3+ epithelial subpopulation in HPV+ metastatic lesions showing elevated epithelial–mesenchymal transition activity and stem-like features.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identifies a stem-like metastatic epithelial state in HPV-associated HNSCC and suggests a potential HPV16 early gene-SERPINB3-MYC-related regulatory mechanism involved in metastatic epithelial plasticity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the molecular mechanisms underlying HPV-related metastatic progression, which may inform the development of targeted therapies for HPV+ HNSCC.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on public datasets and in vitro models, which may limit the generalizability of the findings; however, the integration of multiple analytical approaches supports a moderate level of evidence strength.

问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨湖南省卫生专业人员的知识水平差异及其影响因素,并调查湖南省卫生专业人员对宫颈癌筛查和治疗 HPV相关病变新技术的态度。

采用方便抽样的方式进行多中心横断面调查,向湖南省14个地级市的卫生专业人员发放问卷。

Question and background
This study aimed to explore differences in knowledge levels and their influencing factors, and examine attitudes towards new technologies for cervical cancer screening and treatment for HPV-related lesions among health professionals in Hunan Province.
Design and population
A multicenter cross-sectional survey was conducted using convenience sampling to distribute questionnaires to health professionals across 14 prefecture-level cities in Hunan Province.

A total of 2,777 valid responses were collected, and statistical analysis was performed to examine factors influencing the level of HPV knowledge.

乌兹别克斯坦奇尔奇克妇女中 高危型HPV 基因型组的三年持续性和清除模式:一项试点纵向研究

Three-year persistence and clearance patterns of high-risk HPV genotype groups among women in Chirchik, Uzbekistan: A pilot longitudinal study
作者： Dilobar Utkirovna Abdumalikova
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 的持续存在是宫颈癌发生的核心,但来自中亚的纵向数据仍然有限。这项纵向试点研究评估了乌兹别克斯坦奇尔奇克女性中 hrHPV 基因型组的分布和 3 年随访模式。方法 2021年8月至2022年3月,对5,678名女性进行了hrHPV筛查,其中547名(9.6%)检测结果呈阳性。使用 Xpert HPV 检测进行 HPV 基因分型。单次感染定义为检测到一个基因型组,而多重感染定义为检测到两个或多个基因型组。清除被定义为 3 年随访时 HPV 检测呈阴性。使用 Kaplan-Meier 曲线、对数秩检验和 Cox 回归进行分析。结果 基线时单一感染占主导地位(87.0%、476/547),而双重、三重和四重感染分别占病例的 11.2%、1.5% 和 0.2%。最常检测到的基因型组是 HPV16 (29.8%)、HPV31/33/35/52/58 (19.9%) 和 HPV39/56/66/68 (16.5%)。在 3 年随访中,409 名基线 hrHPV 阳性女性接受了重新评估,其中 108 名仍为 hrHPV 阳性。在持续性 hrHPV 阳性病例中,单一感染仍然占主导地位(94.4%,102/108)。HPV16是随访时最常检测到的基因型组(38.0%),其次是HPV39/56/66/68(24.1%)和HPV31/33/35/52/58(19.4%)。在 36 名女性中观察到新检测到的基因型组,其中 HPV39/56/66/68 最常见(52.8%)。选定的 HPV16 合并感染组的清除率在数值上高于单一 HPV16 感染组,但未观察到统计学上的显着差异(对数秩 p = 0.528)。结论 这项试点研究为乌兹别克斯坦 hrHPV 基因型组的分布和 3 年随访模式提供了初步纵向证据。需要进行更大规模的研究,包括重复的后续测试和基因型水平分析来证实这些发现。

Three-year persistence and clearance patterns of high-risk HPV genotype groups among women in Chirchik, Uzbekistan: A pilot longitudinal study

Authors: Dilobar Utkirovna Abdumalikova
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background High-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) persistence is central to cervical carcinogenesis, yet longitudinal data from Central Asia remain limited. This pilot longitudinal study assessed the distribution of hrHPV genotype groups and 3-year follow-up patterns among women in Chirchik, Uzbekistan. Methods From August 2021 to March 2022, 5,678 women were screened for hrHPV, of whom 547 (9.6%) tested positive. HPV genotyping was performed using the Xpert HPV assay. Single infections were defined as the detection of one genotype group, while multiple infections were defined as the detection of two or more groups. Clearance was defined as a negative HPV test at 3-year follow-up. Kaplan-Meier curves, log-rank tests, and Cox regression were used for analysis. Results Single infections predominated at baseline (87.0%, 476/547), while double, triple, and quadruple infections accounted for 11.2%, 1.5%, and 0.2% of cases, respectively. The most frequently detected genotype groups were HPV16 (29.8%), HPV31/33/35/52/58 (19.9%), and HPV39/56/66/68 (16.5%). At 3-year follow-up, 409 baseline hrHPV-positive women were re-evaluated, and 108 remained hrHPV-positive. Single infections remained predominant among persistent hrHPV-positive cases (94.4%, 102/108). HPV16 was the most frequently detected genotype group at follow-up (38.0%), followed by HPV39/56/66/68 (24.1%) and HPV31/33/35/52/58 (19.4%). Newly detected genotype groups were observed in 36 women, with HPV39/56/66/68 being the most common (52.8%). Clearance rates were numerically higher in selected HPV16 -containing co-infection groups than in single HPV16 infection, but no statistically significant difference was observed (log-rank p = 0.528). Conclusion This pilot study provides preliminary longitudinal evidence on the distribution of hrHPV genotype groups and 3-year follow-up patterns in Uzbekistan. Larger studies with repeated follow-up testing and genotype-level analysis are needed to confirm these findings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
High-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) persistence is central to cervical carcinogenesis, yet longitudinal data from Central Asia remain limited.
Design and population
This pilot longitudinal study assessed the distribution of hrHPV genotype groups and 3-year follow-up patterns among women in Chirchik, Uzbekistan.

Main results
Single infections predominated at baseline (87.0%, 476/547), while double, triple, and quadruple infections accounted for 11.2%, 1.5%, and 0.2% of cases, respectively; at 3-year follow-up, 409 baseline hrHPV-positive women were re-evaluated, and 108 remained hrHPV-positive.

The evidence strength is moderate due to the pilot nature of the study and the need for larger confirmatory studies.

诊断 高风险 诊断 感染后大语言模型对患者问题的反应的评估和比较:专家评级的数字患者教育研究

Evaluation and comparison of large language model responses to patient questions after diagnosis of high-risk human papillomavirus infection: an expert-rated digital patient education study
作者： Zhen Hao, Lin Wang, Yue Wu, Min Zhao, Huijian Li
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在感染高危 诊断 (HPV) 后,患者经常寻求有关癌症风险、随访、治疗、伴侣管理、怀孕和焦虑的指导,这凸显了对可靠的数字患者教育资源的需求。
设计与对象
这项单中心、专家评级的方法学研究向两个大型语言模型 (LLM) 提交了 15 个患者风格的问题,生成了 90 个人工智能生成的答案,并由五位盲法妇科专家进行评估,产生了 450 条专家评级记录。
核心方法
基于常见门诊咨询的 15 个患者风格的问题被提交给 ChatGPT-5.5 Instant 和 DeepSeek-V3,每个问题生成三个独立的答复。五位盲法妇科专家使用 1-5 李克特量表独立评估了所有回答的准确性、安全性、指南一致性、完整性和可理解性,并评估了重大错误和潜在危害。
主要结果
ChatGPT-5.5 Instant had higher response-level scores than DeepSeek-V3 for accuracy (mean difference, 0.25; 95% CI, 0.11–0.39; FDR-adjusted p = 0.010), safety (0.27; 0.07–0.47; p = 0.010), completeness (0.22; 0.07–0.37; p = 0.020), and composite score (0.18; 0.04–0.31; p = 0.027). Responses without major error or potential harm occurred in 42/45 (93.3%; 95% CI, 81.7–98.6%) ChatGPT responses and 38/45 (84.4%; 70.5–93.5%) DeepSeek-V3 responses (risk difference, 8.9 percentage points; 95% CI, −13.3 to 31.1; p = 0.344).
解释与创新
两种模型在评估的质量领域中都表现出了强劲的性能,ChatGPT 在多个领域获得了更高的评级,尽管二元安全性差异不精确且不具有统计显着性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,法学硕士可以对患者有关 高危型HPV 感染的问题提供高质量、符合指南的答复,但这两种模型都需要临床监督以减轻潜在风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到单中心设计的限制,依赖专家评级而不是患者结果,并且缺乏评估模型和问题之外的普遍性,需要进行适度的置信度评估。

Evaluation and comparison of large language model responses to patient questions after diagnosis of high-risk human papillomavirus infection: an expert-rated digital patient education study

Authors: Zhen Hao, Lin Wang, Yue Wu, Min Zhao, Huijian Li
Original abstract (English)
Background After diagnosis of high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) infection, patients often seek guidance on cancer risk, colposcopy, follow-up, treatment, partner management, pregnancy, and anxiety. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for medical consultation, but their response quality and safety in this setting require evaluation. Methods In this single-center, expert-rated methodological study, 15 patient-style questions based on common outpatient consultations were submitted to ChatGPT-5.5 Instant and DeepSeek-V3. Each model generated three independent responses per question, yielding 90 artificial intelligence (AI)-generated responses. Five blinded gynecology experts independently evaluated all responses, producing 450 expert-rating records. Accuracy, safety, guideline concordance, completeness, and understandability were rated on a 1–5 Likert scale. Experts also assessed major error and potential harm. The primary outcome was the proportion of responses without major error or potential harm. Results ChatGPT-5.5 Instant had higher response-level scores than DeepSeek-V3 for accuracy (mean difference, 0.25; 95% CI, 0.11–0.39; FDR-adjusted p = 0.010), safety (0.27; 0.07–0.47; p = 0.010), completeness (0.22; 0.07–0.37; p = 0.020), and composite score (0.18; 0.04–0.31; p = 0.027). The difference in guideline concordance did not remain significant after multiplicity correction (0.16; −0.01 to 0.33; FDR-adjusted p = 0.057), and understandability was similar. Responses without major error or potential harm occurred in 42/45 (93.3%; 95% CI, 81.7–98.6%) ChatGPT responses and 38/45 (84.4%; 70.5–93.5%) DeepSeek-V3 responses (risk difference, 8.9 percentage points; 95% CI, −13.3 to 31.1; p = 0.344). Exploratory question-level risks were observed in HPV16/18 positivity, normal cytology, partner management, and pregnancy scenarios. Conclusion Both two models demonstrated generally strong performance across the evaluated quality domains. ChatGPT achieved higher ratings in several quality domains, but response-level binary safety differences were imprecise and not statistically significant. Both models require guideline-based clinical oversight.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
After a diagnosis of high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) infection, patients frequently seek guidance on cancer risk, follow-up, treatment, partner management, pregnancy, and anxiety, highlighting a need for reliable digital patient education resources.
Design and population
This single-center, expert-rated methodological study submitted 15 patient-style questions to two large language models (LLMs), generating 90 AI-generated responses evaluated by five blinded gynecology experts, producing 450 expert-rating records.
Core methods
Fifteen patient-style questions based on common outpatient consultations were submitted to ChatGPT-5.5 Instant and DeepSeek-V3, each generating three independent responses per question. Five blinded gynecology experts independently evaluated all responses on accuracy, safety, guideline concordance, completeness, and understandability using a 1–5 Likert scale, and assessed major error and potential harm.
Main results
ChatGPT-5.5 Instant had higher response-level scores than DeepSeek-V3 for accuracy (mean difference, 0.25; 95% CI, 0.11–0.39; FDR-adjusted p = 0.010), safety (0.27; 0.07–0.47; p = 0.010), completeness (0.22; 0.07–0.37; p = 0.020), and composite score (0.18; 0.04–0.31; p = 0.027). Responses without major error or potential harm occurred in 42/45 (93.3%; 95% CI, 81.7–98.6%) ChatGPT responses and 38/45 (84.4%; 70.5–93.5%) DeepSeek-V3 responses (risk difference, 8.9 percentage points; 95% CI, −13.3 to 31.1; p = 0.344).
Interpretation and novelty
Both models demonstrated generally strong performance across evaluated quality domains, with ChatGPT achieving higher ratings in several domains, though binary safety differences were imprecise and not statistically significant.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that LLMs can provide high-quality, guideline-concordant responses to patient questions about high-risk HPV infection, but both models require clinical oversight to mitigate potential risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-center design, reliance on expert ratings rather than patient outcomes, and lack of generalizability beyond the evaluated models and questions, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

Regional Head-and-Neck Lymphadenopathy with Dysphagia and Dysphonia: An Airway-Threatening Presentation of HPV-16–Positive Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma (OPSCC)
作者： Zeeshan Rizvi
摘要中文翻译对患者表现的跨学科审查有助于识别替代病理,并促使尽早考虑替代诊断和管理策略。134 简介 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),特别是 HPV-16,目前是口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 的主要原因,主要通过口腔接触传播。尽管 发病率 不断上升,但临床对其可变表现的认识仍然有限。早期识别至关重要,因为即使初始影像显示良性,气道损害也可能迅速进展。病例描述 一名 37 岁女性经历了 14 个月的进行性颈部淋巴结肿大、吞咽困难、发声困难,最终出现右臂丛病。她接受了八次住院治疗,但没有进行淋巴结活检,并接受了多轮类固醇和/或抗生素治疗,暂时掩盖了病情的进展。后来她出现喘鸣,并被送往急诊室,计算机断层扫描显示阴性,她出院了。持续的临床关注促使医生与放射科医生直接沟通并独立重新解释影像学,结果显示广泛的颈部淋巴结肿大,伴有近乎完全的鼻咽阻塞和明显的舌扁桃体肿大压迫软腭。患者紧急返回,气道即将受到损害。继发于 HPV-16 阳性、p16 阳性 OPSCC 的严重上呼吸道阻塞,伴有大淋巴结病变和神经系统受累。由于即将发生气道损害,患者需要使用儿科大小的气管插管进行紧急插管,然后进行气管切开术和四天的机械通气,然后进行最终活检和 诊断。协调气道管理并加快...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了临床对 HPV-16 阳性口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 不同表现的有限认识,强调需要早期识别以防止快速气道损害。
设计与对象
该 病例报告 描述了一名 37 岁女性,患有进行性颈部淋巴结肿大、吞咽困难、发声困难和右臂丛神经病,在确定 诊断 之前曾多次住院治疗。
核心方法
该患者接受了紧急插管、气管切开术、机械通气和最终活检,以确认涉及鼻咽部和口咽部的 HPV-16 阳性鳞状细胞癌。

影像学检查显示广泛的颈部淋巴结肿大,鼻咽几乎完全阻塞,舌扁桃体明显受压;组织病理学证实 HPV-16 阳性 OPSCC 伴有大淋巴结病变和神经系统受累。

科研与公卫意义
包括早期考虑 HPV 相关病因、持续性淋巴结肿大中提示组织 诊断 以及多学科沟通在内的结构化方法可以减少诊断延误并预防危及生命的气道紧急情况。
局限与证据强度
证据强度受到 病例报告 设计的限制,缺乏普遍性,但详细的临床过程和组织病理学证实为研究结果提供了适度的可信度。

Regional Head-and-Neck Lymphadenopathy with Dysphagia and Dysphonia: An Airway-Threatening Presentation of HPV-16–Positive Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma (OPSCC)

Authors: Zeeshan Rizvi
Original abstract (English)
Cross-disciplinary review of patient presentations helps identify alternative pathologies and prompts earlier consideration of alternative diagnoses and management strategies. 134 Introduction Human papillomavirus (HPV), particularly HPV-16, is now a leading cause of oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC), transmitted primarily through oral exposure. Despite rising incidence, clinical awareness of its variable presentations remains limited. Early recognition is essential because airway compromise may progress rapidly even when initial imaging appears benign. CASE DESCRIPTION A 37-year-old woman experienced 14 months of progressive cervical lymphadenopathy, dysphagia, dysphonia, and ultimately right brachial plexopathy. She underwent eight hospitalizations without lymph node biopsy and received multiple rounds of steroids and/or antibiotics, which temporarily masked progression. She later developed stridor and presented to the emergency department where computed tomography was interpreted as negative and she was discharged. Persistent clinical concern prompted direct physician–radiologist communication and independent reinterpretation of imaging, which revealed extensive cervical lymphadenopathy with near-complete nasopharyngeal obstruction and marked lingual tonsillar enlargement compressing the soft palate. The patient returned emergently with impending airway compromise. Severe upper-airway obstruction secondary to HPV-16–positive, p16-positive OPSCC with bulky nodal disease and neurologic involvement. The patient required emergent intubation for impending airway compromise using a pediatric-sized endotracheal tube, followed by tracheotomy and four days of mechanical ventilation before definitive biopsy and diagnosis. Coordinated airway management and expedite...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the limited clinical awareness of variable presentations of HPV-16–positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC), emphasizing the need for early recognition to prevent rapid airway compromise.
Design and population
This case report describes a 37-year-old woman with progressive cervical lymphadenopathy, dysphagia, dysphonia, and right brachial plexopathy who experienced multiple hospitalizations before definitive diagnosis.
Core methods
The patient underwent emergent intubation, tracheotomy, mechanical ventilation, and definitive biopsy to confirm HPV-16–positive squamous cell carcinoma involving the naso- and oropharynx.
Main results
Imaging revealed extensive cervical lymphadenopathy with near-complete nasopharyngeal obstruction and marked lingual tonsillar compression; histopathology confirmed HPV-16–positive OPSCC with bulky nodal disease and neurologic involvement.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that siloed clinical approaches, delayed biopsy, and transient steroid responses can obscure rapidly evolving malignancies, highlighting the need for cross-disciplinary review to identify alternative pathologies.
Scientific and public-health significance
A structured approach including early consideration of HPV-related etiologies, prompt tissue diagnosis in persistent lymphadenopathy, and multidisciplinary communication may reduce diagnostic delays and prevent life-threatening airway emergencies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is limited by the case report design, which lacks generalizability, but the detailed clinical course and histopathological confirmation provide moderate confidence in the findings.

宫颈拭子和尿液样本 高危型HPV 感染和 PAX1 甲基化检测宫颈肿瘤的比较

Comparison of cervical swab and urine samples for high-risk HPV infection and PAX1 methylation in detection of cervical neoplasia
作者： Tsai‐Yin Wei, Wen-Hsuan Lin, Chia-Sui Weng, Yeou-Lih Wang, Chao-Chih Wu, Yu-Chia Hsiao, Li‐Chu Tsai, Hong-Ling Wang, Chih-Long Chang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估宫颈拭子和尿液样本用于检测宫颈肿瘤中 高危型HPV 感染和 PAX1 甲基化的一致性和性能,解决 宫颈癌 筛查中对微创采样方法的需求。
设计与对象
在 104 名患有宫颈细胞学异常和组织病理学确认的女性中进行了一项配对试点研究,评估宫颈拭子和尿液样本检测 高危型HPV 和 PAX1 甲基化的一致性和性能。
核心方法
该研究使用配对样本比较了宫颈拭子和尿液样本之间的 hrHPV 检测和 PAX1 甲基化检测,通过 Cohen κ 统计测量了一致性,并通过敏感性和特异性计算评估了性能。
主要结果
Among 94 pairs with valid hrHPV results, overall agreement was 94.7% (Cohen’s κ = 0.883; 95% CI, 0.783–0.983). For PAX1 methylation, agreement was 79.1% (κ = 0.446; 95% CI, 0.241–0.651).
解释与创新
作者解释说,基于尿液的 hrHPV 检测与宫颈拭子检测的一致性较高,而尿液 PAX1 甲基化的一致性和敏感性较低,表明 宫颈癌 筛查的潜在实用性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,基于尿液的 hrHPV 检测可能是 宫颈癌 筛查中宫颈拭子的可行替代方案,有可能提高可及性并减少侵入性。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项试点,样本量有限,结果可能无法推广。由于研究的探索性和需要进一步验证,证据是中等的。

Comparison of cervical swab and urine samples for high-risk HPV infection and PAX1 methylation in detection of cervical neoplasia

Authors: Tsai‐Yin Wei, Wen-Hsuan Lin, Chia-Sui Weng, Yeou-Lih Wang, Chao-Chih Wu, Yu-Chia Hsiao, Li‐Chu Tsai, Hong-Ling Wang, Chih-Long Chang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Cervical cancer screening commonly requires clinician-collected cervical specimens, prompting interest in less invasive sampling approaches. This paired pilot study assessed agreement in high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) detection and PAX1 methylation testing between cervical swab and urine specimens and evaluated their performance for cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3 or worse (CIN3+). We enrolled 104 women with abnormal cervical cytology and histopathological confirmation. Among 94 pairs with valid hrHPV results, overall agreement was 94.7% (Cohen’s κ = 0.883; 95% CI, 0.783–0.983). Among 91 pairs with valid PAX1 results, agreement was 79.1% (κ = 0.446; 95% CI, 0.241–0.651). For CIN3 + detection, cervical swab and urine hrHPV testing showed sensitivities of 86.8% and 80.6% and specificities of 44.4% and 45.9%, respectively. Cervical swab and urine PAX1 methylation showed lower sensitivities of 52.6% and 41.2% but higher specificities of 85.7% and 90.0%, respectively. Rule-based combinations showed sensitivity–specificity trade-offs. In this referral-based cohort, urine hrHPV testing showed high agreement with cervical swab testing, whereas urine PAX1 methylation had lower agreement and sensitivity. Urine-based PAX1 testing remains exploratory and requires optimization and independent validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the agreement and performance of cervical swab and urine samples for detecting high-risk HPV infection and PAX1 methylation in cervical neoplasia, addressing the need for less invasive sampling methods in cervical cancer screening.
Design and population
A paired pilot study was conducted among 104 women with abnormal cervical cytology and histopathological confirmation, assessing the agreement and performance of cervical swab and urine specimens for detecting high-risk HPV and PAX1 methylation.
Core methods
The study compared hrHPV detection and PAX1 methylation testing between cervical swab and urine specimens using paired samples, with agreement measured by Cohen’s κ statistic and performance evaluated through sensitivity and specificity calculations.
Main results
Among 94 pairs with valid hrHPV results, overall agreement was 94.7% (Cohen’s κ = 0.883; 95% CI, 0.783–0.983). For PAX1 methylation, agreement was 79.1% (κ = 0.446; 95% CI, 0.241–0.651).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that urine-based hrHPV testing showed high agreement with cervical swab testing, while urine PAX1 methylation had lower agreement and sensitivity, indicating potential utility for cervical cancer screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that urine-based hrHPV testing could be a viable alternative to cervical swabs for cervical cancer screening, potentially improving accessibility and reducing invasiveness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a pilot with a limited sample size, and the results may not be generalizable. The evidence is moderate due to the exploratory nature of the research and the need for further validation.

Translate this page into: Retraction: Homeopathic resolution of HPV-related genital warts following robotic hysterectomy: A case report - Received: , Accepted: , Read RETRACTED-ARTICLE associated with this - 10.25259/ASJO_47_2026 The article titled ‘Homeopathic resolution of HPV-related genital warts following robotic hysterectomy: A case report’[1], published in the Asian Journal of Oncology, is hereby retracted. Following publication, the author notified the Editor-in-Chief that the article contained incomplete data due to an oversight during manuscript preparation. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address the scientific integrity issue caused by incomplete data in a published case report. The objective was to retract the article to maintain the accuracy of the scientific record.
Design and population
This is a retraction notice for a previously published case report titled 'Homeopathic resolution of HPV-related genital warts following robotic hysterectomy: A case report' in the Asian Journal of Oncology.
Core methods
The retraction was initiated by the author who notified the Editor-in-Chief about incomplete data due to an oversight during manuscript preparation. The Editorial Board reviewed the matter and determined that retraction was the appropriate action.
Main results
The published report does not accurately or completely represent the underlying findings due to incomplete data. The author has agreed to the retraction of the article.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the retraction is necessary to preserve the integrity of the scientific record, as the published article contains inaccuracies resulting from incomplete data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The retraction ensures that the scientific record remains accurate and reliable, preventing potential misinformation regarding the homeopathic resolution of HPV-related genital warts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is low as this is a retraction notice for a case report with incomplete data, which undermines the reliability of the original findings. The limitation is the lack of complete and accurate data in the original publication.

活乳酸菌制剂治疗HPV感染并发细菌性阴道病患者宫颈炎症及清除病毒感染的疗效

Efficacy of live lactobacillus preparations in treating cervical inflammation and clearing viral infections in patients with HPV infection complicated by bacterial vaginosis
作者： Yan Cui, Xiaoping Cheng

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评价定君生(一种活乳杆菌制剂)辅助治疗人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染并发细菌性阴道病(BV)患者的有效性和安全性。
设计与对象
对152例符合条件的患者进行回顾性分析,随机分为两组:观察组(n=87)接受标准BV抗菌治疗加定君生治疗,对照组(n=65)单独接受标准BV抗菌治疗。对患者进行了六个月的随访。
核心方法
主要结局包括 HPV 清除率和宫颈细胞学炎症改善,次要结局包括 BV 复发率和不良反应 发病率。
主要结果
The results showed that the observation group had a significantly higher HPV clearance rate (73.6% vs 53.9%) and inflammation improvement rate (90.8% vs 76.9%), as well as a lower BV recurrence rate (3.1% vs 13.9%) compared to the control group. Dingjunsheng was identified as an independent protective factor for HPV clearance (OR=2.5, 95%CI:1.3–5.1).
解释与创新
作者得出结论,定君生辅助治疗在改善 HPV 清除、减轻炎症和减少 BV 复发方面显示出有效性,且安全性良好,使其成为这些患者的有价值的 治疗 策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,定君生作为辅助治疗可以改善 HPV 感染并发 BV 患者的预后,有可能降低复发率并提高病毒清除率。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项回顾性分析,可能会引入选择偏差。由于研究设计和样本量,证据强度中等。

Efficacy of live lactobacillus preparations in treating cervical inflammation and clearing viral infections in patients with HPV infection complicated by bacterial vaginosis

Authors: Yan Cui, Xiaoping Cheng
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to evaluate the efficacy and safety of Dingjunsheng, a live Lactobacillus preparation, as adjuvant therapy in patients with human papillomavirus (HPV) infection complicated by bacterial vaginosis (BV). A retrospective analysis was conducted on 152 eligible patients, randomly divided into two groups: the observation group (n=87) receiving standard BV antibacterial therapy plus Dingjunsheng, and the control group (n=65) who received standard BV antibacterial therapy alone. The patients were followed up for six months. Primary outcomes included HPV clearance rate and cervical cytological inflammation improvement, while secondary outcomes involved BV recurrence rate and adverse reaction incidence. The results showed that the observation group had a significantly higher HPV clearance rate (73.6% vs 53.9%) and inflammation improvement rate (90.8% vs 76.9%), as well as a lower BV recurrence rate (3.1% vs 13.9%) compared to the control group. Dingjunsheng was identified as an independent protective factor for HPV clearance (OR=2.5, 95%CI:1.3–5.1). The incidence of adverse reactions was similar between both groups. We conclude that Dingjunsheng adjuvant therapy demonstrated effectiveness in improving HPV clearance, alleviating inflammation, and reducing BV recurrence, with good safety, making it a valuable treatment strategy for these patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the efficacy and safety of Dingjunsheng, a live Lactobacillus preparation, as adjuvant therapy in patients with human papillomavirus (HPV) infection complicated by bacterial vaginosis (BV).
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was conducted on 152 eligible patients, randomly divided into two groups: the observation group (n=87) receiving standard BV antibacterial therapy plus Dingjunsheng, and the control group (n=65) who received standard BV antibacterial therapy alone. The patients were followed up for six months.
Core methods
Primary outcomes included HPV clearance rate and cervical cytological inflammation improvement, while secondary outcomes involved BV recurrence rate and adverse reaction incidence.

The authors concluded that Dingjunsheng adjuvant therapy demonstrated effectiveness in improving HPV clearance, alleviating inflammation, and reducing BV recurrence, with good safety, making it a valuable treatment strategy for these patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that Dingjunsheng as an adjuvant therapy could improve outcomes in patients with HPV infection complicated by BV, potentially reducing recurrence rates and enhancing viral clearance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a retrospective analysis, which may introduce selection bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the study design and sample size.

新加坡初级保健中自我取样 HPV DNA 检测对 宫颈癌 筛查的影响:务实随机对照试验。

Effect of Self-Sampling HPV DNA Testing on Cervical Cancer Screening in Singapore's Primary Care: Pragmatic Randomized Controlled Trial.
作者： Xin Rong Ng, Imm Pin Quek, Michelle Jessica Pereira, Joseph Antonio Molina, Khai Ee Tan, Joanne Ngeow, Sabrina Kay Wye Wong
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在新加坡,宫颈癌 筛查率仍然不理想,国家筛查计划中仅包括临床医生抽样。自采样 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) DNA 检测可能会克服基于临床医生的筛查的障碍并提高采用率。
设计与对象
这项采用 Zelen 设计的务实 2 组随机对照试验在新加坡 NHG 综合诊所和 NHG Health 进行。需要进行 宫颈癌 筛查的 30-69 岁女性被随机分配 (1:1) 进行干预(提供临床医生抽样选择,然后自行抽样)或常规护理(仅临床医生抽样)。
核心方法
主要结果是 高危型HPV DNA 检测,次要结果是筛选摄取。我们进行了亚组分析,通过先前的筛查历史检查这两种结果,并进行多变量分析,以确定与筛查选择相关的因素。
主要结果
From August 2024 to February 2025, 650 women were randomized, and 640 were included in the analysis (320 per arm). Significantly more high-risk HPV was detected in the intervention arm (3.1% vs 0.3%; absolute difference 2.8%; 95% CI, 0.8-4.8; P < .001), driven by greater screening uptake (56.6% vs 42.8%; absolute difference 13.8%; 95% CI, 6.0-21.4; P < .001), particularly among women with regular screening history and those with no prior screening.
解释与创新
提供自我采样 HPV DNA 检测和临床医生采样显着提高了筛查率和 高危型HPV 检出率。然而,受教育程度较低的女性的参与度仍然较低,这凸显了制定有针对性的参与战略的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,自我采样 HPV DNA 检测可以提高初级保健机构中 宫颈癌 筛查覆盖率和 高危型HPV 感染的早期发现。这种方法对于接受临床医生筛查的机会有限的女性特别有益。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在单一医疗保健系统中进行的,这可能会限制对其他环境的推广。尽管采用了随机设计,但由于试验的务实性质以及自我抽样选择中可能存在选择偏差,证据强度中等。

Effect of Self-Sampling HPV DNA Testing on Cervical Cancer Screening in Singapore's Primary Care: Pragmatic Randomized Controlled Trial.

Authors: Xin Rong Ng, Imm Pin Quek, Michelle Jessica Pereira, Joseph Antonio Molina, Khai Ee Tan, Joanne Ngeow, Sabrina Kay Wye Wong
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: In Singapore, cervical cancer screening rates remain suboptimal, with only clinician sampling included in the national screening program. Self-sampling human papillomavirus (HPV) DNA testing might overcome barriers to clinician-based screening and improve uptake. We assessed the effect of offering self-sampling alongside clinician sampling on screening uptake and high-risk HPV detection compared with clinician sampling alone. METHODS: This pragmatic, 2-arm randomized controlled trial with Zelen design was conducted across NHG Polyclinics, NHG Health in Singapore. Women aged 30-69 years due for cervical cancer screening were randomly assigned (1:1) to intervention (offered selection of clinician sampling followed by self-sampling) or usual care (clinician sampling alone). The primary outcome was high-risk HPV DNA detection, and the secondary outcome was screening uptake. We performed subgroup analysis to examine both outcomes by prior screening history. Multivariable analysis identified factors associated with screening choices. RESULTS: From August 2024 to February 2025, 650 women were randomized, and 640 were included in the analysis (320 per arm). Significantly more high-risk HPV was detected in the intervention arm (3.1% vs 0.3%; absolute difference 2.8%; 95% CI, 0.8-4.8;P < .001), driven by greater screening uptake (56.6% vs 42.8%; absolute difference 13.8%; 95% CI, 6.0-21.4; P < .001), particularly among women with regular screening history and those with no prior screening. Among screened participants in the intervention arm, 56.4% chose self-sampling. Lower educational attainment was associated with lower odds of choosing self-sampling over declining screening. CONCLUSIONS: Offering self-sampling HPV DNA testing alongside clinician sampling significantly increased screening uptake and high-risk HPV detection rates. However, uptake remained lower among women with less education, highlighting the need for targeted engagement strategies.Abstract also available in: عربي (Arabic); Deutsch (German); Español (Spanish); Francais (French); हिन्दी (Hindi); Indonesia (Indonesian); (Chinese); (Japanese); Portugues (Portuguese).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In Singapore, cervical cancer screening rates remain suboptimal, with only clinician sampling included in the national screening program. Self-sampling human papillomavirus (HPV) DNA testing might overcome barriers to clinician-based screening and improve uptake.
Design and population
This pragmatic, 2-arm randomized controlled trial with Zelen design was conducted across NHG Polyclinics, NHG Health in Singapore. Women aged 30-69 years due for cervical cancer screening were randomly assigned (1:1) to intervention (offered selection of clinician sampling followed by self-sampling) or usual care (clinician sampling alone).
Core methods
The primary outcome was high-risk HPV DNA detection, and the secondary outcome was screening uptake. We performed subgroup analysis to examine both outcomes by prior screening history and multivariable analysis to identify factors associated with screening choices.
Main results
From August 2024 to February 2025, 650 women were randomized, and 640 were included in the analysis (320 per arm). Significantly more high-risk HPV was detected in the intervention arm (3.1% vs 0.3%; absolute difference 2.8%; 95% CI, 0.8-4.8; P < .001), driven by greater screening uptake (56.6% vs 42.8%; absolute difference 13.8%; 95% CI, 6.0-21.4; P < .001), particularly among women with regular screening history and those with no prior screening.
Interpretation and novelty
Offering self-sampling HPV DNA testing alongside clinician sampling significantly increased screening uptake and high-risk HPV detection rates. However, uptake remained lower among women with less education, highlighting the need for targeted engagement strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that self-sampling HPV DNA testing can enhance cervical cancer screening coverage and early detection of high-risk HPV infections in primary care settings. This approach could be particularly beneficial for women with limited access to clinician-based screening.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a single healthcare system, which may limit generalizability to other settings. Despite the randomized design, the evidence strength is moderate due to the pragmatic nature of the trial and potential for selection bias in self-sampling choices.

lncRNA DINO 对 HPV 基因组 复制的分化独立激活。

Differentiation-independent activation of HPV genome replication by the lncRNA DINO.
作者： Warda Arman, Alison A. McBride, Karl Munger, Karl Münger
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 依赖多种 宿主 细胞因子来复制病毒 宿主,但 宿主 长非编码 RNA (lncRNA) 对病毒 宿主 在生产生命周期中维持和扩增的贡献仍知之甚少。在这项研究中,我们证明 lncRNA 损伤诱导的长非编码 RNA (DINO) 是 HPV DNA 复制的驱动因素。DINO水平在角质形成细胞分化过程中增加,DINO的异位表达促进HPV 基因组复制和复制灶的形成,并且这与角质形成细胞分化信号无关。异位 DINO 表达会增加选定的早期病毒转录水平,包括 E1^E4、E1 和 E2。值得注意的是,DINO 的亚细胞定位也与环境相关:在 DNA 损伤期间,DINO 主要位于细胞质中,但在角质形成细胞分化过程中,观察到核保留。这种差异定位表明 DINO 在角质形成细胞分化和 HPV 生物学中具有独特的功能作用。我们的研究结果强调了 DINO 作为一种促进 HPV 基因组 复制的 lncRNA,并表明 lncRNA 在 宿主 与病毒相互作用中可能发挥着未被充分认识的作用。这项工作为进一步探索lncRNA作为HPV相关疾病的潜在治疗靶点奠定了基础。重要性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是许多肛门生殖道和口腔癌的病原体,但在生产生命周期中触发和支持病毒 基因组 复制的 宿主 因素尚不完全清楚。这项研究将长非编码 RNA DINO 确定为 宿主 调节因子,可促进 HPV DNA 复制、复制焦点形成和独立于角质形成细胞分化的早期病毒基因表达。我们进一步表明 DINO 表现出上下文依赖性亚细胞定位,表明在细胞应激反应和 HPV 生物学中具有不同的功能作用。这些发现揭示了 宿主 lncRNA 在病毒-宿主 相互作用中未被充分认识的作用,并为支持 HPV 基因组 复制的细胞途径提供了新的见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 依赖多种 宿主 细胞因子来复制病毒 基因组,但 宿主 长非编码 RNA (lncRNA) 对病毒 基因组 在生产生命周期中维持和扩增的贡献仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
本研究利用角质形成细胞研究了 lncRNA DINO 在 HPV DNA 复制中的作用,检查其对病毒 基因组 复制、复制灶形成和早期病毒基因表达的影响。
核心方法
该研究测量了角质形成细胞分化过程中的DINO水平,进行了DINO的异位表达,并评估了其对HPV 基因组复制、复制灶形成和早期病毒转录水平(包括E1^E4、E1和E2)的影响。
主要结果
DINO 水平在角质形成细胞分化过程中增加,并且 DINO 的异位表达可独立于角质形成细胞分化信号促进 HPV 基因组 复制和复制灶形成,同时还增加选择的早期病毒转录物水平。
解释与创新
作者将 DINO 解释为 HPV DNA 复制的驱动因素,强调了其在 宿主 与病毒相互作用中未被充分认识的作用,并指出其上下文依赖的亚细胞定位表明在角质形成细胞分化和 HPV 生物学中具有不同的功能作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为探索lncRNA作为HPV相关疾病的潜在治疗靶点奠定了基础,并为支持HPV 基因组复制的细胞途径提供了新的见解。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究是基于角质形成细胞的体外实验,虽然研究结果很重要,但需要进一步的体内验证来确认靶向 DINO 的治疗潜力。

Differentiation-independent activation of HPV genome replication by the lncRNA DINO.

Authors: Warda Arman, Alison A. McBride, Karl Munger, Karl Münger
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Human papillomaviruses (HPVs) rely on multiple host cell factors to replicate the viral genome, yet the contribution of host long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs) to viral genome maintenance and amplification in the productive life cycle remains poorly understood. In this study, we show that the lncRNA damage-induced long non-coding RNA (DINO) is a driver of HPV DNA replication. DINO levels increase during keratinocyte differentiation, and ectopic expression of DINO promotes both HPV genome replication and the formation of replication foci, and this is independent of keratinocyte differentiation signals. Ectopic DINO expression increases select early viral transcript levels, including E1^E4, E1, and E2. Notably, DINO’s subcellular localization is also context-dependent: during DNA damage, DINO is predominantly cytoplasmic, but during keratinocyte differentiation, nuclear retention is observed. This differential localization suggests that DINO has distinct functional roles in keratinocyte differentiation and HPV biology. Our findings highlight DINO as a lncRNA that promotes HPV genome replication and suggest that lncRNAs may play underappreciated roles in host-virus interactions. This work provides a foundation for further exploration of lncRNAs as potential therapeutic targets in HPV-associated diseases. IMPORTANCE Human papillomaviruses (HPVs) are the causative agents of many anogenital tract and oral cancers, yet the host factors that trigger and support viral genome replication during the productive life cycle are incompletely understood. This study identifies the long non-coding RNA DINO as a host regulator that promotes HPV DNA replication, replication focus formation, and early viral gene expression independently of keratinocyte differentiation. We further show that DINO exhibits context-dependent subcellular localization, suggesting distinct functional roles in cellular stress responses and HPV biology. These findings reveal an underappreciated role for host lncRNAs in virus-host interactions and provide new insight into cellular pathways that support HPV genome replication.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomaviruses (HPVs) rely on multiple host cell factors to replicate the viral genome, yet the contribution of host long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs) to viral genome maintenance and amplification in the productive life cycle remains poorly understood.
Design and population
This study investigated the role of the lncRNA DINO in HPV DNA replication using keratinocytes, examining its effects on viral genome replication, replication foci formation, and early viral gene expression.
Core methods
The study measured DINO levels during keratinocyte differentiation, performed ectopic expression of DINO, and assessed its impact on HPV genome replication, replication foci formation, and early viral transcript levels, including E1^E4, E1, and E2.
Main results
DINO levels increase during keratinocyte differentiation, and ectopic expression of DINO promotes HPV genome replication and replication foci formation independently of keratinocyte differentiation signals, while also increasing select early viral transcript levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret DINO as a driver of HPV DNA replication, highlighting its underappreciated role in host-virus interactions, and note its context-dependent subcellular localization suggesting distinct functional roles in keratinocyte differentiation and HPV biology.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide a foundation for exploring lncRNAs as potential therapeutic targets in HPV-associated diseases, offering new insights into cellular pathways that support HPV genome replication.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study is based on in vitro experiments in keratinocytes, and while the findings are significant, further in vivo validation is needed to confirm the therapeutic potential of targeting DINO.

进化 根据 HPV 状态评估口咽癌术后复发和死亡的风险。

Evolution of hazard of recurrence and death after surgery for oropharyngeal cancer according to HPV status.
作者： Fabiola Giudici, Jerry Polesel, Marta Tagliabue, Elisabetta Fratta, Egidio Sia, Vittorio Rampinelli, Anna Menegaldo, Luigi Angelo Vaira, Pawel Golusinski, Didier Dequanter
摘要中文翻译背景:与 HPV 阴性疾病相比,人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 与生存率提高相关。然而,关于复发和癌症相关死亡的风险如何根据 HPV 状态变化的证据有限。方法:这项多中心回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了 2010 年至 2022 年间因 OPSCC 接受颈清扫术的患者。通过 p16 免疫组织化学测定 HPV 状态。使用Kaplan-Meier方法估计总生存期、癌症特异性生存期、无病生存期和无病特异性生存期。使用非参数平滑风险函数,按 p16 状态分层,估计五年内癌症相关死亡、局部复发、区域复发、远处转移和继发性原发肿瘤的时变风险率 (HR)。结果:650 名患者中,284 名(43.7%)患有 p16 阳性肿瘤。两组中癌症相关的 死亡率 在术后第二年达到峰值,但 p16 阴性患者比 p16 阳性患者出现更早且水平明显更高(15 个月与 18 个月;年 HR:12.7% vs 4.1%)。p16 阴性患者的局部和区域复发风险在头两年内最高,与 p16 阳性患者相比,p16 阴性患者的复发风险显着更高且持续时间更长。p16 阴性患者发生第二原发肿瘤的风险高于 p16 阳性患者,且在随访期间两组几乎保持不变。结论:OPSCC 手术后复发和癌症相关死亡的风险在大小和时间上根据 p16 状态而有所不同。危险函数分析提供了超出传统生存估计的临床相关信息,并可支持 OPSCC 中随时间调整的随访策略和患者咨询。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
与 HPV 阴性疾病相比,人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 与生存率提高相关。然而,关于复发和癌症相关死亡的风险如何根据 HPV 状态变化的证据有限。
设计与对象
这项多中心回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了 2010 年至 2022 年间因 OPSCC 接受颈清扫术的患者。
核心方法
HPV 状态通过 p16 免疫组织化学测定。使用Kaplan-Meier方法估计总生存期、癌症特异性生存期、无病生存期和无病特异性生存期。使用非参数平滑风险函数,按 p16 状态分层,估计五年内癌症相关死亡、局部复发、区域复发、远处转移和继发性原发肿瘤的时变风险率 (HR)。
主要结果
在 650 名患者中,284 名(43.7%)患有 p16 阳性肿瘤。两组中癌症相关的 死亡率 在术后第二年达到峰值,但 p16 阴性患者比 p16 阳性患者出现更早且水平明显更高(15 个月与 18 个月;年 HR:12.7% vs 4.1%)。p16 阴性患者的局部和区域复发风险在头两年内最高,与 p16 阳性患者相比,p16 阴性患者的复发风险显着更高且持续时间更长。p16 阴性患者发生第二原发肿瘤的风险高于 p16 阳性患者,且在随访期间两组几乎保持不变。
解释与创新
OPSCC 手术后复发和癌症相关死亡的风险在大小和时间上根据 p16 状态而有所不同。危险函数分析提供了超出传统生存估计的临床相关信息,并可支持 OPSCC 中随时间调整的随访策略和患者咨询。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可能支持 OPSCC 中适时的随访策略和患者咨询。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,可能会引入偏差并限制建立因果关系的能力。证据是温和的,因为它是基于观察数据。

Evolution of hazard of recurrence and death after surgery for oropharyngeal cancer according to HPV status.

Authors: Fabiola Giudici, Jerry Polesel, Marta Tagliabue, Elisabetta Fratta, Egidio Sia, Vittorio Rampinelli, Anna Menegaldo, Luigi Angelo Vaira, Pawel Golusinski, Didier Dequanter
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Human papillomavirus (HPV)-related oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) is associated with improved survival compared with HPV-negative disease. However, limited evidence is available on how the risk of recurrence and cancer-related death evolves according to HPV status. METHODS: This multicentre retrospective cohort study included patients who underwent upfront surgery with neck dissection for OPSCC between 2010 and 2022. HPV status was determined by p16 immunohistochemistry. Overall survival, cancer-specific survival, disease-free survival, and disease-free specific survival were estimated using Kaplan-Meier methods. Time-varying hazard rates (HR) for cancer-related death, local recurrence, regional recurrence, distant metastasis, and secondary primary tumor were estimated over a five-year period using nonparametric smoothed hazard functions, stratified by p16 status. RESULTS: Among 650 patients, 284 (43.7%) had p16-positive tumours. Cancer-related mortality peaked during the second postoperative year in both groups, but occurring earlier and at markedly higher levels in p16-negative than in p16-positive patients (15 vs 18 months; annual HR: 12.7% vs 4.1%). Local and regional recurrence risks were the highest within the first two years, remained significantly higher and more prolonged in p16-negative patients than in p16-positive counterpart. The risk of second primary tumors was higher for p16-negative patients than p16-positive ones, remaining almost constant during follow-up in both groups. CONCLUSIONS: The risk of recurrence and cancer-related death after surgery for OPSCC differs in magnitude and in timing according to p16 status. Hazard function analysis provides clinically relevant information beyond conventional survival estimates and may support time-adapted follow-up strategies and patient counselling in OPSCC.
View research seven-element analysis

影响杭州市男大学生HPV 疫苗接种意向的心理社会因素——基于计划行为模型扩展理论的横断面研究

Psychosocial factors influencing HPV vaccination intention among male college students in Hangzhou city: A cross-sectional study based on an extended theory of planned behavior model.
作者： Zhengkai Gan, Genqiang Mo, Peng Fei Yu, Zhewei Feng, Xiejun Gu, Hui Liang, 顾谢君
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 男性 人乳头瘤病毒 对于减少 HPV 相关疾病的全球负担至关重要。本研究利用扩展的计划行为理论 (TPB) 框架来确定中国男本科生 疫苗接种 意图的决定因素。2025 年 3 月至 5 月在中国杭州进行了一项横断面研究,通过多阶段分层整群抽样招募了 1,310 名参与者。因变量是 疫苗接种 意图。自变量包括社会人口统计学、TPB 结构(行为态度 [BA]、主观规范 [SN] 和感知行为控制 [PBC])、HPV 知识和风险感知。我们采用三步分层线性模型(模型 1:社会人口统计学;模型 2:添加 TPB 结构;模型 3:添加 HPV 知识/风险)和结构方程模型 (SEM) 来测试潜在路径。在 1,310 名参与者中(平衡四个学年),62.7% 表示愿意接受 HPV 疫苗。分层回归显示,社会人口统计学解释了 疫苗接种 意图(模型 1)中 2.1% 的方差,随着包含 TPB 结构(模型 2),该方差增加到 25.1%,并在最终模型(模型 3)中达到 32.7%(F = 126.245,p < .001)。SEM分析进一步验证了模型(χ2/df = 2.102,RMSEA = 0.068,GFI = 0.968),占行为意图总方差的75.9%。PBC 被认为是最有力的预测因子(β = 0.527,p < .001),其次是 BA、SN 和 HPV 相关知识。此外,较高的自我评估感染风险显着预示着更强烈的意图。这项研究的结果表明,提高 疫苗接种 率需要政策干预来提高 疫苗 的可及性和负担能力,同时通过有针对性的健康教育培养积极态度和社会支持。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究通过使用扩展的计划行为理论 (TPB) 框架确定 疫苗接种 意图的心理社会决定因素,解决了增加男性 HPV 疫苗接种 摄取的公共卫生需求。
设计与对象
2025 年 3 月至 5 月在中国杭州进行了一项横断面研究,通过多阶段分层整群抽样招募了 1,310 名男本科生。
核心方法
使用分层线性模型(三步)和结构方程模型(SEM)来量化社会人口统计学、TPB 结构(行为态度、主观规范、感知行为控制)、HPV 知识和风险感知对 疫苗接种 意图的贡献。
主要结果
在最终模型中,TPB 结构和知识解释了 疫苗接种 意图的 32.7% 方差 (F = 126.245,p < .001),感知行为控制是最强的预测因子 (β = 0.527,p < .001),其次是行为态度、主观规范和 HPV 相关知识。
解释与创新
作者对研究结果的解释是,感知行为控制和态度是增加男性 HPV 疫苗接种 意图的关键杠杆,强调了扩展 TPB 模型在该人群中的价值。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,政策干预措施应优先考虑提高 疫苗 的可及性和可负担性,同时通过有针对性的健康教育加强积极态度和社会支持,以提高男性 HPV 疫苗接种 率。
局限与证据强度
横截面设计限制了因果推断,并且对自我报告意图的依赖可能会高估未来的采用率;由于这些限制,证据被评估为中等。

Psychosocial factors influencing HPV vaccination intention among male college students in Hangzhou city: A cross-sectional study based on an extended theory of planned behavior model.

Authors: Zhengkai Gan, Genqiang Mo, Peng Fei Yu, Zhewei Feng, Xiejun Gu, Hui Liang, 顾谢君
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination for males is critical for reducing the global burden of HPV-related diseases. This study utilized an extended Theory of Planned Behavior (TPB) framework to identify determinants of vaccination intentions among Chinese male undergraduate students. A cross-sectional study was conducted between March and May 2025 in Hangzhou, China, recruiting 1,310 participants via multi-stage stratified cluster sampling. The dependent variable was vaccination intention. Independent variables included sociodemographics, TPB constructs (behavior attitude [BA], subjective norms [SN], and perceived behavioral control [PBC]), HPV knowledge and risk perception. We employed a three-step hierarchical linear model (Model 1: sociodemographics; Model 2: adding TPB constructs; Model 3: adding HPV knowledge/risk) and structural equation modeling (SEM) to test latent paths. Among the 1,310 participants (balanced across four academic years), 62.7% expressed a willingness to receive the HPV vaccine. Hierarchical regression revealed that sociodemographics explained 2.1% of the variance in vaccination intention (Model 1), which increased to 25.1% with the inclusion of TPB constructs (Model 2), and reached 32.7% in the final model (Model 3) (F = 126.245, p < .001). SEM analysis further validated the model (χ2/df = 2.102, RMSEA = 0.068, GFI = 0.968), accounting for 75.9% of the total variance in behavioral intention. PBC was identified as the most powerful predictor (β = 0.527, p < .001), followed by BA, SN and HPV-related knowledge. Additionally, higher self-assessed risk of infection significantly predicted stronger intentions. The results of this study indicated that increasing vaccination rates requires policy interventions to improve vaccine accessibility and affordability, alongside fostering positive attitudes and social support through targeted health education.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health need to increase HPV vaccination uptake among males by identifying psychosocial determinants of vaccination intention using an extended Theory of Planned Behavior (TPB) framework.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted between March and May 2025 in Hangzhou, China, enrolling 1,310 male undergraduate students via multi-stage stratified cluster sampling.
Core methods
Hierarchical linear models (three steps) and structural equation modeling (SEM) were used to quantify the contributions of sociodemographics, TPB constructs (behavior attitude, subjective norms, perceived behavioral control), HPV knowledge, and risk perception to vaccination intention.
Main results
In the final model, TPB constructs and knowledge explained 32.7% of variance in vaccination intention (F = 126.245, p < .001), with perceived behavioral control the strongest predictor (β = 0.527, p < .001), followed by behavior attitude, subjective norms, and HPV-related knowledge.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as showing that perceived behavioral control and attitudes are key levers for increasing male HPV vaccination intention, highlighting the value of an extended TPB model in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that policy interventions should prioritize improving vaccine accessibility and affordability while strengthening positive attitudes and social support through targeted health education to raise male HPV vaccination rates.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and reliance on self-reported intention may overestimate future uptake; the evidence is assessed as moderate due to these limitations.

HPV E7-ZER1 轴的分子基础揭示了 HPV 阳性癌症的配体脆弱性。

Molecular basis of the HPV E7-ZER1 axis reveals a ligandable vulnerability in HPV-positive cancers.
作者： Xiaolu Wang, Chenhao Jiang, Yueling Zhao, Ruiyan Li, Yangyang Zhou, Shiqi Yin, Jin Chen, Shuxin Yan, Shuhua Gu, Xinying Li
摘要中文翻译高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)(特别是 HPV-16 和 HPV-18)的持续感染是大多数 宫颈癌 和许多头颈癌的基础。在此,我们报告了病毒癌蛋白 E7 劫持 宿主 CRL2 ZER1 以降解视网膜母细胞瘤 (Rb) 蛋白并驱动 E2F 依赖性增殖的结构和功能基础。晶体结构表明,E7 的 N 端 MH/N-degron 与 ZER1 的犰狳 (ARM) 重复结构域内的一个确定的口袋接合。破坏该界面会消除 E7-ZER1 结合,防止 Rb 降解,沉默 E2F 转录程序,并损害 HPV 阳性癌细胞的增殖。在这些见解的指导下,我们确定了一种一流的小分子抑制剂,可以阻断 E7-ZER1 关联、恢复 Rb 稳定性并选择性抑制体内 HPV 阳性肿瘤的生长。这些结果定义了病毒拟态机制,并指定 ZER1 降解决定子口袋作为 HPV 驱动的恶性肿瘤的可配体治疗靶点,突出了靶向治疗的转化机会。

Molecular basis of the HPV E7-ZER1 axis reveals a ligandable vulnerability in HPV-positive cancers.

Authors: Xiaolu Wang, Chenhao Jiang, Yueling Zhao, Ruiyan Li, Yangyang Zhou, Shiqi Yin, Jin Chen, Shuxin Yan, Shuhua Gu, Xinying Li
Original abstract (English)
Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomaviruses (HPVs), notably HPV-16 and HPV-18, underlies most cervical cancers and many head and neck cancers. Here, we report the structural and functional basis by which the viral oncoprotein E7 hijacks host CRL2 ZER1 to degrade retinoblastoma (Rb) protein and drive E2F-dependent proliferation. Crystal structures reveal that the N-terminal MH/N-degron of E7 engages a defined pocket within the armadillo (ARM)-repeat domain of ZER1. Disruption of this interface abolishes E7-ZER1 binding, prevents Rb degradation, silences E2F transcriptional programs, and impairs proliferation of HPV-positive cancer cells. Guided by these insights, we identify a first-in-class small-molecule inhibitor that blocks E7-ZER1 association, restores Rb stability, and selectively suppresses HPV-positive tumor growth in vivo . These results define a viral mimicry mechanism and nominate the ZER1 degron pocket as a ligandable therapeutic target for HPV-driven malignancies, highlighting translational opportunities for targeted therapy.

Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
Disruption of this interface abolishes E7-ZER1 binding, prevents Rb degradation, silences E2F transcriptional programs, and impairs proliferation of HPV-positive cancer cells.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

头颈鳞状细胞癌中持续性 HPV 特异性活化 T 细胞的特征。

Characterization of persistent HPV-specific activated T cells in head and neck squamous cell carcinoma.
作者： Hiromasa Ishihara, Hirofumi Shibata, Daisuke Muraoka, Ayako Demachi-Okamura, Takanari Okamoto, Ryo Mizuta, Yasunori Fukushima, Yusuke Sugita, Aiko Ogasawara, Reina Nishida
摘要中文翻译背景 人乳头瘤病毒 阳性口咽鳞状细胞癌(HPV + OPSCC)通常具有良好的结果,但仍然容易出现晚期复发。持久的控制可能取决于持续存在的肿瘤特异性 CD8+ T 细胞,但它们的持续存在和在转移中的功能尚不清楚。方法 我们将批量 RNA 测序 (n = 56) 与肿瘤浸润 CD8+ T 细胞 (n = 4) 的单细胞 RNA 和配对 T 细胞受体 (TCR) 测序相结合,包括在治疗三年后获得的纵向原发性肺转移对。使用 HPV 基因分型、HLA 分型、表位预测和 NFAT 报告 Jurkat 荧光素酶测定来识别 HPV16 E6/E7 特异性 TCR 并进行功能验证。评估了库追踪和单细胞/批量转录组学。结果 HPV + 肿瘤比 HPV- 肿瘤表现出更高的推断 CD8+ T 细胞浸润和更好的预后。单细胞分析显示,寡克隆扩增的耗尽簇富含 HPV 肿瘤特异性 CD8+ T 细胞。我们分离并功能验证了九种源自患者的 HPV16 E6/E7 特异性 TCR。识别 E6 中选择性剪接区域(aa 49-110;E6*)的高亲和力受体在转移性病变中持续存在,而低亲和力克隆,包括目前正在临床研究的 E7_11-19 特异性 TCR,则丢失了。与原发性肿瘤相比,转移性病灶表现出干细胞样/TCF7相关CD8+ T细胞群减少,以及表达KLRB1和ZNF683的HPV特异性CD8+ T细胞富集,这与具有抑制信号传导特征的组织适应TRM样转录状态一致。结论 高亲合力 KLRB1 + HPV 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞代表了与 HPV + OPSCC 具有预后和治疗相关性的持续效应子亚群。它们在转移性病变中的持续存在以及与复发风险降低的相关性支持对 KLRB1 相关 HPV 反应性 T 细胞状态作为生物标志物和免疫治疗靶点的进一步研究。

问题与背景

方法 我们将批量 RNA 测序 (n = 56) 与肿瘤浸润 CD8+ T 细胞 (n = 4) 的单细胞 RNA 和配对 T 细胞受体 (TCR) 测序相结合,包括在治疗三年后获得的纵向原发性肺转移对。

Characterization of persistent HPV-specific activated T cells in head and neck squamous cell carcinoma.

Authors: Hiromasa Ishihara, Hirofumi Shibata, Daisuke Muraoka, Ayako Demachi-Okamura, Takanari Okamoto, Ryo Mizuta, Yasunori Fukushima, Yusuke Sugita, Aiko Ogasawara, Reina Nishida
Original abstract (English)
Background Human papillomavirus-positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (HPV + OPSCC) generally has favorable outcomes yet remains prone to late recurrence. Durable control likely depends on persistent tumor-specific CD8+ T cells, but their persistence and function in metastasis are poorly understood. Methods We integrated bulk RNA sequencing ( n = 56) with single-cell RNA and paired T-cell receptor (TCR) sequencing of tumor-infiltrating CD8+ T cells ( n = 4), including a longitudinal primary-lung metastasis pair obtained three years after curative therapy. HPV genotyping, HLA typing, epitope prediction, and NFAT-reporter Jurkat-luciferase assays were used to identify and functionally validate HPV16 E6/E7-specific TCRs. Repertoire tracking and single-cell/bulk transcriptomics were evaluated. Results HPV + tumors showed higher inferred CD8+ T cell infiltration and more favorable prognosis than HPV- tumors. Single-cell analysis revealed oligoclonally expanded exhausted clusters enriched in HPV tumor-specific CD8+ T cells. We isolated and functionally validated nine patient-derived HPV16 E6/E7-specific TCRs. High-avidity receptors recognizing an alternatively spliced region in E6 (aa 49-110; E6*) persisted in metastatic lesions, whereas lower-avidity clones, including an E7_11-19-specific TCR currently under clinical investigation, were lost. Compared with the primary tumor, metastatic lesions showed reduced stem-like/TCF7-associated CD8+ T-cell populations and enrichment of HPV-specific CD8+ T cells expressing KLRB1 and ZNF683 , consistent with a tissue-adapted TRM-like transcriptional state with inhibitory signaling features. Conclusions High-avidity, KLRB1 + HPV-specific CD8+ T cells represent a persistent effector subset with prognostic and therapeutic relevance to HPV + OPSCC. Their persistence in metastatic lesions and association with reduced recurrence risk support further investigation of KLRB1 -associated HPV-reactive T-cell states as biomarkers and immunotherapeutic targets.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Background Human papillomavirus-positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (HPV + OPSCC) generally has favorable outcomes yet remains prone to late recurrence. Durable control likely depends on persistent tumor-specific CD8+ T cells, but their persistence and function in metastasis are poorly understood.
Design and population
Methods We integrated bulk RNA sequencing (n = 56) with single-cell RNA and paired T-cell receptor (TCR) sequencing of tumor-infiltrating CD8+ T cells (n = 4), including a longitudinal primary-lung metastasis pair obtained three years after curative therapy.
Core methods
HPV genotyping, HLA typing, epitope prediction, and NFAT-reporter Jurkat-luciferase assays were used to identify and functionally validate HPV16 E6/E7-specific TCRs. Repertoire tracking and single-cell/bulk transcriptomics were evaluated.
Main results
Results HPV + tumors showed higher inferred CD8+ T cell infiltration and more favorable prognosis than HPV- tumors. Single-cell analysis revealed oligoclonally expanded exhausted clusters enriched in HPV tumor-specific CD8+ T cells. Compared with the primary tumor, metastatic lesions showed reduced stem-like/TCF7-associated CD8+ T-cell populations and enrichment of HPV-specific CD8+ T cells expressing KLRB1 and ZNF683.
Interpretation and novelty
Conclusions High-avidity, KLRB1 + HPV-specific CD8+ T cells represent a persistent effector subset with prognostic and therapeutic relevance to HPV + OPSCC. Their persistence in metastatic lesions and association with reduced recurrence risk support further investigation of KLRB1 -associated HPV-reactive T-cell states as biomarkers and immunotherapeutic targets.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that high-avidity HPV-specific T cells may serve as biomarkers for recurrence risk and targets for immunotherapy in HPV + OPSCC, potentially improving patient outcomes through targeted interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size (n = 4 for single-cell sequencing) and reliance on inferred data for some analyses, though the longitudinal design and functional validation strengthen the findings.

克服免疫接种差距:识别中国自身免疫性炎症性风湿病 (AIIRD) 患者的 HPV 疫苗接种 的可改变屏障

Overcoming immunization Gaps: Identifying Modifiable Barriers to HPV Vaccination in Chinese Patients with Autoimmune Inflammatory Rheumatic Diseases (AIIRDs)
作者： Juan Zhao, Lan Mi, Hui Bi, Dai Zhang, Zhuoli Zhang
摘要中文翻译摘要 [背景与目的]女性自身免疫性炎症性风湿病(AIIRD)更容易感染人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)和HPV相关的宫颈癌,但HPV筛查率和疫苗接种率较低。我们进行了一项真实世界调查,以评估中国 AIIRD 患者对 HPV 疫苗接种 的认知和状况。[方法] 2024年4月1日至2024年8月31日期间纳入年龄≥9岁的AIIRD女性患者。符合条件的参与者填写了一份预定义的41项调查问卷,该调查问卷通过社交媒体邀请和门诊诊所亲自招募的方式分发。我们评估了人口统计学、风湿病史、对 HPV 感染和 疫苗接种 的认识、HPV 检测和 Z​​XQTERMLQXZ 状态以及接受 HPV 疫苗 的意愿,然后对 SLE 和 RA 患者之间的这些因素进行了比较。[结果]共有562名参与者完成了问卷,中位年龄为34.9岁(IQR 29.7-42.6)。HPV感染和疫苗接种的知晓率分别为82.0%和85.9%。然而,只有 69.2% 的参与者认识到与 HPV 相关的 宫颈癌 风险增加,并且只有 52.3% 的参与者在过去 5 年中接受过 宫颈癌 筛查。虽然72.2%的人表示愿意接受HPV 疫苗,但实际疫苗接种的比率仅为23.0%。在受访者中,有 334 名 SLE 患者和 98 名 RA 患者。与RA患者相比,SLE患者年龄较小,对HPV 疫苗接种的知晓率较高,但疫苗接种率较低。[结论] AIIRDs、特别是SLE患者对HPV感染和疫苗接种的认知度较高,且接受HPV 疫苗的意愿强烈。然而,宫颈癌 筛查和实际 HPV 疫苗接种 率仍然很低。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
患有自身免疫性炎症性风湿病(AIIRD)的女性更容易感染人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)和HPV相关的宫颈癌,然而,HPV筛查和疫苗接种率较低。
设计与对象
我们进行了一项真实世界调查,以评估中国 AIIRD 患者对 HPV 疫苗接种 的认知和状况。年龄≥9岁的AIIRD女性患者于2024年4月1日至2024年8月31日期间入组。
核心方法
符合条件的参与者完成了一份包含 41 项的预定义调查问卷,该调查问卷通过社交媒体邀请和门诊诊所现场招募的方式分发。我们评估了人口统计数据、风湿病史、对 HPV 感染和 疫苗接种 的认识、HPV 检测和 Z​​XQTERMLQXZ 状态以及接受 HPV 疫苗 的意愿。
主要结果
共有 562 名中位年龄为 34.9(IQR 29.7-42.6)岁的参与者完成了调查问卷。HPV感染和疫苗接种的知晓率分别为82.0%和85.9%。然而,只有 69.2% 的参与者认识到与 HPV 相关的 宫颈癌 风险增加,并且只有 52.3% 的参与者在过去 5 年中接受过 宫颈癌 筛查。虽然72.2%的人表示愿意接受HPV 疫苗,但实际疫苗接种的比率仅为23.0%。在受访者中,有 334 名 SLE 患者和 98 名 RA 患者。与RA患者相比,SLE患者年龄较小,对HPV 疫苗接种的知晓率较高,但疫苗接种率较低。
解释与创新
AIIRDs(尤其是 SLE)患者表现出对 HPV 感染和 疫苗接种 的高度认知,以及接受 HPV 疫苗 的强烈意愿。然而,宫颈癌 筛查和实际 HPV 疫苗接种 率仍然很低。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要解决中国 AIIRD 患者(特别是 SLE 患者)中 HPV 疫苗接种 的可改变障碍,以提高 疫苗接种 的覆盖率并降低 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横断面设计和对自我报告数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入回忆偏差。由于样本量较大,但缺乏随机性和潜在的选择偏差,证据强度中等。

Overcoming immunization Gaps: Identifying Modifiable Barriers to HPV Vaccination in Chinese Patients with Autoimmune Inflammatory Rheumatic Diseases (AIIRDs)

Authors: Juan Zhao, Lan Mi, Hui Bi, Dai Zhang, Zhuoli Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract [Background and Objectives] Women with autoimmune inflammatory rheumatic diseases (AIIRDs) are more vulnerable to human papillomavirus (HPV) infection and HPV-related cervical cancers, however, the HPV screening and vaccination rate was low. We conducted a real-world survey to evaluate the awareness and status of HPV vaccination in Chinese AIIRDs patients. [Methods] Female patients with AIIRDs aged ≥ 9 years were enrolled between 1 April 2024 and 31 Aug 2024. Eligible participants completed a predefined 41-item questionnaire, which was distributed via social media invitations and in-person recruitment at outpatient clinics. We evaluated the demographics, rheumatic disease history, awareness of HPV infection and vaccination, HPV testing and vaccination status and willingness to receive HPV vaccine These factors were then compared between SLE and RA patients. [Results] A total of 562 participants with a median age of 34.9 (IQR 29.7–42.6) years completed the questionnaires. The awareness rates of HPV infection and vaccination were 82.0% and 85.9%, respectively. However, only 69.2% participants recognized the increased risk of cervical cancer associated with HPV, and just 52.3% had undergone cervical cancer screening in the past 5 years. Although 72.2% expressed willingness to receive the HPV vaccine, the actual vaccination rate was only 23.0%. Among the respondents, there were 334 SLE patients and 98 RA patients. Compared to RA patients, SLE patients were younger, had higher awareness of HPV vaccination, but a lower vaccination rate. [Conclusion] Patients with AIIRDs, SLE in particular, demonstrate high awareness of HPV infection and vaccination, as well as strong willingness to receive HPV vaccine. However, cervical cancer screening and actual HPV vaccination rates remain low.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Women with autoimmune inflammatory rheumatic diseases (AIIRDs) are more vulnerable to human papillomavirus (HPV) infection and HPV-related cervical cancers, however, the HPV screening and vaccination rate was low.
Design and population
We conducted a real-world survey to evaluate the awareness and status of HPV vaccination in Chinese AIIRDs patients. Female patients with AIIRDs aged ≥ 9 years were enrolled between 1 April 2024 and 31 Aug 2024.
Core methods
Eligible participants completed a predefined 41-item questionnaire, which was distributed via social media invitations and in-person recruitment at outpatient clinics. We evaluated the demographics, rheumatic disease history, awareness of HPV infection and vaccination, HPV testing and vaccination status and willingness to receive HPV vaccine.
Main results
A total of 562 participants with a median age of 34.9 (IQR 29.7–42.6) years completed the questionnaires. The awareness rates of HPV infection and vaccination were 82.0% and 85.9%, respectively. However, only 69.2% participants recognized the increased risk of cervical cancer associated with HPV, and just 52.3% had undergone cervical cancer screening in the past 5 years. Although 72.2% expressed willingness to receive the HPV vaccine, the actual vaccination rate was only 23.0%. Among the respondents, there were 334 SLE patients and 98 RA patients. Compared to RA patients, SLE patients were younger, had higher awareness of HPV vaccination, but a lower vaccination rate.
Interpretation and novelty
Patients with AIIRDs, SLE in particular, demonstrate high awareness of HPV infection and vaccination, as well as strong willingness to receive HPV vaccine. However, cervical cancer screening and actual HPV vaccination rates remain low.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need to address modifiable barriers to HPV vaccination in Chinese AIIRDs patients, particularly SLE patients, to improve vaccination coverage and reduce the risk of HPV-related cervical cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data, which may introduce recall bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the large sample size but the lack of randomization and potential selection bias.

喷射念珠菌抗原治疗疣 治疗:另一种药物输送。

Jet Injections With Candida Antigen for Wart Treatment: An Alternative Drug Delivery.
作者： Mary Nan Leath, Theodore Katz, Kimberly A. Eisenstein
摘要中文翻译疣是由 人乳头瘤病毒 引起的常见儿科疾病。免疫疗法是一种经过充分研究的治疗疣的 治疗,通常通过使用注射器和针头进行病灶内注射来进行。我们报道了一名 12 岁男孩的疣病例,在使用无针喷射注射装置进行两次无痛、无针、病灶内念珠菌抗原注射后,该男孩的疣完全消退。这是病灶内免疫治疗的一种痛苦较小的替代方案,特别适用于儿科或针头恐惧症患者。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
疣是由 人乳头瘤病毒 引起的常见儿科疾病,免疫疗法是针对疣的经过充分研究的 治疗。
设计与对象
我们报告了一名 12 岁男孩的疣病例,在使用无针喷射注射装置进行两次病灶内念珠菌抗原注射后,该男孩的疣完全消退。
核心方法
治疗 涉及使用无针喷射注射装置施用无痛、无针病灶内念珠菌抗原。
主要结果
经过两次 治疗 疗程后,患者的疣完全消失。
解释与创新
这种方法是病灶内免疫治疗的一种痛苦较小的替代方案,特别适用于儿科或针头恐惧症患者。
科研与公卫意义
该方法为疣 治疗 提供了更舒适的选择,有可能提高儿科人群的依从性。
局限与证据强度
证据基于单个 病例报告,限制了普遍性和强度。

Jet Injections With Candida Antigen for Wart Treatment: An Alternative Drug Delivery.

Authors: Mary Nan Leath, Theodore Katz, Kimberly A. Eisenstein
Original abstract (English)
Warts are a common pediatric condition caused by human papillomavirus. Immunotherapy is a well-studied treatment for warts and is classically administered via intralesional injection with a syringe and needle. We report a case of warts in a 12-year-old boy who achieved complete resolution following two sessions of pain-free, and needleless, intralesional Candida antigen administered using a needle free jet injection device. This is a less painful alternative for administration of intralesional immunotherapy, particularly useful in pediatric or needle phobic patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Warts are a common pediatric condition caused by human papillomavirus, and immunotherapy is a well-studied treatment for warts.
Design and population
We report a case of warts in a 12-year-old boy who achieved complete resolution following two sessions of intralesional Candida antigen administered using a needle-free jet injection device.
Core methods
The treatment involved pain-free, needleless intralesional Candida antigen administered using a needle-free jet injection device.
Main results
The patient achieved complete resolution of warts following two sessions of treatment.
Interpretation and novelty
This approach represents a less painful alternative for administration of intralesional immunotherapy, particularly useful in pediatric or needle-phobic patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
This method offers a more comfortable option for wart treatment, potentially improving adherence in pediatric populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on a single case report, limiting generalizability and strength.

提高学术儿童医院青少年的常规 疫苗接种 率。

Improving Rates of Routine Vaccinations in Adolescents at an Academic Children's Hospital.
作者： Maria G Biancarelli, Hannah Rauchle, Kathleen Waddicor, Benjamin Ethier, Sara F Forman, Gabriela Vargas, Joshua Borus
摘要中文翻译简介:该项目旨在将青少年医学实践中的 疫苗接种 完成率提高到 95%。我们的目标是将了解最新脑膜炎球菌、人乳头瘤病毒 以及破伤风、白喉和百日咳 疫苗接种 信息的个人比例提高到 80%,并验证这些努力是否带来了更公平的护理。方法:我们使用改进模型。经过现状分析后,该团队实施了捆绑干预措施,包括获取丢失的记录、通知提供者需要 疫苗接种 的患者,以及在 疫苗接种 到期时提供电子病历警报。患者接受了 疫苗 教育,护士提供了麻醉喷雾剂,以减轻与针头相关的 疫苗 犹豫。结果:统计过程控制图显示完整 疫苗接种 记录的百分比从 88% 转变为 95%。脑膜炎球菌、人乳头瘤病毒 以及破伤风、白喉和百日咳联合治疗完成百分比的平均变化从 70% 升至 76%。所有种族和付款人亚组都通过干预措施得到改善。干预前后完成百分比的种族差异分析显示,参考组、西班牙裔患者和其他种族群体之间观察到 2 个差异。在干预前后,公共和私人保险患者之间没有观察到差异。结论:捆绑干预与较高的 疫苗 记录和管理率相关。高度敬业的护理人员和创新技术推动了成功。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管有公共卫生建议,青少年脑膜炎球菌、人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 以及破伤风、白喉和百日咳的 疫苗接种 发病率仍然不理想,因此需要有针对性的干预措施来提高覆盖率和公平性。
设计与对象
这项质量改进研究是在青少年医学实践中进行的,在进行现状分析后使用捆绑干预方法来确定 疫苗接种 文档和管理中的差距。
核心方法
该团队应用了改进模型框架并实施了多组件干预,包括获取缺失的 疫苗接种 记录、提供者通知、针对 疫苗接种 到期的电子病历警报、患者教育以及护士施用的麻醉喷雾以解决与针头相关的犹豫。
主要结果
统计过程控制图显示,完整的 疫苗接种 记录从 88% 增加到 95%,脑膜炎球菌、HPV 和 Tdap 综合完成率的平均变化从 70% 到 76%。
解释与创新
捆绑干预与更高的 疫苗 记录和管理率相关,并且在所有种族和付款人亚组中观察到改善,这表明系统级别的变化可以提高 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖率并减少差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持实施系统级干预措施,例如提供者警报、患者教育和程序调整,以提高 HPV 疫苗接种 率并促进青少年护理中的健康公平。
局限与证据强度
该研究对单中心设计的依赖和缺乏随机性限制了因果推断,尽管在不同亚组中观察到的改善加强了实践水平干预的证据基础。

Improving Rates of Routine Vaccinations in Adolescents at an Academic Children's Hospital.

Authors: Maria G Biancarelli, Hannah Rauchle, Kathleen Waddicor, Benjamin Ethier, Sara F Forman, Gabriela Vargas, Joshua Borus
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: This project sought to increase the vaccination completion rate to 95% in an adolescent medicine practice. We aimed to increase the percentage of individuals who were up to date on their meningococcal, human papillomavirus, and tetanus, diphtheria, and pertussis vaccinations to 80% and to verify that these efforts led to more equitable care. METHODS: We used the Model for Improvement. After a current-state analysis, the team implemented a bundled intervention that included obtaining missing records, notifying providers about patients who needed vaccinations, and providing electronic medical record alerts when vaccinations were due. Patients received vaccine education, and nurses offered a numbing spray to mitigate needle-related vaccine hesitancy. RESULTS: Statistical process control charts showed a shift in the percentage of complete vaccination records from 88% to 95%. There was a mean shift for the combined meningococcal, human papillomavirus, and tetanus, diphtheria, and pertussis completion percentage from 70% to 76%. All race and payer subgroups improved with the interventions. Racial disparity analysis for the completion percentage pre- and postintervention showed that there were 2 disparities observed between the reference group, Hispanic patients, and other racial groups. No disparities were observed between publicly and privately insured patients before or after the interventions. CONCLUSIONS: The bundled intervention was associated with higher rates of vaccine documentation and administration. The highly engaged nursing staff and innovative technology drove success.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Adolescent vaccination rates for meningococcal, human papillomavirus (HPV), and tetanus, diphtheria, and pertussis remain suboptimal despite public health recommendations, creating a need for targeted interventions to improve coverage and equity.
Design and population
This quality-improvement study was conducted in an adolescent medicine practice using a bundled intervention approach after a current-state analysis to identify gaps in vaccination documentation and administration.
Core methods
The team applied the Model for Improvement framework and implemented a multi-component intervention including obtaining missing vaccination records, provider notifications, electronic medical record alerts for due vaccinations, patient education, and nurse-administered numbing spray to address needle-related hesitancy.
Main results
Statistical process control charts demonstrated an increase in complete vaccination records from 88% to 95% and a mean shift in combined meningococcal, HPV, and Tdap completion from 70% to 76%.
Interpretation and novelty
The bundled intervention was associated with higher rates of vaccine documentation and administration, with improvements observed across all race and payer subgroups, suggesting that system-level changes can enhance HPV vaccination coverage and reduce disparities.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the implementation of system-level interventions such as provider alerts, patient education, and procedural accommodations to improve HPV vaccination rates and advance health equity in adolescent care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s reliance on a single-center design and lack of randomization limits causal inference, though the observed improvements across diverse subgroups strengthen the evidence base for practice-level interventions.

Journal: Biosensors & bioelectronics  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-27  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.bios.2026.119074  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 312, 119074
