由单链 RNA 触发的 DNA 纳米结构的可编程 2D 到 3D 结构转变。

Programmable 2D-to-3D structural transformation of DNA nanostructures triggered by single-stranded RNA.
作者： Xiaoqiu Zheng, Tingyu Zhu, Bingbing Lyu, Liyou Guo, Yilin Wang, Hong Zhang, Dong-Yang Zhang, Hongfei He, Jiazhen Lyu
摘要中文翻译DNA 纳米结构的可控结构转变为分子器件提供了巨大的潜力。立足点介导的链置换是驱动此类重排的常见策略,通常需要专用的立足点序列来启动反应,这使得在完美匹配的 DNA 系统中调节构象变化具有挑战性。为了规避这一限制,我们仅使用两条短 DNA 链有效地组装了二维 (2D) DNA 三角形。受RNA结构多样性的启发,我们发现相应RNA序列诱导的链位移可以改变没有立足点的三角形的组装路径,从而形成三维(3D)RNA-DNA混合四边形。所有纳米结构及其转化途径均通过低温电子显微镜(cryo-EM)和质谱(MS)分析得到证实。此外,为了研究我们设计的可转化DNA纳米结构是否可以作为目标特异性信号报告系统,我们开发了一种基于等温扩增结合单链RNA置换诱导的DNA纳米结构转化(SDT)的核酸检测策略,成功检测了临床样本中的人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)。我们的实验结果表明,RNA 链置换为构建可转化 DNA 纳米结构提供了新的见解,并为分子诊断中的动态 DNA 纳米结构提供了一种新颖的设计策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决在完美匹配的 DNA 系统中调节构象变化的挑战,这对于开发先进的分子设备至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究涉及仅使用两条短 DNA 链设计和组装二维 DNA 三角形,重点是其响应 RNA 序列的结构转变。
核心方法
这些方法包括使用立足点介导的链位移来触发结构变化,并通过低温电子显微镜(cryo-EM)和质谱(MS)分析验证转化途径。
主要结果
由相应RNA序列引起的链位移改变了DNA三角形的组装途径,从而无需立足点即可形成三维RNA-DNA混合四边形。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,RNA 链置换为构建动态 DNA 纳米结构提供了一种新策略,这可能有助于分子诊断。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为开发更有效和更具体的信号报告系统提供信息,用于在临床环境中检测 HPV。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的证据,实验设计没有提供系统在真实临床场景中性能的详细验证。

Programmable 2D-to-3D structural transformation of DNA nanostructures triggered by single-stranded RNA.

Authors: Xiaoqiu Zheng, Tingyu Zhu, Bingbing Lyu, Liyou Guo, Yilin Wang, Hong Zhang, Dong-Yang Zhang, Hongfei He, Jiazhen Lyu
Original abstract (English)
Controllable structural transformation of DNA nanostructures offers substantial potential for molecular devices. Toehold-mediated strand displacement, a common strategy for driving such rearrangements, generally requires a dedicated toehold sequence to initiate the reaction, making the regulation of conformational changes in perfectly matched DNA systems challenging. To circumvent this limitation, we herein efficiently assembled a two-dimensional (2D) DNA triangle using only two short DNA strands. Inspired by the structural diversity of RNA, we found that strand displacement induced by the corresponding RNA sequence can alter the assembly pathway of the triangle without a toehold, leading to the formation of a three-dimensional (3D) RNA-DNA hybrid quadrilateral. All nanostructures and their transformation pathways were confirmed by cryogenic-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) and mass spectrometry (MS) analyses. Further, to investigate whether our designed transformable DNA nanostructure can serve as a target-specific signal reporting system, we developed a nucleic acid detection strategy based on isothermal amplification coupled with single-stranded RNA displacement-induced transformation of the DNA nanostructure (SDT), which successfully detected human papillomavirus (HPV) in clinical samples. Our experimental results demonstrate that RNA strand displacement provides new insights into constructing transformable DNA nanostructures and offers a novel design strategy for dynamic DNA nanostructures in molecular diagnostics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of regulating conformational changes in perfectly matched DNA systems, which is critical for developing advanced molecular devices.
Design and population
The research involved the design and assembly of a two-dimensional DNA triangle using only two short DNA strands, with the focus on its structural transformation in response to RNA sequences.
Core methods
The methods included the use of toehold-mediated strand displacement to trigger structural changes, and the transformation pathways were validated through cryogenic-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) and mass spectrometry (MS) analyses.
Main results
Strand displacement induced by the corresponding RNA sequence altered the assembly pathway of the DNA triangle, resulting in the formation of a three-dimensional RNA-DNA hybrid quadrilateral without the need for a toehold.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that RNA strand displacement provides a new strategy for constructing dynamic DNA nanostructures, which could be useful in molecular diagnostics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results may inform the development of more efficient and specific signal reporting systems for detecting HPV in clinical settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence, and the experimental design does not provide detailed validation of the system's performance in real-world clinical scenarios.

21例“同步”宫颈内膜和卵巢腺癌临床病理分析

Clinicopathological analysis of 21 cases of "Synchronous" endocervical and ovarian adenocarcinomas.
作者： Haining Zhang, Xiaozhuo Gao, Lili Sun, Xuerui Ma, Ning Zhang, Fujing Sun, Yanmei Zhu
摘要中文翻译全面探讨“同步”宫颈内膜和卵巢腺癌的临床病理特征、免疫组织化学(IHC)特征和HPV状态。对21例“同步”宫颈内膜和卵巢腺癌进行回顾性分析。HE染色用于形态学观察。IHC染色检测p16、Mucin 6、Claudin18.2、NapsinA和P504S的表达。使用 RNAscope 方法进行 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染检测。21例宫颈癌病例中,8例表现为假复层核排列,顶端有丝分裂象和凋亡小体明显,11例细胞胞浆清晰,细胞边界清晰,其余2例表现为蹄钉细胞。卵巢肿瘤再现了原发性卵巢上皮肿瘤的形态谱,并表现出侵袭性生长模式。部分病例可见子宫内膜/肌层和输卵管受累,部分病例表现为淋巴管间隙侵犯和淋巴结转移。IHC分析显示,8例宫颈和卵巢腺癌均呈p16弥漫性强阳性,11例共表达Claudin18.2和Mucin 6,2例NapsinA和P504S同时表达阳性。所有 8 例宫颈内膜和卵巢腺癌病例的 RNAscope 检测结果均为阳性。8 例最终分类为 HPV 相关 (HPVA) 宫颈内腺癌 (EAC) 卵巢转移,其余 13 例为 HPV 独立 (HPVI) EAC 转移。EAC 卵巢转移的 诊断 需要仔细整合形态学特征与其他临床病理学特征。标记物 p16、Claudin18.2 和 Mucin 6 对 诊断 有用,必要时建议进行 HPV RNAscope 检测。卵巢转移可能涉及沿子宫内膜/输卵管的上皮内扩散、传统的淋巴管侵犯和侵袭性生长模式。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨“同步”宫颈内膜和卵巢腺癌的临床病理特征、免疫组化特征和HPV状态。
设计与对象
对21例“同步”宫颈内膜和卵巢腺癌进行回顾性分析。
核心方法
HE染色进行形态学观察,IHC染色检测p16、Mucin 6、Claudin18.2、NapsinA、P504S的表达,RNAscope法检测人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染。
主要结果
21例宫颈癌病例中,8例表现为假复层核排列,顶端有丝分裂象和凋亡小体明显,11例细胞胞浆清晰,细胞边界清晰,其余2例表现为蹄钉细胞。IHC分析显示,8例宫颈和卵巢腺癌均呈p16弥漫性强阳性,11例共表达Claudin18.2和Mucin 6,2例NapsinA和P504S同时表达阳性。所有 8 例宫颈内膜和卵巢腺癌病例的 RNAscope 检测结果均为阳性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,EAC 卵巢转移的 诊断 需要仔细整合形态学特征与其他临床病理学特征。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,p16、Claudin18.2 和 Mucin 6 是 诊断 的有用标记,必要时建议进行 HPV RNAscope 检测。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和缺乏纵向数据而受到限制,但由于详细的临床病理学分析,证据是强有力的。

Clinicopathological analysis of 21 cases of "Synchronous" endocervical and ovarian adenocarcinomas.

Authors: Haining Zhang, Xiaozhuo Gao, Lili Sun, Xuerui Ma, Ning Zhang, Fujing Sun, Yanmei Zhu
Original abstract (English)
To comprehensively investigate the clinicopathological characteristics, immunohistochemical (IHC) profiles and HPV status of "synchronous" endocervical and ovarian adenocarcinoma. Retrospective analysis of 21 cases of "synchronous" endocervical and ovarian adenocarcinoma was conducted. HE staining was used for morphological observation. IHC staining was performed to detect expression of p16, Mucin 6, Claudin18.2, NapsinA, and P504S. Testing for human papillomavirus (HPV) infection was carried out using the RNAscope method. Among the 21 cervical cancer cases, eight exhibited pseudostratified nuclear arrangement with prominent apical mitotic figures and apoptotic bodies, 11 had cells with clear cytoplasm and distinct cell borders, while the remaining two displayed hobnail cells. The ovarian tumors recapitulated the morphological spectrum of primary ovarian epithelial tumors and demonstrated invasive growth patterns. Endometrial/myometrial and fallopian tube involvement was observed in some cases, while others presented with lymphovascular space invasion and lymph node metastasis. IHC analysis revealed that all eight cases of both cervical and ovarian adenocarcinoma exhibited diffuse and strong positivity for p16, 11 cases co-expressed Claudin18.2 and Mucin 6, and two cases were positive for both NapsinA and P504S. RNAscope testing results were positive for all eight endocervical and ovarian adenocarcinoma cases. The final classification of eight cases was ovarian metastasis of HPV-associated (HPVA) endocervical adenocarcinoma (EAC) and the remaining 13 as metastasis of HPV independent (HPVI) EAC. The diagnosis of ovarian metastasis from EAC requires careful integration of morphological features with other clinicopathological characteristics. The markers p16, Claudin18.2 and Mucin 6 are useful for diagnosis, with HPV RNAscope testing recommended when necessary. Ovarian metastasis may involve intraepithelial spread along the endometrium/fallopian tube, traditional lymphovascular invasion, and invasive growth patterns.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the clinicopathological characteristics, immunohistochemical profiles and HPV status of "synchronous" endocervical and ovarian adenocarcinoma.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis of 21 cases of "synchronous" endocervical and ovarian adenocarcinoma was conducted.
Core methods
HE staining was used for morphological observation, IHC staining was performed to detect expression of p16, Mucin 6, Claudin18.2, NapsinA, and P504S, and testing for human papillomavirus (HPV) infection was carried out using the RNAscope method.
Main results
Among the 21 cervical cancer cases, eight exhibited pseudostratified nuclear arrangement with prominent apical mitotic figures and apoptotic bodies, 11 had cells with clear cytoplasm and distinct cell borders, while the remaining two displayed hobnail cells. IHC analysis revealed that all eight cases of both cervical and ovarian adenocarcinoma exhibited diffuse and strong positivity for p16, 11 cases co-expressed Claudin18.2 and Mucin 6, and two cases were positive for both NapsinA and P504S. RNAscope testing results were positive for all eight endocervical and ovarian adenocarcinoma cases.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the diagnosis of ovarian metastasis from EAC requires careful integration of morphological features with other clinicopathological characteristics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that p16, Claudin18.2 and Mucin 6 are useful markers for diagnosis, with HPV RNAscope testing recommended when necessary.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the lack of longitudinal data, but the evidence is strong due to the detailed clinicopathological analysis.

Journal: Frontiers in Oral Health  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-29  |  DOI: 10.3389/froh.2026.1800454  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 7
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

HOMINY(人乳头瘤病毒、人类免疫缺陷病毒和尼日利亚青年口腔微生物群相互作用)的基线特征前瞻性纵向 队列研究

Baseline characteristics of the HOMINY (human papillomavirus, human immunodeficiency virus, and oral microbiota interplay in Nigerian youth) prospective longitudinal cohort
作者： Erika Juhlin, Morgan C. Byrd, Rong Jiang, Toluwalope Gbolahan, Fidelis E. Eki-Udoko, Ozoemene N. Obuekwe, Yana Bromberg, Nicolas F. Schlecht, Modupe O. Coker, Nosayaba Osazuwa-Peters
摘要中文翻译背景 HOMINY(人乳头瘤病毒,尼日利亚青少年中的人类免疫缺陷病毒和口腔微生物群的相互作用)队列研究 是一项前瞻性纵向研究,旨在调查尼日利亚感染和未感染 HIV 的母子对的口腔 HPV 感染和口腔微生物组。本分析的目的是描述 队列研究 的基线人口统计和临床特征,包括 HPV 认知、HPV 疫苗接种 和按 HIV 状态划分的 宫颈癌 筛查实践。方法 贝宁大学教学医院前瞻性招募母亲(≥ 18 岁)和儿童(9-18 岁)。参与者根据艾滋病毒感染和暴露状况进行分组。使用标准化问卷、病历审查和 病例报告 表格收集基线人口统计、行为和 HPV 相关数据。使用参数和非参数检验评估组间差异。结果 共有 1,075 名参与者入组。在母亲中,68% 的人艾滋病毒呈阳性。对 HPV (<7%) 和 HPV 疫苗 (3.0%) 的认知度较低,不到 1% 的人接受过 HPV 疫苗接种,并且只有 19.1% 的人报告曾接受过 宫颈癌 筛查。与未感染艾滋病毒的母亲相比,感染艾滋病毒的母亲首次性行为的年龄更小,终身性伴侣更多,受教育程度更低。在儿童中,对 HPV 和 HPV 疫苗 的认识几乎不存在,只有一名儿童报告了 HPV 疫苗接种。出生特征因艾滋病毒感染状况而异,剖腹产和其他围产期暴露在艾滋病毒暴露群体中较高。结论 基线结果表明,在实施尼日利亚国家 HPV 疫苗接种 计划之前,受 HIV 影响的家庭在 HPV 认识、疫苗接种 和 宫颈癌 筛查方面存在巨大差距。这突显了关键的预防差距和持续的癌症风险,凸显了尼日利亚青年迫切需要有针对性的 HPV 和 HIV 预防。作为撒哈拉以南非洲最大的专注于口腔 HPV 和 HIV 的前瞻性母子 队列研究,HOMINY 为未来口腔 HPV 感染、口腔微生物组和 HPV 疫苗接种 影响的纵向研究提供了重要基础。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
HOMINY 前瞻性纵向 队列研究 研究调查了尼日利亚感染和未感染 HIV 的母子对的口腔 HPV 感染和口腔微生物组,重点关注 HPV 认知、疫苗接种 和按 HIV 状况划分的 宫颈癌 筛查实践。
证据基础与方法
这是一项在贝宁大学教学医院进行的前瞻性纵向研究,招募了母亲(≥18 岁)和儿童(9-18 岁)。参与者根据艾滋病毒感染和暴露状况进行分组。使用标准化问卷、病历审查和 病例报告 表格收集基线数据,并使用参数和非参数检验评估组间差异。
共识与结论
基线结果显示,HPV 知晓率 (<7%)、HPV 疫苗接种(知晓率 3.0%,疫苗接种率 <1%)和 宫颈癌 筛查(母亲中 19.1%)存在巨大差距。与未感染艾滋病毒的母亲相比,感染艾滋病毒的母亲报告首次性行为更早,终生性伴侣更多,受教育程度较低。儿童几乎没有 HPV 意识或 疫苗接种。
争议与缺口
该研究强调了关键的预防差距和持续的癌症风险,但没有解决 HPV 传播 的机制或长期结果。值得注意的是,有关 HPV 特定类型及其与该人群口腔微生物组动态的关联的数据有限。
科研与实践启示
尼日利亚青年迫切需要制定有针对性的 HPV 和 HIV 预防策略,特别是解决认识不足和 疫苗接种 率的问题。HOMINY 队列研究 为未来口腔 HPV 感染、口腔微生物组和 HPV 疫苗接种 影响的纵向研究奠定了基础。

Baseline characteristics of the HOMINY (human papillomavirus, human immunodeficiency virus, and oral microbiota interplay in Nigerian youth) prospective longitudinal cohort

Authors: Erika Juhlin, Morgan C. Byrd, Rong Jiang, Toluwalope Gbolahan, Fidelis E. Eki-Udoko, Ozoemene N. Obuekwe, Yana Bromberg, Nicolas F. Schlecht, Modupe O. Coker, Nosayaba Osazuwa-Peters
Original abstract (English)
Background The HOMINY (Human Papillomavirus, Human Immunodeficiency Virus, and Oral Microbiota Interplay in Nigerian Youth) cohort is a prospective longitudinal study established to investigate oral HPV infection and the oral microbiome among mother-child pairs with and without HIV in Nigeria. The objective of this analysis was to describe baseline demographic and clinical characteristics of the cohort, including HPV awareness, HPV vaccination, and cervical cancer screening practice by HIV status. Method Mothers (aged ≥ 18) and children (aged 9–18) were prospectively enrolled at the University of Benin Teaching Hospital. Participants were grouped by HIV infection and exposure status. Baseline demographic, behavioral, and HPV-related data were collected using standardized questionnaires, medical record review, and case report forms. Group differences were assessed using parametric and non-parametric tests. Results A total of 1,075 participants were enrolled. Among mothers, 68% were HIV-positive. Awareness of HPV (<7%) and the HPV vaccine (3.0%) was low with fewer than 1% having received HPV vaccination, and only 19.1% reported ever undergoing cervical cancer screening. Mothers living with HIV reported younger age at sexual debut, more lifetime sexual partners, and lower education attainment than HIV-uninfected mothers. Among children, awareness of HPV and the HPV vaccine was virtually absent, and only one child reported HPV vaccination. Birth characteristics differed by HIV status, with Caesarian sections and other perinatal exposures being higher among HIV-exposed groups. Conclusion Baseline findings demonstrate substantial gaps in HPV awareness, vaccination, and cervical cancer screening among families affected by HIV before implementation of Nigeria's national HPV vaccination program. This highlights critical prevention gaps and sustained cancer risk, underscoring urgent needs for targeted HPV and HIV prevention in Nigerian youth. As the largest prospective mother-child cohort focused on oral HPV and HIV in sub-Saharan Africa, HOMINY provides an important foundation for future longitudinal studies of oral HPV infection, the oral microbiome, and the impact of HPV vaccination.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The HOMINY prospective longitudinal cohort study investigates oral HPV infection and the oral microbiome among mother-child pairs with and without HIV in Nigeria, focusing on HPV awareness, vaccination, and cervical cancer screening practices by HIV status.
Evidence base and method
This is a prospective longitudinal study conducted at the University of Benin Teaching Hospital, enrolling mothers (aged ≥18) and children (aged 9–18). Participants were grouped by HIV infection and exposure status. Baseline data were collected using standardized questionnaires, medical record review, and case report forms, with group differences assessed using parametric and non-parametric tests.
Consensus and conclusions
Baseline findings reveal substantial gaps in HPV awareness (<7%), HPV vaccination (3.0% awareness, <1% vaccinated), and cervical cancer screening (19.1% among mothers). Mothers with HIV reported younger sexual debut, more lifetime sexual partners, and lower education than HIV-uninfected mothers. Children had virtually no HPV awareness or vaccination.
Controversies and gaps
The study highlights critical prevention gaps and sustained cancer risk but does not address mechanisms of HPV transmission or long-term outcomes. Limited data on HPV-specific types and their associations with oral microbiome dynamics in this population are noted.
Research and practice implications
There is an urgent need for targeted HPV and HIV prevention strategies in Nigerian youth, particularly addressing low awareness and vaccination rates. The HOMINY cohort provides a foundation for future longitudinal studies on oral HPV infection, the oral microbiome, and HPV vaccination impact.

Journal: Australian and New Zealand journal of obstetrics and gynaecology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-01  |  DOI: 10.1111/ajo.70165  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 66, 4, e70165
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

妇科医生对活性 监测 对诊断为宫颈上皮内瘤变 2 的女性的看法和可接受性:一项定性研究。

Gynaecologists' Views and Acceptability of Active Surveillance for Women Diagnosed With Cervical Intraepithelial Neoplasia 2: A Qualitative Study.
作者： Dan Luo, Ashleigh R. Sharman, Deborah Bateson, Megan A. Smith, Kirsten I. Black, Katy Bell, Kirsten J McCaffery, Rachael H Dodd
摘要中文翻译背景:持续致癌 HPV 感染可引起宫颈上皮变化,导致癌前病变,如 CIN2。主动 监测 是一种保守的选择,可减少切除风险,同时在需要时允许及时 治疗。然而,对于临床医生在澳大利亚医疗保健环境中提供活性 监测 的观点、可接受性和经验知之甚少。目的:探讨妇科医生在向诊断为 CIN2 的女性提供活性 监测 方面的观点、可接受性和经验。材料和方法:对澳大利亚二级和三级医疗机构的妇产科专家和实习生进行了单独的半结构化访谈。使用归纳主题分析对数据进行分析。结果:进行了二十五次深度访谈。参与者对活性 监测 表达了积极的看法,认为它是一种不会增加长期癌症风险的安全选择。在制定 CIN2 的管理建议时,参与者考虑了各种相互关联的诊断、患者、临床医生和政策层面的因素。为了支持共同决策,参与者使用了多种沟通策略,例如解剖模型和视觉图像来帮助解决患者知识差距,并为患者提供了时间和空间来反思他们首选的管理计划。参与者确定了可以支持提供活性 监测 和患者遵守这一策略的策略,包括加强临床医生教育和培训、增加资源以及改进信息技术的使用以支持活性 监测 的后续测试。结论:更深入地了解影响临床医生对 CIN2 的推荐和实施活性 监测 的多层次因素可能有助于制定有针对性的干预措施,以解决障碍并加强推动因素,最终支持更广泛地提供活性 监测。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究重点关注澳大利亚医疗保健背景下妇科医生对诊断患有宫颈上皮内瘤变 2 (CIN2) 的女性的积极 监测 的看法、可接受性和经验。核心问题是探讨临床医生对实施这种保守管理方法的看法。
证据基础与方法
一项定性研究,采用对澳大利亚二级和三级医疗机构的妇产科专家和实习生进行个人半结构化访谈。使用归纳主题分析对数据进行分析。
共识与结论
参与者对活性 监测 表达了积极的看法,认为它是一种不会增加长期癌症风险的安全选择。他们强调了在制定 CIN2 管理建议时多层次因素的重要性,包括诊断、患者、临床医生和政策层面的考虑。
争议与缺口
该研究强调了对影响临床医生建议和积极 监测 实施的多层次因素的了解有限。证据差距包括更广泛采用的障碍和支持患者坚持积极 监测 的策略。
科研与实践启示
需要有针对性的干预措施来解决障碍并加强提供积极 监测 的推动因素。建议包括加强临床医生教育和培训、增加资源以及改进信息技术的使用以支持后续测试。强调解剖模型和视觉图像等沟通策略,以促进共同决策。

Gynaecologists' Views and Acceptability of Active Surveillance for Women Diagnosed With Cervical Intraepithelial Neoplasia 2: A Qualitative Study.

Authors: Dan Luo, Ashleigh R. Sharman, Deborah Bateson, Megan A. Smith, Kirsten I. Black, Katy Bell, Kirsten J McCaffery, Rachael H Dodd
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Persistent oncogenic HPV infection can cause cervical epithelial changes leading to premalignant lesions such as CIN2. Active surveillance, a conservative option, reduces exposure to excisional risks while allowing timely treatment when needed. However, little is known about clinicians' perspectives, acceptability, and experience of offering active surveillance in the Australian healthcare context. AIMS: To explore gynaecologists' perspectives, acceptability, and experiences in providing active surveillance to women diagnosed with CIN2. MATERIALS AND METHODS: Individual semi-structured interviews were conducted with obstetrics and gynaecology specialists and trainees in secondary and tertiary healthcare settings across Australia. Data were analysed using inductive thematic analysis. RESULTS: Twenty-five in-depth interviews were conducted. Participants expressed positive views about active surveillance, regarding it as a safe option that does not increase long-term cancer risk. When formulating management recommendations for CIN2, participants considered various interrelated diagnostic, patient, clinician, and policy-level factors. To support shared decision-making, participants used several communication strategies such as anatomical models and visual images to help address patient knowledge gaps and they provided patients with time and space to reflect on their preferred management plan. Participants identified strategies that could support offering active surveillance and patients' adherence to this, including enhanced clinician education and training, increased resourcing, and improved use of information technology to support follow-up tests for active surveillance. CONCLUSIONS: A deeper understanding of multi-level factors influencing clinicians' recommendations and implementation of active surveillance for CIN2 may inform the development of targeted interventions to address barriers and strengthen enablers, ultimately supporting wider offering of active surveillance.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study focuses on gynaecologists' perspectives, acceptability, and experiences regarding active surveillance for women diagnosed with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2 (CIN2) in the Australian healthcare context. The central question is to explore clinicians' views on implementing this conservative management approach.
Evidence base and method
A qualitative study using individual semi-structured interviews with obstetrics and gynaecology specialists and trainees in secondary and tertiary healthcare settings across Australia. Data were analyzed using inductive thematic analysis.
Consensus and conclusions
Participants expressed positive views about active surveillance, considering it a safe option that does not increase long-term cancer risk. They emphasized the importance of multi-level factors, including diagnostic, patient, clinician, and policy-level considerations, when formulating management recommendations for CIN2.
Controversies and gaps
The study highlights limited understanding of multi-level factors influencing clinicians' recommendations and implementation of active surveillance. Evidence gaps include barriers to wider adoption and strategies to support patient adherence to active surveillance.
Research and practice implications
Targeted interventions are needed to address barriers and strengthen enablers for offering active surveillance. Recommendations include enhanced clinician education and training, increased resourcing, and improved use of information technology to support follow-up tests. Communication strategies such as anatomical models and visual images are emphasized to facilitate shared decision-making.

用于预防、检测和 Z​​XQTERMVQXZ 咽部感染的纳米材料:转化路线图

Nanomaterials for the Prevention, Detection, and Treatment of Pharyngeal Human Papillomavirus Infection: A Translational Roadmap
作者： Lorena Păun, Mihai Dumitru, Diana Gabriela Iacob, O. Pătrașcu, Daniela Vrînceanu, Rareș Oanca, Alexandru-Darius Dragomir-Serboiu, Andreea Marinescu, Monica-Mihaela Cirstoiu, Lorena Adriana Paun
摘要中文翻译高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)(特别是 HPV16)的咽部感染在生物学上与宫颈感染不同,因为它发生在韦氏环的特殊淋巴上皮环境中。本综述评估了用于预防、检测和 Z​​XQTERMVQXZ 咽部 HPV 的纳米颗粒材料,重点是结构-特性-功能关系、粘膜性能和转化可行性。比较了脂质纳米颗粒平台、聚合物纳米颗粒平台、无机系统和混合平台的组成、粒径分布、表面电荷、胶体稳定性、生物降解性、有效负载兼容性、释放行为和制造复杂性。有证据表明,脂质和聚合物系统是粘膜 疫苗接种 和局部核酸递送的最可靠的近期候选者,因为它们在粘液暴露组织中的可控制造、分析易处理性和生物学合理性能之间提供了最佳平衡。相比之下,无机治疗诊断学和支持 CRISPR 的平台的开发仍处于早期阶段,因为重复的粘膜给药、淋巴组织中的保留以及组合产品监管带来了巨大的负担。提出了一个转化路线图,其中材料选择以临床相关质量属性、标准化唾液和粘液相关测定、人类扁桃体类器官测试以及对可制造性、安全性和监管策略的早期关注为指导。该领域前景广阔,但直接的咽部 HPV 数据仍然有限;因此,迫切需要进行比较研究,将纳米颗粒结构与可测量的结果(如扁桃体沉积、上皮摄取、免疫激活和局部耐受性)联系起来。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的咽部感染,特别是 HPV16,由于其发生在韦氏环中,因此在生物学上与宫颈感染不同。核心问题涉及评估用于预防、检测和 Z​​XQTERMVQXZ 咽部 HPV 的纳米材料,强调结构-特性-功能关系、粘膜性能和转化可行性。
证据基础与方法
该综述评估了用于咽部 HPV 的纳米颗粒材料,根据成分、粒径分布、表面电荷、胶体稳定性、生物降解性、有效负载兼容性、释放行为和制造复杂性,比较了脂质纳米颗粒平台、聚合物纳米颗粒平台、无机系统和混合平台。
共识与结论
脂质和聚合物系统被认为是粘膜 疫苗接种 和局部核酸递送的最可靠的近期候选者,因为它们在粘液暴露组织中的可控制造、分析易处理性和生物学合理性能之间取得了平衡。
争议与缺口
由于重复粘膜给药、淋巴组织滞留以及组合产品监管等方面的挑战,无机治疗诊断和支持 CRISPR 的平台的开发仍处于早期阶段。直接咽部 HPV 数据有限,突出表明需要进行将纳米颗粒结构与可测量结果联系起来的比较研究。
科研与实践启示
提出了转化路线图,通过临床相关的质量属性、标准化唾液和粘液相关测定、人类扁桃体类器官测试以及对可制造性、安全性和监管策略的早期关注来指导材料选择。

Nanomaterials for the Prevention, Detection, and Treatment of Pharyngeal Human Papillomavirus Infection: A Translational Roadmap

Authors: Lorena Păun, Mihai Dumitru, Diana Gabriela Iacob, O. Pătrașcu, Daniela Vrînceanu, Rareș Oanca, Alexandru-Darius Dragomir-Serboiu, Andreea Marinescu, Monica-Mihaela Cirstoiu, Lorena Adriana Paun
Original abstract (English)
Pharyngeal infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV), particularly HPV16, is biologically distinct from cervical infection because it occurs within the specialized lymphoepithelial environment of Waldeyer’s ring. This review evaluates nanoparticle materials for the prevention, detection, and treatment of pharyngeal HPV, with an emphasis on structure–property–function relationships, mucosal performance, and translational feasibility. Lipid nanoparticle platforms, polymeric nanoparticle platforms, inorganic systems, and hybrid platforms are compared with respect to composition, particle size distribution, surface charge, colloidal stability, biodegradability, payload compatibility, release behavior, and manufacturing complexity. Evidence suggests that lipid and polymeric systems are the most credible near-future candidates for mucosal vaccination and localized nucleic acid delivery because they offer the best balance between controllable fabrication, analytical tractability, and biologically plausible performance in mucus-exposed tissue. By contrast, the development of inorganic theranostics and CRISPR-enabled platforms remains at an earlier stage because repeated mucosal dosing, retention in lymphoid tissue, and combined product regulation impose substantial burdens. A translational roadmap is proposed in which material selection is guided by clinically relevant quality attributes, standardized saliva- and mucus-relevant assays, human tonsil organoid testing, and early attention to manufacturability, safety, and regulatory strategy. The field is promising, but direct pharyngeal HPV data remain limited; accordingly, there is an urgent need for comparative studies that connect nanoparticle architecture to measurable outcomes such as tonsillar deposition, epithelial uptake, immune activation, and local tolerability.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on pharyngeal infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV), particularly HPV16, which is biologically distinct from cervical infection due to its occurrence in Waldeyer’s ring. The central question involves evaluating nanomaterials for the prevention, detection, and treatment of pharyngeal HPV, emphasizing structure–property–function relationships, mucosal performance, and translational feasibility.
Evidence base and method
The review evaluates nanoparticle materials for pharyngeal HPV, comparing lipid nanoparticle platforms, polymeric nanoparticle platforms, inorganic systems, and hybrid platforms based on composition, particle size distribution, surface charge, colloidal stability, biodegradability, payload compatibility, release behavior, and manufacturing complexity.
Consensus and conclusions
Lipid and polymeric systems are identified as the most credible near-future candidates for mucosal vaccination and localized nucleic acid delivery due to their balance between controllable fabrication, analytical tractability, and biologically plausible performance in mucus-exposed tissue.
Controversies and gaps
The development of inorganic theranostics and CRISPR-enabled platforms remains at an early stage due to challenges with repeated mucosal dosing, retention in lymphoid tissue, and combined product regulation. Direct pharyngeal HPV data are limited, highlighting the need for comparative studies linking nanoparticle architecture to measurable outcomes.
Research and practice implications
A translational roadmap is proposed, guiding material selection through clinically relevant quality attributes, standardized saliva- and mucus-relevant assays, human tonsil organoid testing, and early attention to manufacturability, safety, and regulatory strategy.

高危型HPV 菌株在 宫颈癌 中诱导的表观遗传修饰和 宿主 基因沉默特征

Epigenetic Modification and Host Gene Silencing Profile Induced by High-Risk HPV Strains in Cervical Cancer
作者： Anindita Kundu, Bhaskar Narayan Chaudhuri, Partha Guchhait, Arup Kumar Dawn, Satadal Das
摘要中文翻译高危人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)持续感染是宫颈癌的主要驱动因素;然而,仅病毒存在不足以诱发恶性肿瘤。从初始感染到浸润性癌的进展需要病毒 基因组 和 宿主 细胞的调节机制发生一系列协调一致的改变。本综述重点关注 HR-HPV 绕过 宿主 监管控制的三个主要机制:表观遗传修饰、免疫逃避和内分泌信号传导。具体来说,我们描述了病毒癌蛋白 E6 和 E7 如何招募 宿主 DNA 甲基转移酶(特别是 DNMT1、DNMT3A 和 DNMT3B),以通过启动子高甲基化沉默肿瘤抑制基因,例如 CCNA、hTERT 和 E-钙粘蛋白。除了 DNA 甲基化之外,HPV 还通过与 p300/CBP、NuRD 复合物和 Polycomb 族蛋白的相互作用来破坏组蛋白乙酰化和甲基化模式,同时使非编码 RNA 和特定 microRNA 失调,以强化这种转录沉默状态。该综述进一步详细介绍了 HPV 如何通过驱动 Th1 细胞因子转变为 Th2 细胞因子、下调 MHC I 类表达以及将骨髓源性抑制细胞和调节性 T 细胞招募到宫颈微环境来逃避免疫清除。此外,我们还讨论了辅助因子的作用,重点关注雌二醇和 HPV 癌基因之间的协同相互作用;这种相互作用通过基质成纤维细胞和浸润免疫细胞上的雌激素受体α信号传导促进局部免疫抑制。我们还研究了高胎次作为关键流行病学风险因素的贡献。对三项不同研究的患者数据进行比较分析,以说明人口统计和生殖变量如何与不同人群的宫颈病变严重程度相关。最终,宫颈癌的发生源于多途径相互作用,其中病毒癌基因、表观遗传变化、免疫逃逸和激素因素
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述将高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 视为 宫颈癌 的主要病因驱动因素,重点关注 HR-HPV 如何破坏 宿主 监管机制以驱动恶性转化的核心问题。该范围明确针对 HR-HPV 类型(例如 HPV16、HPV18)及其相关的宫颈癌发生,不包括非目标乳头瘤病毒和非 HPV 环境。
证据基础与方法
这是一篇叙述性综述,综合了 HR-HPV 癌蛋白(E6、E7)改变 宿主 表观遗传调节、免疫逃避和激素辅助因子相互作用的机制。该综述不报告系统检索方法、资格标准或纳入研究计数,也不进行荟萃分析或共识过程。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,HR-HPV 癌蛋白 E6 和 E7 招募 宿主 DNA 甲基转移酶(DNMT1、DNMT3A、DNMT3B)通过启动子高甲基化来沉默肿瘤抑制基因(例如 CCNA、hTERT、E-钙粘蛋白)。此外,HPV 通过与 p300/CBP、NuRD 和 Polycomb 族蛋白的相互作用破坏组蛋白修饰模式,同时通过 Th1 到 Th2 细胞因子转变、MHC I 类下调以及骨髓源性抑制细胞和调节性 T 细胞的募集来驱动免疫逃逸。
争议与缺口
该综述强调了不同人群之间人口和生殖风险因素(例如高胎次)的异质性,但没有解决相互矛盾的发现或薄弱的方法。除了粗略提及三项研究之外,它还缺乏不同人群病变严重程度的比较数据,从而留下了关于雌二醇和胎次等辅助因子在宫颈癌发生中的相对贡献的未解决的问题。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,针对 HPV 驱动的表观遗传修饰(例如 DNMT 抑制)和免疫逃避途径(例如 MHC I 类上调)可以为治疗策略提供信息。它还建议优先研究激素辅助因子相互作用(例如雌激素受体α信号传导)和流行病学风险分层,以改善 宫颈癌 的预防和早期检测。

Epigenetic Modification and Host Gene Silencing Profile Induced by High-Risk HPV Strains in Cervical Cancer

Authors: Anindita Kundu, Bhaskar Narayan Chaudhuri, Partha Guchhait, Arup Kumar Dawn, Satadal Das
Original abstract (English)
Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) is the primary driver of cervical cancer; however, viral presence alone is insufficient to induce malignancy. The progression from initial infection to invasive carcinoma requires a coordinated series of alterations in both the viral genome and the host cell's regulatory machinery. This review focuses on three principal mechanisms by which HR-HPV bypasses host regulatory controls: epigenetic modifications, immune evasion, and endocrine signaling. Specifically, we describe how the viral oncoproteins E6 and E7 recruit host DNA methyltransferases—particularly DNMT1, DNMT3A, and DNMT3B—to silence tumor suppressor genes such as CCNA, hTERT, and E-cadherin via promoter hypermethylation. Beyond DNA methylation, HPV disrupts histone acetylation and methylation patterns through interactions with p300/CBP, the NuRD complex, and Polycomb group proteins, while simultaneously dysregulating non-coding RNAs and specific microRNAs to reinforce this transcriptionally silenced state. The review further details how HPV escapes immune clearance by driving a Th1-to-Th2 cytokine shift, downregulating MHC class I expression, and recruiting myeloid-derived suppressor cells and regulatory T cells to the cervical microenvironment. Additionally, we discuss the role of cofactors, focusing on the synergistic interaction between estradiol and HPV oncogenes; this interaction promotes local immunosuppression via estrogen receptor alpha signaling on stromal fibroblasts and infiltrating immune cells. We also examine the contribution of high parity as a key epidemiological risk factor. A comparative analysis of patient data across three distinct studies is presented to illustrate how demographic and reproductive variables correlate with cervical lesion severity in different populations. Ultimately, cervical carcinogenesis stems from multi-pathway interactions where viral oncogenes, epigenetic changes, immune escape, and hormonal factor
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) as the primary etiological driver of cervical cancer, focusing on the central question of how HR-HPV disrupts host regulatory mechanisms to drive malignant transformation. The scope explicitly targets HR-HPV types (e.g., HPV16, HPV18) and their associated cervical carcinogenesis, excluding non-target papillomaviruses and non-HPV contexts.
Evidence base and method
This is a narrative review that synthesizes mechanisms by which HR-HPV oncoproteins (E6, E7) alter host epigenetic regulation, immune evasion, and hormonal cofactor interactions. The review does not report systematic search methods, eligibility criteria, or included-study counts, nor does it perform meta-analysis or consensus processes.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that HR-HPV oncoproteins E6 and E7 recruit host DNA methyltransferases (DNMT1, DNMT3A, DNMT3B) to silence tumor suppressor genes (e.g., CCNA, hTERT, E-cadherin) via promoter hypermethylation. Additionally, HPV disrupts histone modification patterns through interactions with p300/CBP, NuRD, and Polycomb group proteins, while driving immune escape via Th1-to-Th2 cytokine shifts, MHC class I downregulation, and recruitment of myeloid-derived suppressor cells and regulatory T cells.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights heterogeneity in demographic and reproductive risk factors (e.g., high parity) across populations but does not resolve conflicting findings or weak methods. It also lacks comparative data on lesion severity across diverse populations beyond a cursory mention of three studies, leaving unresolved questions about the relative contributions of cofactors like estradiol and parity in cervical carcinogenesis.
Research and practice implications
The review implies that targeting HPV-driven epigenetic modifications (e.g., DNMT inhibition) and immune evasion pathways (e.g., MHC class I upregulation) could inform therapeutic strategies. It also suggests prioritizing research on hormonal cofactor interactions (e.g., estrogen receptor alpha signaling) and epidemiological risk stratification to improve cervical cancer prevention and early detection.

治疗 对非免疫功能低下成人肛门生殖器疣的副作用:随机对照试验的网络荟萃分析

Adverse Effects of Treatments for Anogenital Warts in Non-immunocompromised Adults: A Network Meta-analysis of Randomized Controlled Trials
作者： Réda Saib, Elisa Joly, S. Fouéré, C Derancourt, Babacar Tounkara, Cyril Ferdynus, A. Bertolotti
摘要中文翻译简介:国际肛门生殖器疣 (AGW) 治疗指南提出了多种一线选择,但并未根据安全性或耐受性优先考虑 治疗。本研究旨在比较免疫功能正常的成人中用于外部 AGW 的局部、全身和消融 治疗 的安全性。方法:对截至 2025 年 8 月发表的随机对照试验进行系统评价和频率网络荟萃分析。不良事件 (AE) 分为低级、中级或高级局部 AE(LGL、MGL、HGL)和低级一般 AE(LGG)。使用随机效应模型以安慰剂作为参考来估计相对风险。结果:我们纳入了 107 项随机对照试验,涉及 12,423 名参与者。与局部治疗相比,消融手术与中度至重度局部 AE 发生率较高相关。在外用 治疗 中,5% 咪喹莫特和西多福韦乳膏与较低的严重 AE 发生率相关,而鬼臼毒素则更常与 LGL 相关。SUCRA 排名将局部 治疗 的耐受性置于消融疗法之上。西多福韦乳膏的安全性最好,其次是 5% 咪喹莫特。结论:该网络荟萃分析强调了 AGW 治疗 耐受性的显着差异,并为以患者为中心的治疗决策提供了比较框架。它还强调了未来试验中标准化 AE 报告的必要性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 治疗 对非免疫功能低下成人肛门生殖器疣 (AGW) 的不良影响,比较局部、全身和消融疗法,以优先考虑安全性和耐受性。
证据基础与方法
截至 2025 年 8 月,对涉及 12,423 名参与者的 107 项随机对照试验 (RCT) 进行了系统回顾和频率网络荟萃分析,不良事件分为低、中或高级别局部和一般 AE。
共识与结论
外用 治疗(例如西多福韦乳膏、5% 咪喹莫特)比消融疗法具有更好的耐受性,西多福韦乳膏显示出最佳的安全性,SUCRA 排名更倾向于外用治疗方案。
争议与缺口
该审查强调了 治疗 耐受性的显着差异,但指出未来试验中需要标准化不良事件报告。
科研与实践启示
研究结果为以患者为中心的治疗决策提供了一个比较框架,并强调在未来的试验中改进 AE 报告。

Adverse Effects of Treatments for Anogenital Warts in Non-immunocompromised Adults: A Network Meta-analysis of Randomized Controlled Trials

Authors: Réda Saib, Elisa Joly, S. Fouéré, C Derancourt, Babacar Tounkara, Cyril Ferdynus, A. Bertolotti
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: International guidelines for the management of anogenital warts (AGWs) propose multiple first-line options but do not prioritize treatments based on safety or tolerability. This study aimed to compare the safety profiles of topical, systemic, and ablative treatments used for external AGWs in immunocompetent adults. METHODS: A systematic review and frequentist network meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials published through August 2025 was performed. Adverse events (AEs) were classified as low-, moderate-, or high-grade local AEs (LGL, MGL, HGL) and low-grade general AEs (LGG). Relative risks were estimated using random-effects models with placebo as the reference. RESULTS: We included 107 RCTs involving 12,423 participants. Ablative procedures were associated with higher rates of moderate-to-severe local AEs compared with topical therapies. Among topical treatments, imiquimod 5% and cidofovir cream were associated with lower rates of severe AEs, whereas podophyllotoxin was more often associated with LGL. SUCRA rankings placed topical treatments above ablative therapies in terms of tolerability. Cidofovir cream demonstrated the best safety profile, followed by imiquimod 5%. CONCLUSION: This network meta-analysis highlights significant differences in tolerability across AGW treatments and offers a comparative framework for patient-centered therapeutic decision-making. It also emphasizes the need for standardized AE reporting in future trials.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on adverse effects of treatments for anogenital warts (AGWs) in non-immunocompromised adults, comparing topical, systemic, and ablative therapies to prioritize safety and tolerability.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review and frequentist network meta-analysis of 107 randomized controlled trials (RCTs) involving 12,423 participants, published through August 2025, with adverse events classified as low-, moderate-, or high-grade local and general AEs.
Consensus and conclusions
Topical treatments (e.g., cidofovir cream, imiquimod 5%) had better tolerability than ablative therapies, with cidofovir cream demonstrating the best safety profile and SUCRA rankings favoring topical options.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights significant differences in tolerability across treatments but notes the need for standardized adverse event reporting in future trials.
Research and practice implications
Findings offer a comparative framework for patient-centered therapeutic decision-making and emphasize improving AE reporting in future trials.

Journal: International Journal of Biology Pharmacy and Allied Sciences  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-27  |  DOI: 10.31032/ijbpas/2026/15.8.10374  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 – 综述

HUMAN PAPILLOMA VIRUS VACCINE – A REVIEW
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译宫颈和其他肛门生殖器恶性肿瘤主要由人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 引起。由于预防性 疫苗接种,HPV 相关疾病的 患病率 在全球范围内急剧下降。本文深入概述了 HPV 疫苗,包括其作用机制、适应症、剂量、安全性、全球可用性、制造商和成本效益。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),特别是其在引起宫颈和其他肛门生殖器恶性肿瘤中的作用,并概述 HPV 疫苗,包括其作用机制、适应症、剂量、安全性、全球可用性、制造商和成本效益。
证据基础与方法
该审查基于总结 疫苗 对 HPV 相关疾病的影响的摘要,包括其作用机制、适应症、剂量、安全性、全球可用性、制造商和成本效益。证据来自摘要,不包括详细的研究设计或荟萃分析方法。
共识与结论
宫颈癌和其他肛门生殖器恶性肿瘤主要是由 HPV 引起的,并且由于预防性 疫苗接种,HPV 相关疾病的 患病率 在全球范围内急剧下降。它还强调了 疫苗 的作用机制、适应症、剂量、安全性、全球可用性、制造商和成本效益作为协议的关键领域。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据并未报告与 HPV 疫苗 功效、安全性或成本效益相关的争议或证据差距。没有提到具体的限制或未解决的问题。
科研与实践启示
该审查意味着需要继续研究 HPV 疫苗 的有效性、安全性和成本效益,特别是在不同的人群和地区。它还表明了扩大 疫苗 在全球范围内的可用性和可访问性的重要性。

世界卫生组织东南亚区域的 HPV 疫苗接种:对采用情况、实施挑战和加速途径的范围审查。

HPV vaccination in the WHO South-East Asia Region: A scoping review of uptake, implementation challenges, and pathways to acceleration.
作者： Siddhesh Kolambkar, Mansi Tomar, Nazneen Arif, Showket Hussain, Ekta Gupta, Shalini Singh
摘要中文翻译宫颈癌 仍然是世卫组织东南亚区域 (SEAR) 的一个主要公共卫生问题,其中 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 覆盖率仍然不均匀。本次范围界定审查综合了 SEAR 国家 HPV 疫苗 的采用和实施策略以及挑战的证据。遵循 Arksey 和 O'Malley 框架以及 PRISMA 范围审查 (ScR) 指南,对 2003 年 1 月至 2026 年 2 月期间发表的 46 项研究进行了分析。11 个 SEAR 国家中有 10 个已在全国范围内引入 HPV 疫苗接种,其中不丹、斯里兰卡和泰国的覆盖率超过 90%,而一些国家仍处于早期实施或扩展阶段。确定了四个交叉障碍:政策和财政限制、社会文化阻力、卫生系统能力薄弱以及 疫苗 犹豫不决。基于这些发现,提出了 SEAR-HPV 综合实施模型 (SHIM),以加强政策支持、可持续融资、社区参与、劳动力准备和数字监控。本综述重点介绍了以证据为基础的策略,以加速 HPV 疫苗 的扩大规模并推进该地区的 宫颈癌 消除目标。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查重点关注世卫组织东南亚区域 (SEAR) 的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种,研究其采用情况、实施挑战以及加速 疫苗接种 覆盖的途径,以解决 宫颈癌 作为主要公共卫生问题的问题。
证据基础与方法
该范围界定审查综合了 2003 年 1 月至 2026 年 2 月期间发表的 46 项研究的证据,遵循 Arksey 和 O'Malley 框架以及 PRISMA 范围界定审查 (ScR) 指南。
共识与结论
11 个 SEAR 国家中有 10 个已在全国范围内引入 HPV 疫苗接种,其中不丹、斯里兰卡和泰国的覆盖率超过 90%。确定了四个交叉障碍:政策和财政限制、社会文化阻力、卫生系统能力薄弱以及 疫苗 犹豫不决。
争议与缺口
审查强调了 SEAR 国家的覆盖范围不均匀,一些国家仍处于早期实施或扩展阶段,表明可持续融资和卫生系统能力方面存在差距。
科研与实践启示
SEAR-HPV 综合实施模型 (SHIM) 旨在加强政策支持、可持续融资、社区参与、劳动力准备和数字监测,以加速 HPV 疫苗 的扩大规模并推进 宫颈癌 消除目标。

HPV vaccination in the WHO South-East Asia Region: A scoping review of uptake, implementation challenges, and pathways to acceleration.

Authors: Siddhesh Kolambkar, Mansi Tomar, Nazneen Arif, Showket Hussain, Ekta Gupta, Shalini Singh
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer remains a major public health concern in the WHO South-East Asia Region (SEAR), where human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination coverage remains uneven. This scoping review synthesizes evidence on HPV vaccine uptake and implementation strategies and challenges across SEAR countries. Following the Arksey and O'Malley framework and PRISMA-Scoping Review (ScR) guidelines, 46 studies published between January 2003 and February 2026 were analyzed. Ten of eleven SEAR countries have introduced HPV vaccination nationally, with Bhutan, Sri Lanka, and Thailand achieving coverage above 90%, while several countries remain in early implementation or expansion phases. Four cross-cutting barriers were identified: policy and financial constraints, sociocultural resistance, weak health-system capacity, and vaccine hesitancy. Based on these findings, SEAR-HPV Integrated Implementation Model (SHIM) is proposed to strengthen policy support, sustainable financing, community engagement, workforce preparedness, and digital monitoring. This review highlights evidence-informed strategies to accelerate HPV vaccine scale-up and advance cervical cancer elimination goals in the region.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination in the WHO South-East Asia Region (SEAR), examining uptake, implementation challenges, and pathways to accelerate vaccination coverage to address cervical cancer as a major public health concern.
Evidence base and method
This scoping review synthesized evidence from 46 studies published between January 2003 and February 2026, following the Arksey and O'Malley framework and PRISMA-Scoping Review (ScR) guidelines.
Consensus and conclusions
Ten of eleven SEAR countries have introduced HPV vaccination nationally, with Bhutan, Sri Lanka, and Thailand achieving coverage above 90%. Four cross-cutting barriers were identified: policy and financial constraints, sociocultural resistance, weak health-system capacity, and vaccine hesitancy.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights uneven coverage across SEAR countries, with several countries still in early implementation or expansion phases, indicating gaps in sustainable financing and health-system capacity.
Research and practice implications
The SEAR-HPV Integrated Implementation Model (SHIM) is proposed to strengthen policy support, sustainable financing, community engagement, workforce preparedness, and digital monitoring to accelerate HPV vaccine scale-up and advance cervical cancer elimination goals.

耳鼻喉科实践中的食管鳞状乳头状瘤:耳鼻喉科医生的病例系列和诊断注意事项。

Esophageal Squamous Papilloma in the Practice of Otolaryngology: A Case Series and Diagnostic Considerations for the otolaryngologist.
作者： Yuval Nachalon, Nogah Nativ-Zeltzer, Kurt B. Schaberg, Peter C. Belafsky, Nogah Nativ‐Zeltzer
摘要中文翻译背景:食管鳞状乳头状瘤(ESP)是一种罕见的良性上呼吸消化道病变,通常无症状。虽然历史上由胃肠病学家管理,但经鼻食管镜检查已被纳入一些耳鼻喉科实践中,扩大了耳鼻喉科医师在诊断这些病变中的作用。目的:描述三级吞咽困难中心内的 ESP 病例系列,并评估 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 与慢性粘膜刺激之间的关联。方法:对 2 年时间内在三级中心诊断为 ESP 的所有个体进行回顾性病例系列研究。对高危和低危 HPV 的组织病理学和 DNA 原位杂交 (ISH) 进行了综述。结果:发现 5 例病例。所有患者均出现吞咽困难或胃灼热。所有病例中 ESP 都是偶然发现的,与环咽功能障碍、食管裂孔疝或食管炎并存。一名患者有 HPV+ 口咽癌病史。所有病变均通过内镜成功切除。所有标本中 ISH 的 HPV 均为阴性。结论和意义:ESP 是一种良性实体,经常在吞咽困难检查过程中偶然遇到。这些结果支持慢性刺激而不是 HPV 为主要病因。认可对于耳鼻喉科医生确保适当的切除和 监测 至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究重点关注食管鳞状乳头状瘤 (ESP),这是一种罕见的上呼吸消化道良性病变,旨在描述三级吞咽困难中心的病例系列,同时评估 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 与慢性粘膜刺激之间的关联。
证据基础与方法
在一所三级医疗中心进行了一项为期2年的回顾性病例系列研究,并对高危型和低危型HPV的组织病理学和DNA原位杂交(ISH)结果进行了审查。
共识与结论
ESP 是一种良性实体,在吞咽困难检查过程中经常偶然遇到,结果支持慢性刺激而不是 HPV 作为主要病因。
争议与缺口
该研究确定样本量较小(5 个病例),并且所有样本均未检测到 HPV,这表明需要进行更大规模的研究来确认慢性刺激的病因作用。
科研与实践启示
识别 ESP 对于耳鼻喉科医生来说至关重要,以确保适当的切除和 监测,特别是对于吞咽困难或胃灼热的患者。

Esophageal Squamous Papilloma in the Practice of Otolaryngology: A Case Series and Diagnostic Considerations for the otolaryngologist.

Authors: Yuval Nachalon, Nogah Nativ-Zeltzer, Kurt B. Schaberg, Peter C. Belafsky, Nogah Nativ‐Zeltzer
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Esophageal squamous papilloma (ESP) is a rare, benign, often asymptomatic lesion of the upper aerodigestive tract. While historically managed by gastroenterologists, transnasal esophagoscopy has been incorporated into some otolaryngology practices, expanding the role of the otolaryngologist in diagnosing these lesions. AIMS: To describe a case series of ESP within a tertiary dysphagia center and evaluate the association between human papillomavirus (HPV) and chronic mucosal irritation. METHODS: A retrospective case series was conducted of all individuals diagnosed with ESP at a tertiary center over a 2-year period. Histopathology and DNA in-situ hybridization (ISH) for high and low-risk HPV were reviewed. RESULTS: Five cases were identified. All patients presented with dysphagia or heartburn. ESP was an incidental finding in all cases, co-existing with cricopharyngeal dysfunction, hiatal hernia, or esophagitis. One patient had a history of HPV+ oropharyngeal carcinoma. All lesions were successfully resected endoscopically. ISH was negative for HPV in all specimens. CONCLUSIONS AND SIGNIFICANCE: ESP is a benign entity frequently encountered incidentally during the workup of dysphagia. These results support chronic irritation rather than HPV as the primary etiology. Recognition is vital for otolaryngologists to ensure appropriate resection and surveillance.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study focuses on esophageal squamous papilloma (ESP), a rare benign lesion of the upper aerodigestive tract, and aims to describe a case series within a tertiary dysphagia center while evaluating the association between human papillomavirus (HPV) and chronic mucosal irritation.
Evidence base and method
A retrospective case series was conducted over a 2-year period at a tertiary center, with histopathology and DNA in-situ hybridization (ISH) for high and low-risk HPV reviewed.
Consensus and conclusions
ESP is a benign entity frequently encountered incidentally during dysphagia workup, and the results support chronic irritation rather than HPV as the primary etiology.
Controversies and gaps
The study identifies a small sample size (five cases) and the absence of HPV detection in all specimens, suggesting a need for larger studies to confirm the etiological role of chronic irritation.
Research and practice implications
Recognition of ESP is vital for otolaryngologists to ensure appropriate resection and surveillance, particularly in patients with dysphagia or heartburn.

头颈癌中的循环肿瘤 DNA:美国头颈学会的评论。

Circulating Tumor DNA in Head and Neck Cancer: A Review From the American Head and Neck Society.
作者： Sagar Kansara, Kevin Contrera, Scott Roof, Anne C. Kane, Rula Mualla, Eleni M Rettig, Nishant Agrawal, Barry Wenig, Joseph M Curry, Daniel Faden
摘要中文翻译重要性:尽管过去几十年来对头颈癌的了解取得了进展,但可靠的生物标志物的发现仍然难以实现。随着 人乳头瘤病毒 相关口咽癌和 Epstein-Barr 病毒相关鼻咽癌的出现,循环肿瘤 DNA (ctDNA) 和致癌病毒 DNA 已成为潜在有前途的生物标志物。观察结果:美国头颈协会针对 ctDNA 目前的用途进行了审查。回顾了正在进行的临床试验,并讨论了其在监测 治疗 反应和测量微小残留病 (MRD) 方面的效用。尽管 ctDNA 在推动精准肿瘤学方面前景广阔,但其常规临床应用仍存在一些问题,包括测试变异性和不准确测试结果的影响。需要优化临床试验设计和进一步研究,才能将 ctDNA 推向头颈癌治疗的最前沿。结论和相关性:针对病毒 ctDNA 的液体活检是头颈癌诊断和 监测 环境中一种有前途的生物标志物。正在进行的临床试验将有助于阐明如何实施 ctDNA 来监测 治疗 反应、评估 MRD 并制定 治疗 决策,以通过精准肿瘤学方法调整 治疗。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是循环肿瘤 DNA (ctDNA) 作为头颈癌的生物标志物,特别讨论了其在头颈癌类型的诊断和 监测 设置中的实用性,包括 HPV 相关的口咽癌和 Epstein-Barr 病毒相关的鼻咽癌。
证据基础与方法
美国头颈协会对 ctDNA 的当前用途进行了审查,并审查了正在进行的临床试验,以讨论其在监测 治疗 反应和测量微小残留病 (MRD) 方面的用途。
共识与结论
针对病毒 ctDNA 的液体活检是头颈癌诊断和 监测 设置中一种很有前景的生物标志物,正在进行的临床试验预计将阐明其在监测 治疗 反应、评估 MRD 以及通过精准肿瘤学方法制定 治疗 决策方面的实施情况。
争议与缺口
关于 ctDNA 的常规临床使用仍然存在一些问题,包括测试变异性和不准确测试结果的影响,需要优化临床试验设计和进一步研究,以将 ctDNA 带到头颈癌管理的最前沿。
科研与实践启示
优化的临床试验设计和进一步的研究对于将 ctDNA 带到头颈癌管理的最前沿是必要的,正在进行的试验预计将有助于实施 ctDNA 来监测 治疗 反应、评估 MRD 并通过精准肿瘤学方法调整 治疗。

Circulating Tumor DNA in Head and Neck Cancer: A Review From the American Head and Neck Society.

Authors: Sagar Kansara, Kevin Contrera, Scott Roof, Anne C. Kane, Rula Mualla, Eleni M Rettig, Nishant Agrawal, Barry Wenig, Joseph M Curry, Daniel Faden
Original abstract (English)
IMPORTANCE: Despite gains in understanding head and neck cancer over the past several decades, the discovery of reliable biomarkers remains elusive. With the advent of human papillomavirus-associated oropharynx cancer and Epstein-Barr virus-associated nasopharynx cancer, circulating tumor DNA (ctDNA) and oncogenic viral DNA have emerged as potentially promising biomarkers. OBSERVATIONS: A review was conducted by the American Head and Neck Society to address the current utility of ctDNA. Ongoing clinical trials were reviewed, with discussion of its utility in monitoring treatment response and measuring minimal residual disease (MRD). Although ctDNA holds promise in the push for precision oncology, several questions remain regarding its routine clinical use, including test variability and implications of inaccurate test results. Optimized clinical trial designs and further research are necessary to bring ctDNA to the forefront of head and neck cancer management. CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: Liquid biopsy targeting viral ctDNA is a promising biomarker in the diagnostic and surveillance settings across head and neck cancer types. Ongoing clinical trials will help elucidate how ctDNA can be implemented to monitor treatment response, assess MRD, and frame treatment decisions to adapt treatment via a precision oncology approach.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on circulating tumor DNA (ctDNA) as a biomarker in head and neck cancer, specifically addressing its utility in diagnostic and surveillance settings across head and neck cancer types, including HPV-associated oropharynx cancer and Epstein-Barr virus-associated nasopharynx cancer.
Evidence base and method
A review was conducted by the American Head and Neck Society to address the current utility of ctDNA, with ongoing clinical trials reviewed to discuss its utility in monitoring treatment response and measuring minimal residual disease (MRD).
Consensus and conclusions
Liquid biopsy targeting viral ctDNA is a promising biomarker in the diagnostic and surveillance settings across head and neck cancer types, with ongoing clinical trials expected to elucidate its implementation for monitoring treatment response, assessing MRD, and framing treatment decisions via a precision oncology approach.
Controversies and gaps
Several questions remain regarding the routine clinical use of ctDNA, including test variability and implications of inaccurate test results, necessitating optimized clinical trial designs and further research to bring ctDNA to the forefront of head and neck cancer management.
Research and practice implications
Optimized clinical trial designs and further research are necessary to bring ctDNA to the forefront of head and neck cancer management, with ongoing trials expected to help implement ctDNA for monitoring treatment response, assessing MRD, and adapting treatment via a precision oncology approach.

女性同种异体造血细胞移植幸存者对 宫颈癌 筛查的看法:一项回顾性研究。

Female allogeneic hematopoietic cell transplant survivors' perspectives on cervical cancer screening: a retrospective study.
作者： Jessica J Currier, Emily van Houweling, Katherine Bolten, Ngoc Wasson, Talia Frost-Belansky, Prajna Woonnimani, Arpita P. Gandhi, Amanda S Bruegl
摘要中文翻译目的:由于免疫抑制,女性同种异体造血细胞移植 (AlloHCT) 幸存者患 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关恶性肿瘤的风险增加。指南建议每年对宫颈年龄为 21-65 岁的 AlloHCT 幸存者进行 宫颈癌 筛查。本研究探讨了 宫颈癌 筛查知识、行为、态度、经验和偏好(特别是提供者收集与自我收集的方法),并确定了依从性障碍和促进因素。方法:我们对 2012 年至 2023 年间在俄勒冈健康与科学大学接受 AlloHCT 的 21-65 岁女性 AlloHCT 幸存者进行了一项混合方法观察研究。参与者完成了人口统计调查,并参加了焦点小组,探讨筛查依从性、看法和偏好。结果:23 名幸存者完成了调查,16 名幸存者于 2024 年秋季加入了焦点小组。参与者年龄中位数为 49 岁(范围 32-61);大多数是白人(87%)和私人保险(91%)。骨髓移植 (BMT) 的中位年龄为 41 岁(范围 19-56 岁)。原发性 诊断 包括急性髓系白血病或骨髓增生异常综合征 (65%)。报告的并发症包括移植物抗 宿主 病 (GVHD) (44%)、慢性 GVHD (39%) 和阴道 GVHD (22%)。对 宫颈癌 风险增加的认识促进了筛查依从性,而对网络内提供者的访问有限是一个障碍。由于对测试准确性的信心以及临床医生参与的保证,所有参与者都更喜欢提供者收集的筛查。心理因素,特别是担心复发或继发性癌症,强烈影响筛查偏好。结论:AlloHCT 幸存者的 宫颈癌 筛查偏好是由生理和心理 治疗 相关因素决定的。自行收集 HPV 可能是 BMT 之前、年度门诊就诊之间或出现获取障碍时的补充选择。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究重点关注 21-65 岁的女性同种异体造血细胞移植 (AlloHCT) 幸存者,她们因免疫抑制而面临 HPV 相关恶性肿瘤的风险增加。中心问题探讨他们的 宫颈癌 筛选知识、行为、态度、经验和偏好,特别是比较提供者收集的方法与自我收集的方法,同时识别依从障碍和促进因素。
证据基础与方法
对 2012 年至 2023 年间在俄勒冈健康与科学大学接受移植的女性 AlloHCT 幸存者进行了一项混合方法观察性研究。参与者完成了人口调查和焦点小组,以探索筛查依从性、看法和偏好。
共识与结论
AlloHCT 幸存者的 宫颈癌 筛查偏好是由生理和心理 治疗 相关因素决定的。由于对测试准确性的信心以及临床医生参与的保证,所有参与者都更喜欢提供者收集的筛查。HPV 自我采集可以作为 BMT 之前、每年访视之间或出现获取障碍时的补充选择。
争议与缺口
该研究发现,与网络内提供者的接触有限是筛选依从性的障碍。心理因素,特别是对复发或继发性癌症的担忧,强烈影响偏好,但该研究并未解决更广泛的系统或地理访问差异。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应探索改善网络内医疗服务提供者的接触机会并解决筛查心理障碍的策略。实践意义包括考虑将 HPV 自我采集作为克服获取障碍的补充选择,同时保持每年提供者采集的筛查作为首选方法。

Female allogeneic hematopoietic cell transplant survivors' perspectives on cervical cancer screening: a retrospective study.

Authors: Jessica J Currier, Emily van Houweling, Katherine Bolten, Ngoc Wasson, Talia Frost-Belansky, Prajna Woonnimani, Arpita P. Gandhi, Amanda S Bruegl
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: Female allogeneic hematopoietic cell transplantation (AlloHCT) survivors are at increased risk for human papillomavirus (HPV)-related malignancies due to immunosuppression. Guidelines recommend annual cervical cancer screening for AlloHCT survivors with a cervix aged 21-65. This study explored cervical cancer screening knowledge, behaviors, attitudes, experiences, and preferences-specifically provider-collected versus self-collected methods-and identified adherence barriers and facilitators. METHODS: We conducted a mixed-methods observational study of female AlloHCT survivors aged 21-65 who received AlloHCT at Oregon Health & Science University between 2012 and 2023. Participants completed demographic surveys and participated in focus groups exploring screening adherence, perceptions, and preferences. RESULTS: Twenty-three survivors completed the survey, and 16 joined focus groups in fall 2024. The median participant age was 49 years (range 32-61); most were white (87%) and privately insured (91%). Median age at bone marrow transplantation (BMT) was 41 years (range 19-56). Primary diagnosis included acute myeloid leukemia or myelodysplastic syndrome (65%). Reported complications included graft versus host disease (GVHD) (44%), chronic GVHD (39%), and vaginal GVHD (22%). Awareness of increased cervical cancer risk facilitated screening adherence, while limited access to in-network providers was a barrier. All participants preferred provider-collected screening due to confidence in test accuracy and reassurance from clinician involvement. Psychological factors, particularly worrying about recurrence or secondary cancers, strongly influenced screening preferences. CONCLUSION: Cervical cancer screening preferences among AlloHCT survivors were shaped by physical and psychological treatment-related factors. HPV self-collection may be a supplemental option before BMT, between annual clinic visits, or when access barriers arise.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study focuses on female allogeneic hematopoietic cell transplant (AlloHCT) survivors aged 21-65, who are at increased risk for HPV-related malignancies due to immunosuppression. The central question explores their cervical cancer screening knowledge, behaviors, attitudes, experiences, and preferences, specifically comparing provider-collected versus self-collected methods, while identifying adherence barriers and facilitators.
Evidence base and method
A mixed-methods observational study was conducted with female AlloHCT survivors who received transplants at Oregon Health & Science University between 2012 and 2023. Participants completed demographic surveys and focus groups to explore screening adherence, perceptions, and preferences.
Consensus and conclusions
Cervical cancer screening preferences among AlloHCT survivors are shaped by physical and psychological treatment-related factors. All participants preferred provider-collected screening due to confidence in test accuracy and reassurance from clinician involvement. HPV self-collection may serve as a supplemental option before BMT, between annual visits, or when access barriers arise.
Controversies and gaps
The study identified limited access to in-network providers as a barrier to screening adherence. Psychological factors, particularly worry about recurrence or secondary cancers, strongly influenced preferences, but the study did not address broader systemic or geographic access disparities.
Research and practice implications
Future research should explore strategies to improve access to in-network providers and address psychological barriers to screening. Practice implications include considering HPV self-collection as a supplemental option to overcome access barriers while maintaining annual provider-collected screening as the preferred method.

鳞状细胞肛门癌:当前的治疗方法。

Squamous Cell Anal Carcinoma: Current Therapeutic Approaches.
作者： Valentina Zen, Francesca Bof, Rossana Intini, Giulia Maddalena, Sara Lonardi, Francesca Bergamo
摘要中文翻译肛门鳞状细胞癌(SCAC)是一种相对罕见的恶性肿瘤,但其 发病率 一直在上升,很大程度上是由于 人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染,这是主要的病因驱动因素。高级鳞状上皮内病变 (HSIL) 的 HPV 疫苗接种 和 治疗 显着降低进展为浸润性癌症的风险,而免疫抑制个体则面临更高的疾病发展易感性。大多数患者被诊断为局限性 SCAC,明确的同步放化疗仍然是治疗标准,可提供较高的疾病控制率和总生存率。转移性疾病预后不良,主要通过全身治疗进行治疗,铂类化疗是首选一线 治疗;最近的 III 期数据确立了卡铂-紫杉醇与 retifanlimab(抗 PD1)的组合作为新标准。正在进行的研究重点是针对 HPV 的策略和新型免疫疗法,这些疗法有望进一步改善 SCAC 早期和晚期的结果。

Squamous Cell Anal Carcinoma: Current Therapeutic Approaches.

Authors: Valentina Zen, Francesca Bof, Rossana Intini, Giulia Maddalena, Sara Lonardi, Francesca Bergamo
Original abstract (English)
Anal squamous cell carcinoma (SCAC) is a relatively rare malignancy, but its incidence has been rising, largely due to infection with human papillomavirus (HPV), which is the main etiological driver. HPV vaccination and treatment of high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (HSIL) significantly lower the risk of progression to invasive cancer, while immunosuppressed individuals face a higher susceptibility to disease development. Most patients are diagnosed with localized SCAC, for which definitive concurrent chemoradiotherapy remains the standard of care, offering high rates of disease control and overall survival Metastatic disease carries a poor prognosis and is primarily managed with systemic therapy with platinum-based chemotherapy is the preferred first-line treatment; recent phase III data established the combination of carboplatin-paclitaxel with retifanlimab (anti-PD1) as a new standard. Ongoing research is focused on HPV-targeted strategies and novel immunotherapies, which hold promise for further improving outcomes in both early and advanced stages of SCAC.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Anal squamous cell carcinoma (SCAC) is a relatively rare malignancy, but its incidence has been rising, largely due to infection with human papillomavirus (HPV), which is the main etiological driver.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
Ongoing research is focused on HPV-targeted strategies and novel immunotherapies, which hold promise for further improving outcomes in both early and advanced stages of SCAC.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
Most patients are diagnosed with localized SCAC, for which definitive concurrent chemoradiotherapy remains the standard of care, offering high rates of disease control and overall survival Metastatic disease carries a poor prognosis and is primarily managed with systemic therapy with platinum-based chemotherapy is the preferred first-line treatment; recent phase III data established the combination of carboplatin-paclitaxel with retifanlimab (anti-PD1) as a new standard.

宫颈癌 筛查中初级 HPV 检测的实施和影响:大型学术医疗中心的历史观点和机构经验。

Implementation and impact of primary HPV testing in cervical cancer screening: Historical perspectives and institutional experience at a large academic medical center.
作者： Rebecca A Yoda, Eric C Huang
摘要中文翻译认识到高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是几乎所有 宫颈癌 的病原体,从根本上重塑了筛查策略,推动了高灵敏度分子 HPV 检测的开发和采用。在过去的几十年里,宫颈癌 筛查指南已从主要基于细胞学的方法转向基于 HPV 的方法,初级 HPV 检测现已被美国癌症协会推荐为首选方法,并预计将得到美国预防服务工作组的类似认可。本文追溯了 进化 筛查的 进化,从最初的巴氏测试到当前的指南建议,并讨论了过渡到初级 HPV 筛查系统的后勤要求,包括 HPV 阳性病例的分诊策略。作者还介绍了启动初级 HPV 检测的机构经验,强调了其对细胞病理学实验室工作流程和样本量的影响。

Implementation and impact of primary HPV testing in cervical cancer screening: Historical perspectives and institutional experience at a large academic medical center.

Authors: Rebecca A Yoda, Eric C Huang
Original abstract (English)
Recognition of high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) as the etiologic agent in nearly all cervical cancers has fundamentally reshaped screening strategies, driving the development and adoption of highly sensitive molecular HPV assays. Over the past several decades, cervical cancer screening guidelines have shifted from primarily cytology-based approaches toward HPV-based modalities, with primary HPV testing now recommended as the preferred method by the American Cancer Society and anticipated to be similarly endorsed by the United States Preventive Services Task Force. This article traces the evolution of cervical cancer screening, from the original Papanicolaou test to current guideline recommendations, and discusses the logistical requirements for transitioning to a primary HPV screening system, including triage strategies for HPV-positive cases. The authors additionally present an institutional experience of initiating primary HPV testing, highlighting its impact on cytopathology laboratory workflow and specimen volumes.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on human papillomavirus (HPV) as the etiologic agent in cervical cancer and the evolution of screening strategies toward primary HPV testing.
Evidence base and method
The article is a historical and institutional review tracing the evolution of cervical cancer screening from cytology-based approaches to HPV-based modalities, with no explicit systematic search or meta-analysis reported.
Consensus and conclusions
Primary HPV testing is now the preferred screening method per the American Cancer Society and anticipated endorsement by the USPSTF, with high sensitivity for detecting cervical cancer precursors.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights logistical challenges in transitioning to primary HPV testing, including triage strategies for HPV-positive cases, but does not address broader evidence gaps or conflicting findings.
Research and practice implications
The authors emphasize the need for institutional planning to manage workflow and specimen volume changes during the transition to primary HPV testing.

[睾丸生殖细胞肿瘤和阴茎癌:基于分子的诊断和治疗]。

[Testicular Germ Cell Tumors and Penile Cancer: Molecular-Based Diagnosis and Treatment].
作者： Pia Paffenholz, Axel Heidenreich, Richard Weiten
摘要中文翻译分子诊断和靶向治疗正日益向个性化医疗方法发展。本综述总结了睾丸生殖细胞肿瘤(TGCT)和阴茎癌的最新进展,重点关注新兴生物标志物和靶向治疗策略。传统的血清肿瘤标志物,如 AFP、β-HCG 和 LDH 仍然很重要,但受到敏感性和特异性欠佳的限制。新型生物标志物,特别是 microRNA miR-371a-3p,已证明具有更高的诊断准确性,并且可以改善 TGCT 中的风险分层、疾病复发检测和 Z​​XQTERMVQXZ 决策。此外,循环肿瘤 DNA (ctDNA) 的分析已成为一种有前途的液体活检方法。在阴茎鳞状细胞癌中,基于 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 状态的分子分类和全面的基因组分析与预后和治疗选择变得越来越相关。可操作的分子改变的鉴定促进了免疫疗法、抗 EGFR 策略和抗体药物偶联物的发展。早期临床数据还表明,ctDNA 可能为 治疗 监测和早期复发检测提供有价值的信息。尽管有这些有希望的发现,但在将其中许多方法纳入常规临床实践之前,还需要进一步的前瞻性研究和标准化方法。总体而言,这些进展凸显了向生物标志物驱动的精准肿瘤学的持续转变,以及对 TGCT 和阴茎癌患者进行更个体化管理的潜力。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注睾丸生殖细胞肿瘤 (TGCT) 和阴茎癌,研究基于分子的 诊断 和 治疗 方法,重点关注生物标志物和靶向治疗。
证据基础与方法
该综述总结了 TGCT 和阴茎癌的最新进展,强调了新兴的生物标志物和靶向治疗策略,但没有报告具体的数据库、搜索日期和研究计数。
共识与结论
miR-371a-3p 等新型生物标志物在 TGCT 中提供更高的诊断准确性,并且基于 HPV 状态的分子分类与阴茎癌预后和 治疗 选择越来越相关。可行的分子改变使免疫疗法和抗 EGFR 策略成为可能。

[Testicular Germ Cell Tumors and Penile Cancer: Molecular-Based Diagnosis and Treatment].

Authors: Pia Paffenholz, Axel Heidenreich, Richard Weiten
Original abstract (English)
Molecular diagnostics and targeted therapies are increasingly evolving towards a personalized medicine approach. This review summarizes recent advances in testicular germ cell tumors (TGCTs) and penile cancer, with a focus on emerging biomarkers and targeted therapeutic strategies. Conventional serum tumor markers such as AFP, β-HCG, and LDH remain important but are limited by suboptimal sensitivity and specificity. Novel biomarkers, particularly microRNA miR-371a-3p, have demonstrated substantially higher diagnostic accuracy and may improve risk stratification, detection of disease recurrence, and treatment decision-making in TGCTs. In addition, the analysis of circulating tumor DNA (ctDNA) has emerged as a promising liquid biopsy procedure. In penile squamous cell carcinoma, molecular classification based on human papillomavirus (HPV) status and comprehensive genomic profiling are becoming increasingly relevant for prognosis and therapeutic selection. The identification of actionable molecular alterations has facilitated the development of immunotherapy, anti-EGFR strategies, and antibody-drug conjugates. Early clinical data also suggest that ctDNA may provide valuable information for treatment monitoring and early relapse detection. Despite these promising findings, further prospective studies and standardized methodologies are required before many of these approaches can be incorporated into routine clinical practice. Overall, these developments highlight the ongoing transition towards biomarker-driven precision oncology and the potential for more individualized management of patients with TGCTs and penile cancer.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on testicular germ cell tumors (TGCTs) and penile cancer, examining molecular-based diagnosis and treatment approaches, with emphasis on biomarkers and targeted therapies.
Evidence base and method
The review summarizes recent advances in TGCTs and penile cancer, highlighting emerging biomarkers and targeted therapeutic strategies, though specific databases, search dates, and study counts are not reported.
Consensus and conclusions
Novel biomarkers like miR-371a-3p offer higher diagnostic accuracy in TGCTs, and molecular classification based on HPV status is increasingly relevant for penile cancer prognosis and treatment selection. Actionable molecular alterations have enabled immunotherapy and anti-EGFR strategies.
Controversies and gaps
Conventional serum tumor markers (AFP, β-HCG, LDH) have limited sensitivity and specificity. While ctDNA shows promise for treatment monitoring, further prospective studies and standardized methodologies are needed before widespread clinical implementation.
Research and practice implications
Prospective studies and standardized methodologies are required to incorporate biomarker-driven approaches into routine clinical practice, enabling more individualized management of TGCTs and penile cancer.

HPV 阳性口咽鳞状细胞癌的治疗耐药:分子机制、临床挑战和精准策略

Therapeutic resistance in HPV-positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma: molecular mechanisms, clinical challenges, and precision strategies
作者： Yiming Meng, Jing Sun, Yushu Ma, Cuicui Kong
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 阳性口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 对放化疗表现出异常的敏感性,5 年总生存率超过 80%,从而为 治疗 降级提供了令人信服的理由。然而,15%–20% 的患者出现治疗耐药和局部复发,导致结果明显较差。本综述系统地剖析了 HPV 阳性 OPSCC 中 治疗 耐药的分子结构,特别强调对放疗、铂类化疗和 EGFR 靶向治疗(西妥昔单抗)产生耐药性的机制。我们描绘了核心驱动因素——异常 DNA 损伤反应 (DDR) 信号、表观遗传重编程、癌症干细胞 (CSC) 可塑性和非编码 RNA 网络——并查明可操作的漏洞。患者来源的类器官、基因工程小鼠模型和关键临床试验(特别是 RTOG 1016)的贡献和局限性受到严格评价。我们解决了持续存在的争议,包括降级和耐药性之间的复杂关系,并强调缺乏综合耐药性生物标志物。最后,我们提出了一个路线图,以弥合转化差距并加速制定规避阻力的精准战略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 HPV 阳性口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 的治疗耐药性,研究分子机制、临床挑战以及解决放化疗、铂类化疗和 EGFR 靶向治疗耐药性的精准策略。
证据基础与方法
该综述系统地剖析了 HPV 阳性 OPSCC 中 治疗 耐药的分子结构,批判性地评价了源自患者的类器官、基因工程小鼠模型和关键临床试验(特别是 RTOG 1016)的贡献和局限性。

HPV 阳性 OPSCC 对放化疗表现出异常的敏感性,5 年总生存率超过 80%,但 15%–20% 的患者出现治疗耐药和局部复发。耐药性的核心驱动因素包括异常的 DNA 损伤反应信号、表观遗传重编程、癌症干细胞可塑性和非编码 RNA 网络。
争议与缺口
持续存在的争议包括 治疗 降级和耐药之间的复杂关系,以及缺乏综合耐药生物标志物。审查强调了未解决的机制和对可采取行动的漏洞的需求。
科研与实践启示
提出了一个路线图,以弥合转化差距并加速制定规避耐药性的精准策略,强调需要综合生物标志物和有针对性的干预措施。

Therapeutic resistance in HPV-positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma: molecular mechanisms, clinical challenges, and precision strategies

Authors: Yiming Meng, Jing Sun, Yushu Ma, Cuicui Kong
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV)-positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) exhibits exceptional sensitivity to chemoradiotherapy, with 5-year overall survival exceeding 80%, thereby providing a compelling rationale for treatment de-escalation. However, 15%–20% of patients develop therapeutic resistance and locoregional recurrence, resulting in markedly inferior outcomes. This Review systematically dissects the molecular architecture of treatment resistance in HPV-positive OPSCC, with particular emphasis on mechanisms conferring resistance to radiotherapy, platinum-based chemotherapy, and EGFR-targeted therapy (cetuximab). We delineate core drivers — aberrant DNA damage response (DDR) signaling, epigenetic reprogramming, cancer stem cell (CSC) plasticity, and non-coding RNA networks—and pinpoint actionable vulnerabilities. The contributions and limitations of patient-derived organoids, genetically engineered mouse models, and pivotal clinical trials (notably RTOG 1016) are critically appraised. We address persistent controversies, including the complex relationship between de-escalation and resistance, and highlight the lack of integrative resistance biomarkers. Finally, we propose a roadmap to bridge translational gaps and accelerate the development of precision strategies that circumvent resistance.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on therapeutic resistance in HPV-positive oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC), examining molecular mechanisms, clinical challenges, and precision strategies to address resistance to chemoradiotherapy, platinum-based chemotherapy, and EGFR-targeted therapy.
Evidence base and method
The review systematically dissects the molecular architecture of treatment resistance in HPV-positive OPSCC, critically appraises contributions and limitations of patient-derived organoids, genetically engineered mouse models, and pivotal clinical trials (notably RTOG 1016).
Consensus and conclusions
HPV-positive OPSCC exhibits exceptional sensitivity to chemoradiotherapy with 5-year overall survival exceeding 80%, but 15%–20% of patients develop therapeutic resistance and locoregional recurrence. Core drivers of resistance include aberrant DNA damage response signaling, epigenetic reprogramming, cancer stem cell plasticity, and non-coding RNA networks.
Controversies and gaps
Persistent controversies include the complex relationship between treatment de-escalation and resistance, and the lack of integrative resistance biomarkers. The review highlights unresolved mechanisms and the need for actionable vulnerabilities.
Research and practice implications
A roadmap is proposed to bridge translational gaps and accelerate the development of precision strategies that circumvent resistance, emphasizing the need for integrative biomarkers and targeted interventions.

宫颈癌 (HPV) 疫苗 在河流州 KELGA 育龄妇女中的可用性和利用

Availability and Utilization of the Cervical Cancer (HPV) Vaccine among Reproductive-Age Women in KELGA, Rivers State
作者： Queen Amadi, Mercy Mgbere, Aleruchi Lenchi Oji, Jane Baridah Kue
摘要中文翻译尽管有有效的预防性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗,但 宫颈癌 仍然是尼日利亚女性癌症相关发病率和 死亡率 的主要原因之一。在一些社区,包括河流州 Ikwerre 地方政府区 (KELGA) 的 Igwuruta、Omagwa 和 Aluu,疫苗 的使用率仍然较低,这些社区的卫生机构记录和轶事证据表明,晚期宫颈疾病持续出现,而及时 疫苗接种 本来可以预防。本研究评估了 宫颈癌 疫苗 的可用性及其在 KELGA 免疫诊所就诊的育龄妇女中的利用水平,并检验了这两种结构与总体 疫苗 接受度之间的关系。采用横断面描述性调查设计,并使用访谈员填写的结构化问卷来收集 325 名育龄妇女(15-49 岁)的数据,这些妇女是通过三个初级卫生中心的简单随机抽样从 379 份问卷中选出的(85.5% 的答复率)。使用描述性统计(频率、百分比、平均值和标准差)和卡方独立性检验对数据进行分析,显着性水平为 0.05。结果显示,人们对 疫苗 的可用性(总平均值 = 3.32)和利用率(总平均值 = 3.31)普遍持积极态度。卡方分析揭示了可用性和接受度之间 (X2 = 159.099,df = 1,p = .000) 以及利用率和接受度之间 (X2 = 125.294,df = 1,p = .000) 之间存在统计显着关系,导致拒绝两个原假设。该研究得出的结论是,疫苗 的可用性和利用率是该人群接受的重要决定因素,尽管偶尔的库存短缺和完成完整给药计划的较低率仍然是令人担忧的领域。建议卫生当局加强 疫苗 供应链管理并建立结构化的随访系统以支持全部剂量完成,从而提高类似社区环境中 宫颈癌 疫苗 的总体接受度和覆盖率。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究重点关注尼日利亚河流州 Ikwerre 地方政府区 (KELGA) 育龄妇女(15-49 岁)人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 的可用性和利用情况,探讨其与 疫苗 接受度的关系。
证据基础与方法
使用结构化、访谈员管理的问卷进行了一项横断面描述性调查,通过简单随机抽样从三个初级卫生中心选出了 325 名参与者(回应率为 85.5%)。使用描述性统计和卡方检验对数据进行分析,p < 0.05。
共识与结论
疫苗 可用性(总平均值 = 3.32)和利用率(总平均值 = 3.31)与接受度显着相关(分别为 X2 = 159.099 和 125.294;p = .000),证实它们是关键决定因素。然而,库存短缺和给药计划不完整仍然令人担忧。
争议与缺口
该研究将偶尔的库存短缺和低全剂量完成率确定为未解决的问题,但没有探讨这些差距的具体原因或地区差异。
科研与实践启示
卫生当局应加强 疫苗 供应链管理并实施结构化随访系统,以提高类似社区环境中的剂量完成和总体接受度。

Availability and Utilization of the Cervical Cancer (HPV) Vaccine among Reproductive-Age Women in KELGA, Rivers State

Authors: Queen Amadi, Mercy Mgbere, Aleruchi Lenchi Oji, Jane Baridah Kue
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer remains one of the leading causes of cancer-related morbidity and mortality among women in Nigeria, despite the availability of an effective preventive human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine. Uptake of the vaccine remains low in several communities, including Igwuruta, Omagwa, and Aluu in Ikwerre Local Government Area (KELGA), Rivers State, where health facility records and anecdotal evidence suggest continued presentation of advanced cervical disease that timely vaccination could have prevented. This study assessed the availability of the cervical cancer vaccine and its level of utilization among reproductive-age women attending immunization clinics in KELGA, and examined the relationship between these two constructs and overall vaccine acceptance. A cross-sectional descriptive survey design was adopted, and a structured, interviewer-administered questionnaire was used to collect data from 325 reproductive-age women (15–49 years) selected through simple random sampling across three primary health centres, out of 379 questionnaires administered (85.5% response rate). Data were analyzed using descriptive statistics (frequencies, percentages, means, and standard deviations) and the Chi-square test of independence, tested at a 0.05 level of significance. Results showed generally favourable perceptions of both availability (grand mean = 3.32) and utilization (grand mean = 3.31) of the vaccine. Chi-square analysis revealed statistically significant relationships between availability and acceptance (X2 = 159.099, df = 1, p = .000) and between utilization and acceptance (X2 = 125.294, df = 1, p = .000), leading to rejection of both null hypotheses. The study concludes that vaccine availability and utilization are significant determinants of acceptance in this population, though occasional stock shortages and lower rates of completing the full dosing schedule remain areas of concern. It is recommended that health authorities strengthen vaccine supply chain management and institute structured follow-up systems to support full dose completion, thereby improving overall cervical cancer vaccine acceptance and coverage in similar community settings.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study focuses on human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine availability and utilization among reproductive-age women (15–49 years) in Ikwerre Local Government Area (KELGA), Rivers State, Nigeria, examining their relationship with vaccine acceptance.
Evidence base and method
A cross-sectional descriptive survey was conducted using a structured, interviewer-administered questionnaire with 325 participants selected via simple random sampling from three primary health centres (85.5% response rate). Data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and Chi-square tests at p < 0.05.
Consensus and conclusions
Vaccine availability (grand mean = 3.32) and utilization (grand mean = 3.31) were significantly associated with acceptance (X2 = 159.099 and 125.294, respectively; p = .000), confirming them as key determinants. However, stock shortages and incomplete dosing schedules remain concerns.
Controversies and gaps
The study identifies occasional stock shortages and low full-dose completion rates as unresolved issues, though specific causes or regional disparities in these gaps are not explored.
Research and practice implications
Health authorities should strengthen vaccine supply chain management and implement structured follow-up systems to improve dose completion and overall acceptance in similar community settings.
